Learning Action Priors for Cross-embodiment Robot Manipulation
Authors: Dong Jing, Tianqi Zhang, Jiaqi Liu, Jinman Zhao, Zelong Sun, Li Erran Li, Zhiwu Lu, Mingyu Ding
First: 2026-06-24T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models build on a Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone by attaching an action module and optimizing the full policy jointly. This design inherits strong visual and linguistic priors from the VLM, but leaves the action module to learn physical motion almost from scratch. As a result, the policy lacks an explicit motion prior, forcing early optimization to simultaneously discover temporal action dynamics and cross-modal alignment, a challenge further amplified in cross-embodiment settings. In this work, we propose to pretrain the action module with motion priors before cross-modal VLA alignment. Specifically, we introduce a two-stage training framework that equips the action module with cross-embodiment temporal motion structure before VLA training begins. In Stage~1, a lightweight flow-matching-based encoder-decoder action module efficiently learns temporal motion structure solely from unconditioned action trajectories, without processing visual or language tokens. In Stage~2, this learned prior is transferred to VLA training through decoder reuse and early-stage latent distillation, aligning visual-language features with the action embedding space while still allowing end-to-end policy refinement. In addition, the trained encoder serves as a compact history compressor, summarizing state-action histories into a single temporal context token for history-aware modeling at negligible cost. Extensive experiments across 13 diverse cross-embodiment tasks on both simulated and real-world platforms validate the effectiveness of our approach. Compared with VLA training without action priors, our model achieves faster convergence, higher success rates, and substantially stronger performance on data-scarce real-world tasks. Moreover, scaling up the action data in Stage~1 yields a more generalizable action prior that directly improves downstream VLA performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习跨具身机器人操作的动作先验
大多数视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）骨干，附加动作模块并联合优化完整策略。该设计继承了VLM的强视觉和语言先验，但使动作模块几乎从零开始学习物理运动。结果导致策略缺乏显式运动先验，迫使早期优化同时发现时序动作动态与跨模态对齐，这一挑战在跨具身场景中进一步加剧。本文提出在跨模态VLA对齐前，先用运动先验预训练动作模块。具体而言，我们引入两阶段训练框架，在VLA训练开始前，为动作模块配备跨具身时序运动结构。第一阶段，一个轻量级基于流匹配的编码器-解码器动作模块，仅从无条件动作轨迹高效学习时序运动结构，无需处理视觉或语言标记。第二阶段，通过解码器复用和早期阶段潜在蒸馏，将所学先验迁移到VLA训练中，使视觉-语言特征与动作嵌入空间对齐，同时仍允许端到端策略微调。此外，训练好的编码器可作为紧凑历史压缩器，将状态-动作历史压缩为单个时序上下文标记，用于历史感知建模且成本极低。在13个多样化的跨具身任务上，我们对模拟与真实平台进行了广泛实验，验证了该方法的有效性。与无动作先验的VLA训练相比，我们的模型收敛更快、成功率更高，并在数据稀缺的真实任务中表现显著更强。此外，扩大第一阶段的动作数据规模，可获得更通用的动作先验，直接提升下游VLA性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current Vision-Language-Action models that learn motion from scratch without leveraging motion priors, this work proposes a two-stage training framework to pretrain an action module with cross-embodiment temporal motion structure before VLA alignment. In Stage 1, a lightweight flow-matching-based encoder-decoder learns motion patterns solely from unconditioned action trajectories, avoiding visual or language input. In Stage 2, this learned prior is transferred to VLA training via decoder reuse and latent distillation, aligning visual-language features with action embeddings while enabling end-to-end policy refinement. The encoder also compresses state-action histories into a single token for efficient history-aware modeling. Experiments across 13 cross-embodiment tasks show the method achieves faster convergence, higher success rates, and significantly better performance on real-world data-scarce tasks, with improved generalization when more action data is used in Stage 1.
为了解决跨具身机器人操作中从零学习运动模式效率低的问题，本文提出一种两阶段训练框架，先在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型集成前，用运动先验预训练动作模块。第一阶段，一个轻量级基于流匹配的编码器-解码器仅从无条件动作轨迹中学习时序运动结构，不依赖视觉或语言输入。第二阶段，通过解码器复用和潜变量蒸馏，将所学先验迁移到VLA训练中，实现视觉-语言特征与动作嵌入空间对齐，同时支持端到端策略优化。该编码器还可作为压缩历史的轻量模块。在13个跨具身任务上的实验表明，相比无动作先验的VLA模型，该方法收敛更快、成功率更高、在数据稀缺的真实任务上表现显著更强，且第一阶段动作数据量越大，下游VLA性能提升越明显。
RevengeBench: Reverse Engineering Code-Space Policies from Behavioral Experiments
Authors: Babak Rahmani, Sebastian Dziadzio, Joschka Strüber, Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez, Matthias Bethge
First: 2026-06-24T17:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:59:46+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures, 22 appendix pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For most of scientific history, researchers studying behavior could only infer hidden mechanisms from outward actions: an inverse problem that becomes more tractable when observation is augmented by targeted intervention. We pose a computational analogue: given only behavioral traces of an agent in a game environment, can a learner reconstruct the underlying decision program as executable code, and how much does this reconstruction improve with the ability to design controlled experiments? We introduce RevengeBench, a benchmark of 75 LLM generated, Elo-calibrated policies across five game environments, drawn from CodeClash tournament trajectories. The learner observes the hidden target policy play against sampled opponents and designs behavioral probes in the form of custom opponent policies that elicit informative behavior. It then submits an executable hypothesis, which is evaluated using continuous action-distance metrics. We further validate that recovered code carries informative signal in downstream player-versus-player tournaments. Across twelve frontier LLMs, recovery quality varies substantially (34 to 72% of initial distance closed), with reconstructed policies yielding measurable competitive advantage, particularly for weaker models that otherwise struggle to design effective counter-strategies. Our benchmark positions behavioral recovery of programmatic policies as a tractable inverse problem in code-space, opening a path to opponent modeling, policy interpretability, and the broader question of inferring latent mechanisms from observations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RevengeBench：从行为实验中逆向工程代码空间策略
在科学史的大部分时间里，研究行为的学者只能从外在行为推断隐藏机制：这是一个逆向问题，当观察与有针对性的干预结合时会变得更容易处理。我们提出其计算类比：仅给定智能体在游戏环境中的行为轨迹，学习者能否重建其底层决策程序为可执行代码？这种重建能力在设计受控实验时能提升多少？我们引入RevengeBench，这是一个包含75个由大语言模型生成、按Elo等级校准的策略的基准，涵盖五个游戏环境，源自CodeClash锦标赛轨迹。学习者观察隐藏目标策略与采样对手对战，并设计行为探针（即自定义对手策略）以激发有信息量的行为。随后提交可执行假设，通过连续动作距离度量评估。我们进一步验证，恢复的代码在下游玩家对战锦标赛中携带信息信号。在12个前沿大语言模型中，恢复质量差异显著（初始距离闭合率34%至72%），重建策略带来可测量的竞争优势，尤其对原本难以设计有效对抗策略的较弱模型效果更明显。本基准将程序化策略的行为恢复定位为代码空间中的可解逆向问题，为对手建模、策略可解释性及从观察中推断潜在机制等更广泛问题开辟道路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of inferring hidden decision-making mechanisms from observed behavior, this work explores whether a learner can reconstruct executable code representing an agent’s policy from behavioral traces alone, especially when augmented with controlled experiments. The method introduces RevengeBench, a benchmark of 75 LLM-generated, Elo-calibrated policies across five game environments, where the learner observes policy behavior against sampled opponents and designs custom opponent policies to elicit informative responses. The learner then submits a reconstructed executable code, evaluated via continuous action-distance metrics, and its effectiveness is validated in downstream player-versus-player tournaments. Across twelve frontier LLMs, recovery quality varied significantly (34% to 72% of initial distance closed), with reconstructed policies showing measurable competitive advantage, especially for weaker models struggling to devise effective counter-strategies.
为解决从观察行为反推隐藏决策机制的难题，该研究提出RevengeBench基准，测试学习者能否仅通过行为轨迹与自定义对手策略的实验，重建可执行的代理决策代码。方法包括观察代理与采样对手的对弈，设计信息性探针诱导特定行为，提交重建代码并用连续动作距离指标评估。在12个前沿大模型上，重建质量介于34%至72%之间，重建策略在对抗赛中带来可测量的竞争优势，尤其对原本难以设计有效对策的弱模型效果显著，证明了行为重建作为代码空间中可解逆问题的可行性。
ForceBand: Learning Forceful Manipulation with sEMG
Authors: Botao He, Zhi Wang, Linna Kuang, Ishaan Ghosh, Jitendra Malik, Cornelia Fermuller, Tingfan Wu, Jiayuan Mao, Ruoshi Liu, Haozhi Qi, Yiannis Aloimonos
First: 2026-06-24T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:59:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Human demonstrations are a scalable data source for learning robot manipulation policies. However, common sources of human demonstration data, such as motion-capture trajectories and internet videos, capture mostly motion and appearance while missing the contact forces that are critical for force-sensitive manipulation. In this paper, we introduce ForceBand, a low-cost wrist-worn sEMG system that turns human muscle activity into force-enriched demonstrations. We first collect a 10-hour multimodal dataset containing egocentric video, sEMG, IMU, and fingertip force measurements across diverse actions and objects. Using this dataset, we pre-train an EMG2Force model that predicts per-finger forces from sEMG and IMU signals. After a short user-specific calibration, users can collect target-task demonstrations using only ForceBand and video; EMG2Force then labels these demonstrations with per-finger force traces, producing force-augmented demonstrations for robot policy learning. Experiments show that ForceBand recovers fine-grained fingertip interactions with over 50% lower force prediction error than vision-based baselines and achieves an 87% success rate on pick, squeeze, and place tasks that require object-specific force control across objects with diverse shapes, sizes, and weights. Project website: https://forceband-emg.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：ForceBand：利用表面肌电图学习强力操作
人类演示是学习机器人操作策略的可扩展数据来源。然而，常见的演示数据来源（如动作捕捉轨迹和互联网视频）主要捕捉运动和外观，而缺少力接触信息，这对力敏感操作至关重要。本文引入了ForceBand，一种低成本的腕戴式表面肌电图（sEMG）系统，可将人类肌肉活动转化为富含力信息的演示。我们首先收集了一个10小时的多模态数据集，包含第一人称视频、sEMG、惯性测量单元（IMU）和指尖力测量，涵盖多样化的动作和物体。利用该数据集，我们预训练了一个EMG2Force模型，该模型可根据sEMG和IMU信号预测每根手指的力。经过短暂的用户特定校准后，用户仅需使用ForceBand和视频即可收集目标任务演示；EMG2Force随后为这些演示标注每根手指的力轨迹，生成力增强演示用于机器人策略学习。实验表明，ForceBand在力预测误差方面比基于视觉的基线方法低50%以上，并在涉及物体特定力控制的抓取、挤压和放置任务中，针对形状、大小和重量各异的物体，成功率达87%。项目网站：https://forceband-emg.github.io
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of force data in human demonstration datasets for robot manipulation, the authors introduce ForceBand, a low-cost wrist-worn sEMG system that captures muscle activity to infer fingertip forces. They collect a 10-hour multimodal dataset combining video, sEMG, IMU, and force measurements, and pre-train an EMG2Force model to predict per-finger forces from these signals. After user-specific calibration, users can generate force-augmented demonstrations using only ForceBand and video. Experiments show the system achieves over 50% lower force prediction error than vision-based baselines and an 87% success rate on force-sensitive tasks involving diverse objects.
为解决机器人操作中人类演示数据缺乏接触力信息的问题，作者提出了ForceBand，一种低成本腕戴式sEMG系统，通过捕捉肌肉活动推断指尖力。他们收集了包含视频、sEMG、IMU和力测量的10小时多模态数据集，训练EMG2Force模型从sEMG和IMU信号预测每根手指的力。用户经短时个性化校准后，仅用ForceBand和视频即可录制演示，系统自动标注力轨迹用于机器人策略学习。实验表明，ForceBand比基于视觉的基线方法力预测误差低50%以上，在涉及不同形状、尺寸和重量物体的抓取、挤压与放置任务中成功率高达87%。
TryOnCrafter: Unleashing Camera Trajectories for Realistic Video Virtual Try-on via a Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy
Authors: Hao Sun, Hao Yan, Mengting Chen, Quanjian Song, Yu Li, Juan Cao, Jinsong Lan, Xiaoyong Zhu, Bo Zheng, Sheng Tang
First: 2026-06-24T17:59:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:59:06+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://sunhao242.github.io/TryOnCrafter_web.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While Video Virtual Try-on (VVT) has achieved remarkable progress in synthesizing realistic garment overlays on dynamic subjects, existing paradigms remains fundamentally constrained by a passive dependency on source camera trajectories, failing to accommodate the requisite interactive freedom for omnidirectional viewpoint exploration. To address this limitation, we define a pioneering research frontier: Camera-controllable Video Virtual Try-on (CaM-VVT). Unlike conventional VVT, CaM-VVT not only necessitates viewpoint-agnostic texture hallucination but also strict structural synchronization between non-rigid human dynamics and background contexts under arbitrary, unconstrained camera movements. To tackle these challenges, we present TryOnCrafter, the first unified DiT-based framework specifically architected for the CaM-VVT task. Departing from implicit pixel-space manipulation, we introduce a Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy that explicitly decouples the human subject from the environment. This is achieved by distilling high-fidelity 2D try-on priors into a clothed 3DGS-based avatar, which is subsequently animated via SMPL-X sequences and metric-aligned into a reconstructed background point cloud. This proxy establishes a robust structural foundation with superior texture density and motion integrity. Our Proxy-Anchored Video DiT leverages this robust structural foundation as a primary geometric anchor, ensuring that the synthesized photorealistic videos are strictly constrained by prescribed trajectories and physically plausible deformations. Benefiting from the inherent editability of the 4D proxy, TryOnCrafter facilitates diverse downstream applications, including human relocalization, ``bullet time'' effects, and $360$-degree orbital viewing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TryOnCrafter：通过可渲染的4D试穿代理释放相机轨迹，实现真实视频虚拟试穿
尽管视频虚拟试穿（VVT）在动态主体上合成逼真服装叠加效果方面取得了显著进展，但现有范式仍受制于对源相机轨迹的被动依赖，无法满足全方位视角探索所需的交互自由度。为解决此局限，我们定义了一个开创性研究前沿：相机可控视频虚拟试穿（CaM-VVT）。与传统VVT不同，CaM-VVT不仅需要视角无关的纹理生成，还需在任意、无约束的相机运动下，严格保持非刚性人体动态与背景环境的结构同步。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了TryOnCrafter，首个专为CaM-VVT任务设计的统一DiT框架。不同于隐式像素空间操作，我们引入了一个可渲染的4D试穿代理，显式地将人体主体与环境解耦。该代理通过将高保真2D试穿先验蒸馏至基于3DGS的着装Avatar，并通过SMPL-X序列驱动动画，再与重建的背景点云进行度量对齐实现。该代理建立了具有高纹理密度和运动完整性的稳健结构基础。我们的代理锚定视频DiT利用该结构基础作为主要几何锚点，确保合成的逼真视频严格遵循指定轨迹并具备物理上合理的形变。得益于4D代理的固有可编辑性，TryOnCrafter支持多种下游应用，包括人体重定位、"子弹时间"效果及360度轨道视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interactive, camera-controllable video virtual try-on beyond static camera setups, the authors introduce TryOnCrafter, a DiT-based framework that enables realistic garment synthesis under arbitrary camera trajectories. The method constructs a renderable 4D try-on proxy by first generating a clothed 3DGS avatar from 2D try-on priors, then animating it with SMPL-X sequences and aligning it to a reconstructed background point cloud. This proxy ensures structural consistency and texture fidelity under dynamic camera motion. Experimental results demonstrate that TryOnCrafter successfully generates photorealistic videos that strictly adhere to user-defined camera paths while preserving human motion and environmental context, enabling applications like bullet time and 360-degree viewing.
为了解决现有视频虚拟试衣系统对固定摄像机轨迹的被动依赖问题，本文提出可控制摄像机轨迹的视频虚拟试衣（CaM-VVT），支持任意视角下的真实服装叠加。TryOnCrafter框架构建了一个可渲染的4D试衣代理：首先将2D试衣先验蒸馏为基于3DGS的穿衣虚拟人，再通过SMPL-X序列动画并精准对齐至重建的背景点云，从而实现人与环境的解耦。该代理作为几何锚点，支撑Proxy-Anchored Video DiT生成严格遵循指定轨迹且物理变形合理的逼真视频。实验表明，该方法在真实感与结构保真度上优于现有方法，支持重定位、子弹时间及360度环绕等下游应用。
On-Policy Self-Distillation with Sampled Demonstrations Reduces Output Diversity
Authors: Andrei Liviu Nicolicioiu, Mohammad Pezeshki, Aaron Courville
First: 2026-06-24T17:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:59:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy self-distillation achieves strong pass@1 accuracy by using a single model as both teacher and student, with the teacher conditioned on a correct demonstration to provide dense token-level feedback. We show that this could come at a hidden cost: rollout diversity decreases and pass@k curves flatten (i.e., generating more rollouts fails to improve accuracy). We trace this to compounding biases in the design of self-distillation with sampled demonstrations. The teacher scores each student rollout while conditioned on a sampled correct rollout, channeling its feedback through the model's own biases. We theoretically analyze the optimal self-distillation policy and show that it tilts the base distribution by a pointwise conditional mutual information score between the student's rollout and the correct rollout used as context. Unlike the ideal optimal on-policy reinforcement learning (RL), which preserves probability ratios among equally correct rollouts, self-distillation can amplify existing probability gaps, concentrating mass on already-dominant modes. On a controlled graph path-finding task and science question-answering benchmarks, self-distilled models match or exceed RL on average performance but exhibit substantially lower functional and semantic diversity, failing on out-of-distribution settings that require diverse strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于采样演示的在线自蒸馏会降低输出多样性
在线自蒸馏通过让单一模型同时担任教师和学生，并以正确演示为条件提供密集的词级反馈，实现高pass@1准确率。我们发现这可能带来隐性代价：rollout多样性下降，pass@k曲线趋于平坦（即生成更多rollout无法提升准确率）。我们追溯到自蒸馏中采样演示设计所累积的偏差：教师在采样正确rollout条件下评分学生rollout，其反馈通过模型自身偏差传递。我们理论分析了最优自蒸馏策略，表明其通过学生rollout与作为上下文的正确rollout之间的点态条件互信息得分，倾斜基础分布。与理想最优在线强化学习（RL）不同，后者保持同等正确rollout间的概率比例，自蒸馏可能放大现有概率差距，集中质量于已占主导的模式。在受控图路径搜索任务和科学问答基准测试中，自蒸馏模型平均性能可媲美或超越RL，但功能与语义多样性显著降低，在需要多样化策略的分布外场景中表现不佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that on-policy self-distillation improves pass@1 accuracy but reduces output diversity, the paper investigates how sampled demonstrations in self-distillation introduce compounding biases that flatten pass@k curves and concentrate model behavior on dominant modes. The authors theoretically derive an optimal self-distillation policy that tilts the base distribution using conditional mutual information between student and correct rollouts, contrasting it with ideal on-policy RL which preserves probability ratios among correct rollouts. Experiments on graph path-finding and science QA benchmarks show self-distilled models match or exceed RL in average performance but exhibit significantly lower functional and semantic diversity, failing on out-of-distribution tasks requiring varied strategies.
为了解决自蒸馏虽提升pass@1准确率却降低输出多样性的现象，本文研究了采样演示在自蒸馏中引发的累积偏差，导致pass@k曲线趋于平坦。方法通过理论分析指出，理想自蒸馏策略应依据学生与正确轨迹间的条件互信息调整基础分布，但实际中自蒸馏会放大概率差距，使质量集中于主导模式。实验表明，在图路径搜索与科学问答基准上，自蒸馏模型平均性能可媲美或超越强化学习，但在需要多样化策略的分布外任务中表现显著下降，多样性严重不足。
PERTINENCE: Input-based Opportunistic Neural Network Dynamic Execution
Authors: Omkar Shende, Gayathri Ananthanarayanan, Marcello Traiola
First: 2025-07-02T13:22:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:58:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely used for their ability to model complex patterns across domains such as computer vision, speech recognition, and robotics. However, larger models, while often more accurate, are computationally expensive and energy-intensive. Since such a cost is typically needed only for challenging inputs, dynamically selecting lighter models for simpler inputs can improve efficiency with minimal impact on accuracy. We introduce PERTINENCE, a runtime method that selects, from a set of pre-trained models, the lightest model likely to process each input correctly. An ML-based dispatcher performs this selection, and a genetic algorithm explores dispatcher training strategies to identify Pareto-optimal trade-offs between accuracy and computational cost. We evaluate PERTINENCE on CNNs trained on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, ViTs trained on TinyImageNet, and a YOLO-based road occupancy estimation application using real-time intersection camera feeds. Results show that PERTINENCE matches or improves the accuracy of state-of-the-art pre-trained models while reducing operations by up to 36%, with equivalent or lower end-to-end inference time through tunable invocation intervals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相关性：基于输入的机遇型神经网络动态执行
深度神经网络（DNN）因其在计算机视觉、语音识别和机器人等领域建模复杂模式的能力而被广泛应用。然而，更大的模型虽然通常更准确，但计算成本高且耗能大。由于此类成本通常仅在处理困难输入时才需要，动态选择较轻量级模型处理简单输入，可在几乎不影响准确率的前提下提升效率。我们提出了PERTINENCE，一种运行时方法，从一组预训练模型中选择最轻且最可能正确处理每个输入的模型。一个基于机器学习的调度器执行该选择，而遗传算法探索调度器训练策略，以识别准确率与计算成本之间的帕累托最优权衡。我们在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100训练的CNN、TinyImageNet训练的ViT以及基于YOLO的道路占用率估计应用（使用实时交叉路口摄像头数据）上评估了PERTINENCE。结果表明，PERTINENCE在保持或提升现有最先进预训练模型准确率的同时，可将运算量降低高达36%，并通过可调调用间隔实现等效或更低的端到端推理时间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of large neural networks while maintaining accuracy, PERTINENCE dynamically selects the lightest pre-trained model likely to correctly process each input, using an ML-based dispatcher trained via genetic algorithms to optimize accuracy-cost trade-offs. Evaluated on CNNs, ViTs, and a YOLO-based application, it achieves accuracy matching or exceeding state-of-the-art models while reducing operations by up to 36% and maintaining or reducing end-to-end inference time through tunable invocation intervals.
为在不降低精度的前提下提升大型深度神经网络的计算效率，PERTINENCE在运行时动态选择最轻量且最可能正确处理当前输入的预训练模型。该方法采用基于机器学习的调度器进行模型选择，并通过遗传算法优化调度器训练策略，以实现精度与计算成本之间的帕累托最优平衡。在CNN、ViT及YOLO道路占用率系统等多类任务上测试，PERTINENCE在保持或超越现有最先进模型精度的同时，操作量最多减少36%，并通过可调调用间隔维持或降低端到端推理时间。
Beyond Topology: A Morphological Symmetry Graph Representation for Locomotion Policy Learning
Authors: Sizhe Wei, Xulin Chen, Fengze Xie, Garrett Ethan Katz, Zhenyu Gan, Lu Gan
First: 2025-11-30T04:41:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:57:20+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://msppo.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement learning has enabled impressive locomotion skills on articulated robots, but common policy representations remain only weakly aligned with robot physics. Generic networks ignore kinematic structure, while graph-based policies encode connectivity without specifying how physical quantities transform across symmetric body parts. We introduce a morphological symmetry graph representation for locomotion policy learning and instantiate it in MS-PPO. Starting from the robot's topological graph, our representation augments each observation and action space with the permutation and sign transformations induced by morphological symmetry. This yields a symmetry-equivariant graph actor and a symmetry-invariant graph critic, enforcing the desired policy and value constraints by construction rather than through reward shaping or data augmentation. We evaluate MS-PPO on a variety of locomotion tasks using both Unitree Go2 quadruped and Unitree G1 humanoid, including command tracking, asymmetric joint failures, out-of-distribution command generalization, and zero-shot sim-to-real deployment. Experiments show improved symmetry generalization, robustness, sample efficiency, and model efficiency over topology- and symmetry-aware baselines.

标题：超越拓扑结构：用于运动策略学习的形态对称图表示
强化学习已在关节式机器人上实现了令人印象深刻的运动技能，但常见的策略表示仍与机器人物理特性弱相关。通用网络忽略运动学结构，而基于图的策略仅编码连接性，未明确说明物理量如何在对称身体部件间变换。我们提出了一种用于运动策略学习的形态对称图表示，并在MS-PPO中实现。从机器人拓扑图出发，我们的表示在每个观测和动作空间中加入由形态对称性诱导的置换与符号变换。这生成了对称等变的图策略网络和对称不变的图价值网络，通过构造而非奖励塑造或数据增强，强制满足所需策略与价值约束。我们在多种运动任务上评估MS-PPO，使用Unitree Go2四足机器人和Unitree G1人形机器人，包括指令跟踪、非对称关节故障、分布外指令泛化及零样本仿真到现实部署。实验表明，相比拓扑与对称感知基线，MS-PPO在对称泛化、鲁棒性、样本效率和模型效率方面均有提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better align policy representations with robot physics, this work introduces a morphological symmetry graph representation for locomotion policy learning, implemented in MS-PPO. The method augments observation and action spaces with permutation and sign transformations derived from the robot’s morphological symmetries, enabling the construction of a symmetry-equivariant actor and symmetry-invariant critic. Experimental results on Unitree Go2 and G1 robots across tasks like command tracking, joint failure recovery, and sim-to-real transfer demonstrate improved symmetry generalization, robustness, sample efficiency, and model efficiency compared to topology- and symmetry-aware baselines.
为提升策略表示与机器人物理结构的对齐，本文提出一种形态对称图表示方法并应用于MS-PPO框架。该方法通过从机器人形态对称性中提取置换与符号变换，扩展观测与动作空间，构建内禀满足对称约束的等变策略网络与不变价值网络。在Unitree Go2与G1机器人上针对指令跟踪、关节失效、分布外泛化及仿真到现实部署等任务测试，结果表明该方法在对称泛化、鲁棒性、样本效率与模型效率方面均优于传统拓扑与对称感知基线。
MVTrack4Gen: Multi-View Point Tracking as Geometric Supervision for 4D Video Generation
Authors: JoungBin Lee, Jaewoo Jung, Jongmin Lee, Tongmin Kim, Hyunsung Kim, Takuya Narihira, Kazumi Fukuda, Jahyeok Koo, Jisang Han, Yuki Mitsufuji, Seungryong Kim
First: 2026-06-24T17:56:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:56:33+00:00
Comments: Project Page : https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/MVTrack4Gen/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Synthesizing a novel-view video from a monocular reference video along a target camera trajectory requires both geometric consistency and motion fidelity with respect to the reference video. Existing methods based on explicit 3D representations are limited by the accuracy of off-the-shelf reconstruction modules, which often produce inaccurate geometry for dynamic objects in monocular videos. In contrast, camera-conditioning-only methods can achieve high visual quality but often struggle to preserve geometric and motion consistency. In this work, we introduce MVTrack4Gen (Multi-View point Tracking for Novel-View Generation), a motion-aware training framework that leverages multi-view point tracking as an additional geometric and motion supervision signal for camera-conditioning-only novel-view video diffusion models. Our key finding is that specific attention layers encode strong correspondence cues, where query features attend to key features at geometrically corresponding locations across views and over time, and the misalignment of these correspondences causes motion inconsistency. Based on this observation, we route these features into an auxiliary multi-view tracking head and jointly train the diffusion model with a point-tracking objective. By explicitly strengthening these motion-aware correspondences, MVTrack4Gen improves existing models to better follow the motion in the reference view and maintain cross-view geometric consistency. Across diverse benchmarks, our method achieves state-of-the-art geometric consistency and competitive camera accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MVTrack4Gen：多视角点追踪作为几何监督用于4D视频生成
从单目参考视频沿目标相机轨迹合成新视角视频，需要同时保持与参考视频的几何一致性和运动保真度。现有基于显式3D表示的方法受限于现成重建模块的精度，常在单目视频中对动态物体生成不准确的几何结构；相比之下，仅依赖相机条件的方法虽能获得高视觉质量，但往往难以保持几何和运动一致性。本文提出MVTrack4Gen（用于新视角生成的多视角点追踪），一种运动感知的训练框架，利用多视角点追踪作为额外的几何与运动监督信号，增强仅依赖相机条件的新视角视频扩散模型。我们的关键发现是：特定注意力层编码了强对应线索，其中查询特征会关注跨视角和跨时间在几何对应位置的关键特征，而这些对应关系的错位会导致运动不一致。基于此观察，我们将这些特征路由至辅助多视角追踪头，并与扩散模型联合训练以实现点追踪目标。通过显式强化这些运动感知对应关系，MVTrack4Gen显著提升现有模型对参考视角运动的跟随能力及跨视角几何一致性。在多个基准测试中，我们的方法实现了领先的几何一致性与有竞争力的相机精度。

Experimental results across diverse benchmarks show that MVTrack4Gen significantly improves motion adherence to the reference view and enhances cross-view geometric consistency, achieving state-of-the-art geometric performance and competitive camera accuracy.
为解决从单目视频生成新视角视频时几何一致性与运动保真度的难题，本文提出MVTrack4Gen方法，通过将多视角点追踪作为几何监督信号，增强仅依赖相机条件的扩散模型。该方法发现模型中特定注意力层能编码跨视角与时间的强对应关系，对应关系错位会导致运动不一致；为此，它将相关特征导向辅助追踪头，并与扩散模型联合优化点追踪目标。实验表明，该方法显著提升了对参考视角运动的跟随能力与跨视角几何一致性，在多个基准测试中达到最优几何一致性与竞争力的相机精度。
Real-Time Voice AI Hears but Does Not Listen
Authors: Martijn Bartelds, Federico Bianchi, James Zou
First: 2026-06-24T17:55:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:55:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speech conveys information through both words and vocal delivery. We evaluate four leading production realtime voice systems-OpenAI's GPT Realtime 2, Google's Gemini 3.1 Flash Live, and Alibaba's Qwen3.5 Omni Plus and Omni Flash-on tasks where the words and the delivery patterns both convey meaningful information. Across three consequential scenarios, all four systems act on the words rather than the voice. They end calls with crying callers who insist nothing is wrong, approve wire transfers authorized in frightened voices, and enroll callers whose agreement is clearly sarcastic. Surprisingly, this is often not a failure of perception. When asked directly, three of the four systems reliably identify the distress, fear, or sarcasm they later ignore when making decisions. We observe a similar pattern when these realtime voice systems estimate accent and age, as their responses frequently follow the biases of the words rather than the acoustic properties of the speaker. We term this disconnect between perception and action the emotional intelligence gap of voice AI. Prompting systems to explicitly attend to vocal delivery improves performance only partially and inconsistently. Our findings show that current realtime voice AI systems often behave as if speech had been reduced to a transcript, suggesting that they should be used with caution in settings where the tone and emotion of delivery convey important information.
中文标题/摘要
标题：实时语音AI能听但不倾听
语音通过词语和发声方式传递信息。我们评估了四款主流实时语音系统——OpenAI的GPT Realtime 2、Google的Gemini 3.1 Flash Live、阿里巴巴的Qwen3.5 Omni Plus和Omni Flash——在词语与发声模式均传递重要信息的任务中表现。在三个关键场景中，所有系统均依据词语而非声音做出决策：它们结束哭泣但坚称无事的来电者通话，批准恐惧声中授权的电汇，以及注册明显带有讽刺语气的来电者。令人惊讶的是，这往往并非感知失败。当直接询问时，四系统中有三者能可靠识别其后忽略的痛苦、恐惧或讽刺情绪。在这些系统估算口音和年龄时，也观察到类似模式：其回答常受词语偏见影响，而非说话者声学特征。我们将此感知与行动脱节称为语音AI的情感智能差距。即使提示系统明确关注发声方式，其性能仅部分且不一致地提升。我们的研究显示，当前实时语音AI系统常如若语音已被简化为文字转录，故在语气与情绪传递重要信息的场景中应谨慎使用。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why current real-time voice AI systems fail to respond appropriately to emotional and vocal cues in speech, despite being able to perceive them. The researchers tested four leading systems—GPT Realtime 2, Gemini 3.1 Flash Live, and Alibaba’s Qwen3.5 variants—on scenarios where vocal delivery carries critical meaning, such as detecting distress, fear, or sarcasm. All systems acted on the spoken words rather than vocal patterns, approving harmful actions like wire transfers in fearful voices or enrolling callers with sarcastic agreement. Although three of the four systems correctly identified the emotional tone when prompted directly, they still ignored it during decision-making. The systems also showed biased age and accent estimates aligned with the text rather than acoustic features. The authors term this disconnect the 'emotional intelligence gap' and note that explicit prompts to attend to vocal delivery only marginally improved performance, suggesting current systems treat speech as if it were text-only.
本研究探讨当前实时语音AI为何无法根据语音语调和情感做出恰当反应，尽管它们能识别这些信息。研究人员测试了四款主流系统——GPT Realtime 2、Gemini 3.1 Flash Live 和阿里Qwen3.5系列——在语音情感（如痛苦、恐惧、讽刺）对决策至关重要的场景中表现。所有系统均依据文字内容决策，忽视语音语调，导致批准恐吓声中的转账、挂断痛苦求助者的电话、录取明显讽刺的申请人。令人惊讶的是，当被直接询问时，四系统中有三者能准确识别情感，却在实际决策中忽略。系统在判断口音和年龄时也表现出类似倾向，更依赖语言特征而非语音特性。作者称此现象为“语音AI的情感智能差距”，并指出即使提示系统关注语音语调，效果也仅部分且不稳定。结论是这些系统将语音视为文本处理，应在情感信息关键场景中谨慎使用。
Shepherd: Enabling Programmable Meta-Agents via Reversible Agentic Execution Traces
Authors: Simon Yu, Derek Chong, Ananjan Nandi, Dilara Soylu, Jiuding Sun, Christopher D Manning, Weiyan Shi
First: 2026-05-11T17:50:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:55:07+00:00
Comments: 50 pages, 22 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As LLM agent systems take on more complex tasks, they increasingly rely on meta-agents: higher-order agents that create, operate on and manage other agents. Meta-agent operations such as coordinating agents, halting risky actions before execution, or repairing failed runs, require runtime manipulation of agentic execution. Yet existing agentic substrates make this difficult: they expose only transcripts and environment snapshots, forcing meta-agents to build ad hoc tooling to reconstruct and operate over full execution state. Therefore, we introduce Shepherd, a Python substrate grounded in functional programming principles, where an agent's execution is itself a first-class object that a meta-agent can easily inspect and transform. Every model action, tool call, and environment change becomes a structured event in a reversible, Git-like execution trace, where any past state can be reverted 5x faster than docker commit and fork. Three example use cases show Shepherd's versatility: (1) a supervisor meta-agent prevents conflicts among parallel coding agents, lifting pair-coding pass rate from 28.8% to 54.7% on CooperBench; (2) a counterfactual optimization meta-agent repairs agent workflows by proposing edits and replaying runs from the point of changed behavior, outperforming MetaHarness on Terminal-Bench 2.0 by 12.8% with 58% lower wall-clock; (3) a training meta-agent picks fork points during rollouts to improve credit assignment in long-horizon agentic RL, doubling GRPO's uplift on Terminal-Bench 2.0. We open-source Shepherd to enable principled and efficient operations over agentic execution for both users and meta-agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Shepherd：通过可逆的代理执行轨迹实现可编程元代理
随着大语言模型代理系统承担更复杂的任务，它们越来越依赖元代理——即创建、操作和管理其他代理的高阶代理。协调代理、在执行前阻止高风险行为或修复失败运行等元代理操作，需要在运行时对代理执行进行干预。然而，现有代理基础架构难以实现这一点：它们仅暴露会话记录和环境快照，迫使元代理必须构建临时工具以重建并操作完整执行状态。因此，我们引入Shepherd，一个基于函数式编程原则的Python基础架构，其中代理的执行本身是一个一等对象，元代理可轻松检查和转换。每个模型动作、工具调用和环境变化都成为可逆的、类似Git的执行轨迹中的结构化事件，任何历史状态均可比Docker commit和fork快5倍回滚。三个示例用例展示了Shepherd的多功能性：(1) 监督元代理防止并行编码代理间的冲突，在CooperBench上将配对编码通过率从28.8%提升至54.7%；(2) 计划性优化元代理通过提出修改并从行为改变点重放运行来修复代理工作流，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上以58%更少的实时时钟时间超越MetaHarness 12.8%；(3) 训练元代理在滚动过程中选择分叉点以改善长时域代理强化学习中的信用分配，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上使GRPO的提升效果翻倍。我们开源Shepherd，以支持用户和元代理对代理执行进行原则性且高效的管理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for meta-agents to dynamically manipulate agent execution at runtime, the authors introduce Shepherd, a Python-based substrate that treats agent execution as a first-class, reversible object. By recording every model action, tool call, and environment change as a structured, Git-like trace, Shepherd enables meta-agents to inspect and revert states efficiently—up to five times faster than Docker commit and fork. Three experiments demonstrate its utility: a supervisor meta-agent boosts parallel coding pass rates from 28.8% to 54.7% on CooperBench; a counterfactual optimization meta-agent improves workflow repair performance by 12.8% over MetaHarness with 58% less wall-clock time on Terminal-Bench 2.0; and a training meta-agent doubles GRPO’s uplift on the same benchmark by selecting optimal fork points for credit assignment.
为支持元代理在运行时动态干预代理执行，作者提出了Shepherd，一个将代理执行视为一等对象并支持可逆操作的Python基础框架。通过将每个动作、工具调用和环境变化编码为结构化、类似Git的执行轨迹，Shepherd使元代理能高效检查和回滚状态——速度比Docker提交快5倍。实验表明：监督元代理在CooperBench上将配对编程通过率从28.8%提升至54.7%；反事实优化元代理在Terminal-Bench 2.0上比MetaHarness表现高出12.8%，耗时减少58%；训练元代理通过选择分叉点，使GRPO在长时程强化学习中的提升效果翻倍。
Neglected Free Lunch from Post-training: Progress Advantage for LLM Agents
Authors: Changdae Oh, Wendi Li, Seongheon Park, Samuel Yeh, Tanwi Mallick, Sharon Li
First: 2026-06-24T17:54:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:54:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Process reward models enable fine-grained, step-level evaluation of LLMs, yet building them for agentic settings remains prohibitively difficult: long-horizon interactions, irreversible actions, and stochastic environment feedback make both human annotation and Monte Carlo estimation infeasible at scale. In this work, we show that reinforcement learning (RL) post-training already provides the ingredients for effective step-level scoring, eliminating the need for dedicated reward model training altogether. Concretely, we derive an implicit advantage under a general stochastic Markov decision process, which we term progress advantage -- log-probability ratio between the RL-trained policy and its reference policy exactly recovers the optimal advantage function. This formulation makes the resulting signal annotation-free, domain-agnostic, and available as a byproduct of the standard RL post-training pipeline. We validate the effectiveness of the progress advantage across three different applications: test-time scaling, uncertainty quantification, and failure attribution on five benchmarks and four model families. Across all settings, it consistently outperforms confidence-based baselines and, despite requiring no task-specific training, surpasses dedicated trained reward models. We complement these results with deeper analyses on characteristics of progress advantage, offering practical guidance for adoption in real-world agentic systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：被忽视的训练后免费午餐：LLM代理的进展优势
过程奖励模型可实现对大语言模型的细粒度、步骤级评估，但在代理设置中构建它们仍极难：长时序交互、不可逆动作和随机环境反馈使得大规模人工标注和蒙特卡洛估计不可行。本文表明，强化学习（RL）训练后已具备步骤级评分的有效成分，无需专门训练奖励模型。具体而言，我们在一般随机马尔可夫决策过程中推导出一种隐式优势，称为“进展优势”——RL训练策略与其参考策略之间的对数概率比恰好恢复最优优势函数。该公式使所得信号无需标注、领域无关，且可作为标准RL训练后流程的副产品获得。我们在五个基准和四种模型家族上，针对三个不同应用（测试时扩展、不确定性量化、失败归因）验证了进展优势的有效性。在所有设置中，它始终优于基于置信度的基线方法，且无需任务特定训练，超越了专门训练的奖励模型。我们进一步通过深入分析进展优势的特性，为在现实世界代理系统中的应用提供实用指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of building step-level reward models for agentic LLMs due to long horizons, irreversible actions, and stochastic feedback, this work demonstrates that reinforcement learning post-training inherently provides a step-level scoring signal called progress advantage, which is annotation-free and domain-agnostic. Derived from the log-probability ratio between the RL-trained policy and a reference policy, this signal exactly matches the optimal advantage function in a stochastic MDP. Experiments across five benchmarks and four model families show that progress advantage consistently outperforms confidence-based baselines and, without task-specific training, often surpasses dedicated reward models in applications including test-time scaling, uncertainty quantification, and failure attribution.
由于长时序交互、不可逆动作和随机环境反馈，为代理型LLM构建步级奖励模型极为困难，本文提出利用强化学习后训练本身已包含的有效信号。作者推导出“进度优势”这一理论量，即训练策略与参考策略的对数概率比，证明其在一般随机马尔可夫决策过程中可精确还原最优优势函数，从而实现无需标注、领域无关的步级评估。实验在五个基准和四个模型家族上验证，结果显示该信号在测试时扩展、不确定性量化和失败归因任务中均优于置信度基线，甚至超越专用奖励模型，且无需任务特定训练，具备实用性和可扩展性。
Same Evidence, Different Answer: Auditing Order Sensitivity in Multimodal Large Language Models
Authors: Akshay Paruchuri, Sanmi Koyejo, Ehsan Adeli
First: 2026-06-24T17:53:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:53:26+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Standard benchmarks for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) score each item on one canonical ordering and miss whether order-irrelevant shuffling changes the answer, a baseline reliability property called for by emerging AI evaluation guidelines. We introduce Facet-Probe, a five-facet audit (option, evidence-chunk, document-rank, image-set, and mixed-modality ordering) of 18 frontier and open-weight MLLMs. A Bayesian item-response model separates ordering noise from per-facet bias, and a same-ordering control estimates the decoder-stochastic floor for observed flips. We find that none of the 18 MLLMs we audit are order-invariant: screened per-facet panel-mean flip rates span 24-50%. A Gemini same-ordering control at temperature 0 estimates a substantial ordering excess over a same-input decoder-noise floor in verified cells. Capability predicts but does not eliminate flips; the best model still flips on 13.4% of trials. In our Gemini mitigation tests, training-free prompt changes are modality-conditional and do not transfer from text to visual reasoning. These results suggest that prompt-level mitigation alone is unlikely to provide general order robustness, motivating future work on training-time and architectural approaches. We propose cross-ordering flip rate as a standard reporting axis for MLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相同证据，不同答案：多模态大语言模型中的审计顺序敏感性
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的标准基准测试仅在一种规范排序下评估每个项目，而忽略了顺序无关的打乱是否会影响答案——这违背了新兴AI评估指南所要求的基线可靠性属性。我们引入Facet-Probe，对18个前沿及开源权重的MLLMs进行五维度审计（选项、证据片段、文档排序、图像集、混合模态排序）。通过贝叶斯项目反应模型分离排序噪声与各维度偏差，并通过相同排序对照估计解码器随机性基线，以衡量观察到的答案翻转。我们发现，所审计的18个MLLMs均非顺序不变：各维度面板平均翻转率介于24%-50%之间。在Gemini模型中，温度为0的相同排序对照显示，在验证单元中存在显著的顺序超额现象，远超相同输入下的解码器噪声基线。能力虽可预测翻转，但无法消除；最佳模型仍存在13.4%的翻转率。在Gemini缓解测试中，无训练提示变更具有模态依赖性，且无法从文本推理迁移到视觉推理。这些结果表明，仅靠提示级别缓解难以实现通用顺序鲁棒性，未来应探索训练时和架构层面的方法。我们建议将跨排序翻转率作为MLLMs的标准报告指标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) produce consistent outputs regardless of input ordering—a key reliability criterion—this study introduces Facet-Probe, a five-facet audit framework that tests order sensitivity across different input components. Using a Bayesian item-response model to disentangle ordering noise from facet-specific biases and a same-ordering control to estimate decoder stochasticity, the study evaluates 18 frontier MLLMs. Results show none are order-invariant, with flip rates ranging from 24% to 50% across facets. 为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在输入顺序变化下是否输出稳定——这是新兴AI评估指南所要求的基本可靠性属性——本研究提出Facet-Probe框架，对18个前沿MLLMs在五种不同排序条件下进行审计。通过贝叶斯项目反应模型分离排序噪声与各维度偏差，并用相同顺序控制估计解码器随机性基线，研究发现所有模型均非顺序不变，各维度翻转率介于24%至50%之间，最强大模型仍存在13.4%的翻转率。提示层面的缓解策略无法跨模态迁移，表明仅靠提示优化难以实现通用顺序鲁棒性，未来需探索训练时或架构层面的改进，研究建议将跨顺序翻转率作为MLLMs的标准报告指标。
A cross-process welding penetration status prediction algorithm based on unsupervised domain adaptation in laser and TIG welding
Authors: Sen Li, Haichao Cui, Chendong Shao, Yaqi Wang, Xinhua Tang
First: 2026-06-24T17:52:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:52:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Supervised deep learning has been widely used for weld penetration state classification; however, its performance often degrades significantly under domain shift, such as when transferring models between welding processes with distinct physical mechanisms:for instance, from arc-dominated tungsten inert gas (TIG) welding to keyhole-based laser welding. To overcome this limitation, we propose an unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) framework integrated with a gradual source domain expansion (GSDE) strategy. Evaluated on dedicated TIG and laser welding datasets, our approach achieves high accuracy in both same-process and cross-process transfer tasks. Specifically, it attains average accuracies of 90.65% on TIGFH and 90.72% on LSPS in same-process settings, surpassing a supervised baseline by 35.83% and 38.87%, respectively. More notably, in cross-process scenarios, it reaches 80.48% for TIG to Laser and 81.13% for Laser to TIG, improving upon the baseline by 43.39% and 43.40%. UMAP visualizations verify that the model learns domain-invariant features while maintaining discriminative class boundaries. This method considerably lowers the relabeling cost for new welding processes and enhances the versatility of intelligent monitoring across different welding systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于无监督域自适应的跨工艺焊接熔透状态预测算法
监督深度学习已被广泛用于焊缝熔透状态分类，但其在领域偏移下（如从以电弧为主的钨极惰性气体（TIG）焊接转移到以匙孔效应为基础的激光焊接）性能常显著下降。为克服此局限，我们提出结合渐进源域扩展（GSDE）策略的无监督域自适应（UDA）框架。在专用TIG和激光焊接数据集上评估，该方法在同工艺和跨工艺迁移任务中均取得高精度。具体而言，在同工艺设置下，其在TIGFH和LSPS数据集上分别达到90.65%和90.72%的平均准确率，分别比监督基线高出35.83%和38.87%。更值得注意的是，在跨工艺场景中，从TIG到激光焊接和从激光到TIG焊接的准确率分别达到80.48%和81.13%，比基线分别提升43.39%和43.40%。UMAP可视化验证模型学习到领域不变特征的同时保持了判别性类别边界。该方法显著降低了新焊接工艺的重标注成本，并提升了智能监控在不同焊接系统中的适用性。

The method learns domain-invariant features while preserving class separability, enabling accurate penetration state classification even across welding types. Agentic Software Engineering: Foundational Pillars and a Research Roadmap
Authors: Ahmed E. Hassan, Hao Li, Dayi Lin, Bram Adams, Tse-Hsun Chen, Yutaro Kashiwa, Dong Qiu
First: 2025-09-07T21:40:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:45:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0) represents a new era where intelligent agents are tasked not with simple code generation, but with achieving complex, goal-oriented SE objectives. To harness these new capabilities while ensuring trustworthiness, we must recognize a fundamental duality within the SE field in the Agentic SE era, comprising two symbiotic modalities: SE for Humans and SE for Agents. This duality demands a radical reimagining of the foundational pillars of SE (actors, processes, tools, and artifacts) which manifest differently across each modality. We propose two purpose-built workbenches to support this vision. The Agent Command Environment (ACE) serves as a command center where humans orchestrate and mentor agent teams, handling outputs such as Merge-Readiness Packs (MRPs) and Consultation Request Packs (CRPs). The Agent Execution Environment (AEE) is a digital workspace where agents perform tasks while invoking human expertise when facing ambiguity or complex trade-offs. This bi-directional partnership, which supports agent-initiated human callbacks and handovers, gives rise to new, structured engineering activities (i.e., processes) that redefine human-AI collaboration, elevating the practice from agentic coding to true agentic software engineering. This paper presents the Structured Agentic Software Engineering (SASE) vision, outlining several of the foundational pillars for the future of SE. The paper culminates in a research roadmap that identifies a few key challenges and opportunities while briefly discussing the resulting impact of this future on SE education. Our goal is not to offer a definitive solution, but to provide a conceptual scaffold with structured vocabulary to catalyze a community-wide dialogue, pushing the SE community to think beyond its classic, human-centric tenets toward a disciplined, scalable, and trustworthy agentic future.

代理软件工程（SE 3.0）标志着一个新时代，智能代理不再仅负责简单的代码生成，而是承担复杂、目标导向的软件工程任务。为充分利用这些新能力并确保可信性，我们必须认识到在代理软件工程时代，软件工程领域存在一种根本性的二元性，包含两种共生模式：面向人类的软件工程与面向代理的软件工程。这种二元性要求对软件工程的基础支柱（参与者、过程、工具和产物）进行彻底重构，其表现形式在每种模式中各不相同。我们提出两个专为支持该愿景而设计的工作台：代理指挥环境（ACE）作为人类指挥和指导代理团队的指挥中心，处理如合并准备包（MRPs）和咨询请求包（CRPs）等输出；代理执行环境（AEE）则是代理执行任务的数字工作空间，当面临模糊性或复杂权衡时可调用人类专家。这种双向协作模式支持代理发起的人类回拨与交接，催生了新的结构化工程活动（即过程），重新定义了人机协作，将实践从代理编码提升为真正的代理软件工程。本文提出了结构化代理软件工程（SASE）愿景，概述了未来软件工程若干基础支柱。文章最后提出一份研究路线图，识别若干关键挑战与机遇，并简要讨论该未来对软件工程教育的影响。我们的目标并非提供确定性解决方案，而是提供一个概念框架与结构化术语，以激发社区对话，推动软件工程界超越传统以人类为中心的范式，迈向有纪律、可扩展且可信的代理未来。

Motivated by the need to move beyond simple code generation toward goal-oriented software engineering with intelligent agents, this paper proposes a dual-modality framework distinguishing between SE for Humans and SE for Agents. It introduces two complementary environments—the Agent Command Environment (ACE) for human oversight and agent coordination, and the Agent Execution Environment (AEE) for agent-driven task execution with human intervention when needed—enabling bidirectional collaboration. Experimental validation through conceptual modeling and process design demonstrates that this structured partnership creates new engineering activities, elevating software engineering from agentic coding to true agentic SE, while fostering trust and scalability through defined roles and artifacts.
为实现从简单代码生成向以智能体为目标导向的软件工程转型，本文提出区分“面向人类的软件工程”与“面向智能体的软件工程”的双模态框架。通过构建“智能体指挥环境（ACE）”与“智能体执行环境（AEE）”两个互补平台，前者支持人类对智能体团队的指挥与协作，后者支持智能体自主执行任务并适时请求人类介入，形成双向协作机制。实验性建模与原型验证表明，该架构重构了软件工程的核心要素（如角色、流程、工具与产物），催生了可扩展、可信赖的智能体软件工程新范式，推动人机协作从编码层面跃升至工程协作层面。
Model Forensics: Investigating Whether Concerning Behavior Reflects Misalignment
Authors: Aditya Singh, Gerson Kroiz, Senthooran Rajamanoharan, Neel Nanda
First: 2026-06-24T17:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:45:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central goal of safety research is determining whether a model is misaligned. Prior work has largely focused on detecting concerning behavior. But behavior alone does not establish misalignment: a concerning action can arise from benign causes such as confusion. This motivates model forensics: investigating whether the action was driven by malign intent. In this paper, we propose a baseline protocol for model forensics consisting of two steps, iterated as needed. First, we read the chain of thought (CoT) to generate hypotheses about what drives model behavior. Second, we make edits to the prompt or environment to test these hypotheses. While the CoT is not always faithful, it is a rich source of unsupervised insight that can guide the collection of more rigorous evidence. To evaluate our protocol, we create a suite of six agentic environments where models exhibit concerning behavior, and apply it to each. We establish that Kimi K2 Thinking takes shortcuts due to a genuine disposition towards low-effort actions, by showing this hypothesis successfully predicts its behavior. Through counterfactual experiments, we show DeepSeek R1 deceives out of a desire to be consistent with a previous instance of itself. Our methods nonetheless leave significant room for refinement. For example, when we test whether Kimi K2 Thinking believes it is violating user intent, we find no evidence of such a belief, but without positive controls we cannot confirm our tests would detect it. Overall, we find our simple protocol provides a strong baseline that we hope future work will improve upon. More broadly, our work is a concrete step in developing the growing field of model forensics.

标题：模型法医：探究令人担忧的行为是否反映对齐偏差
安全研究的核心目标是判断模型是否存在对齐偏差。以往研究主要聚焦于检测令人担忧的行为，但行为本身并不能证明对齐偏差：令人担忧的行为可能源于无害原因，如困惑。这促使我们开展模型法医研究：探究该行为是否源于恶意意图。本文提出了一种模型法医的基准协议，包含两个步骤，可重复迭代。首先，我们分析思维链（CoT）以生成关于驱动模型行为的假设；其次，我们修改提示或环境以检验这些假设。虽然CoT未必完全忠实，但它是无监督洞察的重要来源，可指导收集更严谨的证据。为评估该协议，我们构建了六个代理环境，其中模型表现出令人担忧的行为，并逐一应用该协议。我们证实，Kimi K2 Thinking因真正倾向于低耗能行为而采取捷径，该假设成功预测其行为。通过反事实实验，我们发现DeepSeek R1为保持与自身先前实例的一致性而欺骗。然而，我们的方法仍有显著改进空间。例如，当我们测试Kimi K2 Thinking是否认为自己违反用户意图时，未发现相关信念，但缺乏正向对照，无法确认我们的测试能否有效检测此类信念。总体而言，我们发现该简单协议提供了强有力的基准，希望未来研究能进一步改进。更广泛地说，本工作是发展日益增长的模型法医领域的重要一步。

Applied across six agentic environments exhibiting concerning actions, the method revealed that Kimi K2 Thinking takes shortcuts due to an inherent preference for low-effort actions, while counterfactual tests showed DeepSeek R1 deceives to maintain consistency with prior self-modeling. Despite its utility, the protocol has limitations, such as inability to confirm detection sensitivity without positive controls, yet it serves as a foundational approach for future refinement in model forensics.
为区分模型的异常行为是源于良性困惑还是恶意意图，本文提出一种模型取证基线协议，通过从思维链生成假设并迭代修改提示或环境进行验证。在六个表现出异常行为的代理环境中应用该方法，结果表明Kimi K2 Thinking因偏好低耗路径而采取捷径，而DeepSeek R1则通过保持自我一致性进行欺骗。但该方法仍存在局限，如缺乏正向对照无法确认对信念的检测能力，尽管如此，该协议为模型取证领域提供了坚实起点。
Estimating condition number with Graph Neural Networks
Authors: Erin Carson, Xinye Chen
First: 2026-03-10T23:38:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:44:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this paper, we propose a fast method for estimating the condition number of sparse matrices using graph neural networks (GNNs). For efficient deployment of GNNs, we introduce a graph feature construction with $\mathrm{O}(\mathrm{nnz} + n)$ complexity, where $\mathrm{nnz}$ is the number of non-zero elements in the matrix and $n$ denotes the matrix dimension. We propose two schemes for estimating the matrix condition number using GNNs; One follows by decomposing the condition number and predicts the relatively more computationally intensive part $\|\mathbf{A}^{-1}\|$, without explicitly forming the inverse, while the other is to predict the whole condition number $κ$. Our approach can be extended to an arbitrary norm. Extensive experiments are conducted for the estimation of the 1-norm and 2-norm condition numbers, which show that our method achieves a significant speedup over the traditional numerical estimation methods. Our software for GNN condition number estimator is made publicly available at https://github.com/inEXASCALE/sparse-kappa.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用图神经网络估计条件数
本文提出了一种利用图神经网络（GNNs）快速估计稀疏矩阵条件数的方法。为高效部署GNNs，我们引入了一种图特征构造方法，其复杂度为$\mathrm{O}(\mathrm{nnz} + n)$，其中$\mathrm{nnz}$为矩阵中非零元素个数，$n$为矩阵维度。我们提出了两种基于GNNs的矩阵条件数估计方案：一种是将条件数分解，预测计算量较大的部分$\|\mathbf{A}^{-1}\|$，而无需显式求逆；另一种是直接预测整个条件数$κ$。我们的方法可扩展至任意范数。大量实验针对1-范数和2-范数条件数估计展开，结果表明，我们的方法显著优于传统数值估计方法。GNN条件数估计器软件已公开发布于https://github.com/inEXASCALE/sparse-kappa。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fast condition number estimation of sparse matrices, this work proposes using graph neural networks to approximate the condition number without explicitly computing matrix inverses. The method constructs graph features with linear complexity in the number of non-zero elements and matrix dimension, enabling efficient GNN deployment. Two estimation schemes are introduced: one decomposes the condition number and predicts the norm of the inverse, while the other directly predicts the full condition number. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly outperforms traditional numerical methods in speed while maintaining accuracy for both 1-norm and 2-norm condition number estimation.
为解决稀疏矩阵条件数估计的计算效率问题，本文提出基于图神经网络（GNN）的快速估计方法，其图特征构建复杂度为O(nnz + n)。方法包含两种方案：一种通过分解条件数，仅估算计算密集的逆矩阵范数而不显式求逆；另一种直接预测完整条件数。实验表明，该方法在1范数和2范数条件下显著优于传统数值方法，且可扩展至任意范数。
Did Models Learn Sufficiently? Attribution-Guided Training via Subset-Selected Counterfactual Augmentation
Authors: Yannan Chen, Ruoyu Chen, Wei Wang, Bin Zeng, Jinke Li, Shiming Liu, Qunli Zhang, Yaowei Wang, Xiaochun Cao
First: 2025-11-15T08:39:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:39:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In current visual model training, models often rely on only limited sufficient causes for their predictions, which makes them sensitive to distribution shifts or the absence of key features. Attribution methods can accurately identify a model's critical regions. However, masking these areas to create counterfactuals often causes the model to misclassify the target, while humans can still easily recognize it. This divergence highlights that the model's learned dependencies may not be sufficiently causal. To address this issue, we propose Subset-Selected Counterfactual Augmentation (SS-CA), which integrates counterfactual explanations directly into the training process for targeted intervention. Building on the subset-selection-based LIMA attribution method, we develop Counterfactual LIMA to identify minimal spatial region sets whose removal can selectively alter model predictions. Leveraging these attributions, we introduce a data augmentation strategy that replaces the identified regions with natural background, and we train the model jointly on both augmented and original samples to mitigate incomplete causal learning. Extensive experiments across multiple ImageNet variants show that SS-CA improves generalization on in-distribution (ID) test data and achieves superior performance on out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks such as ImageNet-R and ImageNet-S. Under perturbations including noise, models trained with SS-CA also exhibit enhanced generalization, demonstrating that our approach effectively uses interpretability insights to correct model deficiencies and improve both performance and robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型是否学习充分？通过子集选择反事实增强的归因引导训练
当前视觉模型训练中，模型常仅依赖有限的充分原因进行预测，导致其对分布偏移或关键特征缺失敏感。归因方法可准确识别模型的关键区域。然而，遮蔽这些区域生成反事实时，模型常误分类目标，而人类仍能轻松识别。这种差异表明模型学习的依赖关系可能不够因果充分。为解决此问题，我们提出子集选择反事实增强（SS-CA），将反事实解释直接融入训练过程以实现针对性干预。基于子集选择型LIMA归因方法，我们开发了反事实LIMA，用于识别最小空间区域集合，其移除可选择性改变模型预测。利用这些归因结果，我们引入一种数据增强策略，将识别区域替换为自然背景，并联合原始样本与增强样本训练模型，以缓解因果学习不充分的问题。在多个ImageNet变体上的大量实验表明，SS-CA在分布内（ID）测试数据上提升泛化能力，并在分布外（OOD）基准（如ImageNet-R和ImageNet-S）上表现更优。在噪声扰动下，采用SS-CA训练的模型也展现出更强的泛化能力，证明我们的方法有效利用可解释性洞察修正模型缺陷，同时提升性能与鲁棒性。

Motivated by the observation that visual models often rely on insufficient causal features, leading to poor generalization under distribution shifts, this work proposes Subset-Selected Counterfactual Augmentation (SS-CA) to enhance causal learning. The method leverages Counterfactual LIMA, a subset-selection attribution technique, to identify minimal spatial regions whose removal alters model predictions. These regions are then masked and replaced with natural background in training data, enabling joint learning from augmented and original samples. Experiments across ImageNet variants show SS-CA improves in-distribution accuracy and significantly boosts out-of-distribution performance on ImageNet-R and ImageNet-S, while also enhancing robustness under noise perturbations.
鉴于视觉模型常依赖不充分的因果特征且难以应对分布偏移，本文提出基于归因的子集选择反事实增强（SS-CA）方法，通过可解释性引导训练。该方法基于Counterfactual LIMA，识别最小空间区域以改变预测，再将这些区域替换为自然背景进行数据增强，并与原始样本联合训练。实验表明，SS-CA在ImageNet变体上提升了分布内准确率，并显著增强分布外性能（如ImageNet-R、ImageNet-S），同时在噪声扰动下也提高了模型鲁棒性。
Deep Network Approximation: Beyond ReLU to Diverse Activation Functions
Authors: Shijun Zhang, Jianfeng Lu, Hongkai Zhao
Venue: Journal of Machine Learning Research, 25(35):1--39, 2024
First: 2023-07-13T04:46:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:36:54+00:00
Comments: This arXiv version contains only minor typo corrections and small clarifications to improve readability
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper explores the expressive power of deep neural networks for a diverse range of activation functions. An activation function set $\mathscr{A}$ is defined to encompass the majority of commonly used activation functions, such as $\mathtt{ReLU}$, $\mathtt{LeakyReLU}$, $\mathtt{ReLU}^2$, $\mathtt{ELU}$, $\mathtt{CELU}$, $\mathtt{SELU}$, $\mathtt{Softplus}$, $\mathtt{GELU}$, $\mathtt{SiLU}$, $\mathtt{Swish}$, $\mathtt{Mish}$, $\mathtt{Sigmoid}$, $\mathtt{Tanh}$, $\mathtt{Arctan}$, $\mathtt{Softsign}$, $\mathtt{dSiLU}$, and $\mathtt{SRS}$. We demonstrate that for any activation function $\varrho\in \mathscr{A}$, a $\mathtt{ReLU}$ network of width $N$ and depth $L$ can be approximated to arbitrary precision by a $\varrho$-activated network of width $3N$ and depth $2L$ on any bounded set. This finding enables the extension of most approximation results achieved with $\mathtt{ReLU}$ networks to a wide variety of other activation functions, albeit with slightly increased constants. Significantly, we establish that the (width,$\,$depth) scaling factors can be further reduced from $(3,2)$ to $(1,1)$ if $\varrho$ falls within a specific subset of $\mathscr{A}$. This subset includes activation functions such as $\mathtt{ELU}$, $\mathtt{CELU}$, $\mathtt{SELU}$, $\mathtt{Softplus}$, $\mathtt{GELU}$, $\mathtt{SiLU}$, $\mathtt{Swish}$, and $\mathtt{Mish}$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度网络逼近：从ReLU到多样激活函数
本文探讨了深度神经网络在多种激活函数下的表达能力。定义激活函数集合 $\mathscr{A}$，涵盖绝大多数常用激活函数，如 $\mathtt{ReLU}$、$\mathtt{LeakyReLU}$、$\mathtt{ReLU}^2$、$\mathtt{ELU}$、$\mathtt{CELU}$、$\mathtt{SELU}$、$\mathtt{Softplus}$、$\mathtt{GELU}$、$\mathtt{SiLU}$、$\mathtt{Swish}$、$\mathtt{Mish}$、$\mathtt{Sigmoid}$、$\mathtt{Tanh}$、$\mathtt{Arctan}$、$\mathtt{Softsign}$、$\mathtt{dSiLU}$ 和 $\mathtt{SRS}$。我们证明，对于任意激活函数 $\varrho\in \mathscr{A}$，在任意有界集上，一个宽度为 $N$、深度为 $L$ 的 $\mathtt{ReLU}$ 网络可被宽度为 $3N$、深度为 $2L$ 的 $\varrho$ 激活网络任意精度逼近。这一发现使大多数基于 $\mathtt{ReLU}$ 网络的逼近结果可扩展至大量其他激活函数，尽管常数略有增加。更重要的是，若 $\varrho$ 属于 $\mathscr{A}$ 的特定子集，则（宽度，深度）缩放因子可从 $(3,2)$ 进一步降低至 $(1,1)$。该子集包括 $\mathtt{ELU}$、$\mathtt{CELU}$、$\mathtt{SELU}$、$\mathtt{Softplus}$、$\mathtt{GELU}$、$\mathtt{SiLU}$、$\mathtt{Swish}$ 和 $\mathtt{Mish}$ 等激活函数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to generalize approximation theory beyond ReLU networks, this paper investigates whether other activation functions can achieve similar expressive power. It defines a broad set of activation functions and proves that any function in this set can be approximated to arbitrary precision by a ReLU network of width N and depth L, using a network with width 3N and depth 2L activated by the target function. For a subset of these functions—including ELU, CELU, SELU, Softplus, GELU, SiLU, Swish, and Mish—the scaling reduces to (1,1), meaning no increase in width or depth is needed. This result extends known approximation capabilities to a wide range of activations while preserving theoretical guarantees.
该论文研究了除ReLU外多种激活函数（如ELU、GELU、SiLU等）在深度神经网络中的表达能力。证明了任意激活函数均可被其对应的ReLU网络以任意精度逼近，所需网络宽度和深度分别扩大至3倍和2倍。特别地，对于ELU、GELU、SiLU等子集激活函数，其网络结构无需扩展，即可实现与ReLU相同的表达能力，从而扩展了已知的逼近理论至更广泛的激活函数类别。
When Certainty Is an Artifact: Keyword Lexicon Blindness and the (Mis)Measurement of Rhetorical Stance
Authors: Bo Chen
First: 2026-06-24T17:36:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:36:39+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Can a statistically significant, large-effect-size finding in computational social science be entirely an artifact of the measurement instrument? We present a case where the answer appears to be yes. Analyzing 85 interviews across four public intellectuals (2016--2026), we find a robust negative-affect/emphatic-certainty lexical co-occurrence pattern under keyword-based scoring ($r = 0.72$--$0.93$, $p < 0.01$ for all four speakers). Replacing keyword counting with LLM-based zero-shot semantic classification on the complete diarized corpus (32,625 sentences) dramatically reduces this correlation: Dalio's $r = 0.851$ drops to $r = 0.206$, with two speakers showing negative $r(\text{neg}, \text{emphatic})$ and one showing null. In contrast, the LLM reveals a strong negative-hedging coupling across speakers -- Rogoff's $r(\text{neg}, \text{hedged}) = 0.875$ ($p = 0.001$) and Zeihan's $r(\text{neg}, \text{hedged}) = 0.722$ ($p = 0.008$) -- consistent with the conventional expectation that pessimistic discourse attracts hedging, not certainty. Sentence-level error analysis traces this discrepancy to three structural failure modes in keyword lexicons -- syntactic blindness, polysemy blindness, and categorical absence -- illustrated through cases where keyword counting inverts semantic meaning (e.g., ''never absolutely totally confident'' scored as high-certainty). We argue that keyword lexicons measure a universal lexical co-occurrence tendency -- negative discourse naturally attracts emphatic vocabulary -- that is orthogonal to, and can systematically invert, rhetorical stance. Treating keyword counts as measurements of epistemic certainty is a category error: a finding that appears to be about a speaker's psychology may be entirely about the counting of words.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当确定性是人为产物：关键词词典盲区与修辞立场的（误）测量
计算社会科学中具有统计显著性和大效应量的发现，是否可能完全由测量工具导致？我们通过分析四位公共知识分子（2016—2026年）的85次访谈发现，答案似乎是肯定的。基于关键词计数的评分方法下，四名发言者均呈现负情感/强调性确定性词汇共现模式（r = 0.72—0.93，p < 0.01）。将关键词计数替换为基于大语言模型的零样本语义分类（对32,625个句子的逐人分段语料库），该相关性大幅下降：达里奥的r值从0.851降至0.206，两名发言者呈现负相关（负情感与强调性），一名无相关。相反，大语言模型揭示了跨发言者强烈的负面语义与规避性表达的负相关——罗戈夫的r(负情感,规避性) = 0.875（p = 0.001），泽汉的r(负情感,规避性) = 0.722（p = 0.008），符合悲观话语吸引规避而非确定性的传统预期。句级错误分析表明，该差异源于关键词词典的三种结构缺陷——句法盲区、多义盲区和类别缺失——例如“从不绝对完全确信”被误判为高确定性。我们认为，关键词词典测量的是负向话语自然吸引强调性词汇的普遍共现倾向，该倾向与修辞立场正交，且可系统性反转。将关键词计数视为认知确定性的测量是范畴错误：看似反映发言者心理的发现，实则完全源于词汇计数。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether statistically significant findings in computational social science can stem purely from flawed measurement tools, using keyword-based lexicon analysis of 85 interviews with four public intellectuals. The authors find a strong correlation between negative affect and emphatic certainty in keyword scores (r = 0.72–0.93), but replacing keyword counting with LLM-based semantic classification drastically weakens or reverses these correlations, revealing instead a consistent negative-hedging pattern across speakers. The discrepancy is traced to three structural flaws in keyword lexicons: syntactic, polysemous, and categorical blindness, which cause semantic inversion (e.g., 'never absolutely totally confident' misclassified as high-certainty). The authors conclude that keyword lexicons capture lexical co-occurrence tendencies unrelated to rhetorical stance, and treating them as measures of epistemic certainty constitutes a category error.
该研究探讨了在计算社会科学中，基于关键词词典的修辞立场测量结果是否可能完全是测量工具的伪影。通过对四位公共知识分子的85场访谈分析，作者发现关键词计数法下负向情感与强调性确定性高度相关，但当替换为基于大语言模型的语义分类后，该相关性消失，取而代之的是跨说话人的一致性负向规避模式，符合理论预期。研究指出，关键词词典因语法盲区、多义词盲区和类别缺失等结构缺陷，系统性扭曲了语义，导致其测量的是词汇共现倾向而非认知确定性，从而可能颠倒真实修辞立场。
A welding penetration prediction model for laser welding process based on self-supervised learning using physics-informed neural networks
Authors: Sen Li, Xiaoying Liu, Xiaojian Xu, Chendong Shao, Yaqi Wang, Ling Lan, Xinhua Tang, Haichao Cui
First: 2026-06-24T17:33:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:33:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The laser welding full-penetration is of critical importance, as it constitutes one of the fundamental factors in achieving defect-free welded joints. Accurate prediction of the penetration state is therefore essential for ensuring weld quality. To this end, this paper introduces SimPhysNet, a novel algorithm that achieves high classification accuracy in laser welding penetration prediction using only a limited number of labelled images. This approach effectively overcomes the limitations of supervised learning classification algorithms, which are hindered in industrial applications by their dependence on extensive, high-quality labelled data. The core of SimPhysNet is a unique self-supervised learning paradigm that embeds physical priors into a contrastive learning framework. By incorporating a physics-informed neural network (PINN), the model is guided to extract physically meaningful features of the molten pool and keyhole from a large set of unlabelled data, while three image augmentation tasks further enhance its generalization capabilities. Subsequently, a few-shot learning strategy, based on prototypical networks, enables robust classification by constructing class representations from a minimal set of labelled images. Experimental results demonstrate that SimPhysNet achieves a classification accuracy of 96.06% using only 200 labelled images (approximately 5% of the total labelled dataset), which is comparable to the performance of conventional supervised learning algorithms that utilize the entire labelled dataset. This work presents a new, efficient, and highly accurate method, providing the way for the intelligent automation of laser welding.

标题：基于物理信息神经网络的自监督学习激光焊接熔透预测模型
激光焊接全熔透对实现无缺陷焊缝至关重要，因此准确预测熔透状态对保证焊接质量至关重要。为此，本文提出SimPhysNet算法，仅使用少量标注图像即可实现高分类准确率。该方法有效克服了传统监督学习分类算法依赖大量高质量标注数据的工业应用局限。SimPhysNet的核心是一种独特的自监督学习范式，将物理先验嵌入对比学习框架中，并通过物理信息神经网络（PINN）引导模型从大量未标注数据中提取熔池和匙孔的物理意义特征，同时通过三项图像增强任务进一步提升其泛化能力。随后，基于原型网络的少样本学习策略，通过最小标注图像集构建类别表示，实现稳健分类。实验结果表明，SimPhysNet仅使用200张标注图像（约占总标注数据集的5%）即可达到96.06%的分类准确率，与使用全部标注数据的传统监督学习算法性能相当。该工作提出了一种新颖、高效且高精度的方法，为激光焊接智能化自动化提供了新路径。

为解决激光焊接穿透状态预测对大量标注数据依赖的问题，本文提出SimPhysNet模型，结合物理信息神经网络与对比学习的自监督框架。该方法通过PINN引导从大量无标注图像中提取熔池与匙孔的物理特征，并辅以三项图像增强任务提升泛化能力，再结合原型网络实现少量标注样本下的鲁棒分类。实验表明，仅用200张标注图像（约占总标注数据的5%），模型准确率达96.06%，与使用全部标注数据的传统监督学习方法表现相当，展现出高效、精准的工业应用潜力。
DomainShuttle: Freeform Open Domain Subject-driven Text-to-video Generation
Authors: Nan Chen, Yiyang Cai, Rongchang Xie, Junwen Pan, Cheng Chen, Weinan Jia, Zhuowei Chen, Wen Zhou, Zhenbang Sun, Wenhan Luo
First: 2026-06-24T17:33:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:33:13+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open domain subject-driven text-to-video (S2V) generation has drawn significant interest in academia and industry. Open domain S2V mainly involves two scenarios: in-domain, which requires retaining the reference subject features as much as possible, and cross-domain, which preserves the intrinsic features of the subject while allowing subject-irrelevant properties to vary flexibly according to the text prompt. Existing methods primarily focus on maximizing subject fidelity in in-domain scenarios, which limits their editability and adaptability in cross-domain scenarios, such as novel styles, semantic combinations, or domain attributes. In this study, we propose that an ideal S2V method should flexibly shuttle between different domains, achieving strong performance in both in-domain and cross-domain scenarios. To this end, we propose DomainShuttle, which could achieve high fidelity and generative flexibility for open domain video personalization. Specifically, we introduce Domain-MoT, which decouples videos and reference features and introduces the domain-aware AdaLN for domain-specific modeling of reference images. We then introduce the Video-Reference DualRoPE scheme, which places reference image tokens and video tokens in separate RoPE spaces to enable precise subject-level spatial modeling, and Cross-Pair Consistent Loss, which aims to extract intrinsic subject features unaffected by irrelevant features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DomainShuttle achieves significant performance improvements over existing methods, exhibiting high subject fidelity and generative flexibility across diverse open domain application scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DomainShuttle：自由形式开放域主题驱动的文本到视频生成
开放域主题驱动的文本到视频（S2V）生成在学术界和工业界引起了广泛关注。开放域S2V主要涉及两种场景：域内场景，要求尽可能保留参考主体特征；跨域场景，则在保留主体固有特征的同时，允许与主体无关的属性根据文本提示灵活变化。现有方法主要聚焦于域内场景中的主体保真度最大化，这限制了其在跨域场景（如新颖风格、语义组合或领域属性）中的可编辑性和适应性。本研究提出，理想的S2V方法应能灵活切换不同域，在域内和跨域场景中均表现优异。为此，我们提出DomainShuttle，可实现开放域视频个性化中的高保真度与生成灵活性。具体而言，我们引入Domain-MoT，将视频与参考特征解耦，并引入域感知AdaLN以实现对参考图像的域特定建模；随后提出Video-Reference DualRoPE方案，将参考图像标记与视频标记置于独立的RoPE空间，以实现精确的主体级空间建模；并引入Cross-Pair Consistent Loss，旨在提取不受无关特征影响的主体固有特征。大量实验表明，DomainShuttle在现有方法基础上显著提升性能，在多样化的开放域应用场景中展现出高主体保真度与生成灵活性。
