Driven by the need for flexible and adaptable text-to-video generation across diverse domains, this study introduces DomainShuttle, a framework designed to balance subject fidelity and generative freedom in open-domain scenarios. It achieves this by decoupling video content from reference features using Domain-MoT and domain-aware AdaLN, enabling precise modeling of subject attributes while allowing style and context variations. The Video-Reference DualRoPE scheme further enhances subject-level spatial alignment by separating reference and video token embeddings, while Cross-Pair Consistent Loss ensures intrinsic subject features remain stable despite irrelevant changes. Experiments show DomainShuttle outperforms existing methods in both in-domain fidelity and cross-domain creativity, demonstrating strong adaptability across varied prompts and domains.
为实现跨域场景下兼具高保真度与生成灵活性的主体驱动文本到视频生成，本文提出DomainShuttle框架，兼顾域内与跨域场景表现。其核心包括Domain-MoT模块，用于解耦视频与参考特征并引入域感知AdaLN进行针对性建模；Video-Reference DualRoPE机制将参考图像与视频token置于独立RoPE空间以实现精准空间对齐；Cross-Pair Consistent Loss则确保提取的主体特征不受无关属性干扰。实验表明，DomainShuttle在多种开放域应用中显著优于现有方法，兼具高保真度与强生成灵活性。
The Unfireable Safety Kernel: Execution-Time AI Alignment for AI Agents and Other Escapable AI Systems
Authors: Seth Dobrin, Łukasz Chmiel
First: 2026-06-24T17:32:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:32:27+00:00
Comments: Pre-print submitted for publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents are granted access to tools, APIs, and other infrastructure, making them active principals in those systems. The dominant approach places controls inside the agent's own runtime: system prompts, output filters, and guardrail libraries. Any control in the agent's address space is reachable by inputs that influence it; this generalizes to any AI system with sufficient reach into its own runtime, a class we term escapable AI systems. We identify four properties that an authorization mechanism must satisfy for architectural control rather than for cooperative requests: process separation, pre-action enforcement on a structurally only path, fail-closed at both the request and system levels, and externalized signed evidence verifiable outside the controlled system's trust boundary. We position this layer as execution-time AI alignment, complementing training-time alignment (RLHF, Constitutional AI) and inference-time alignment. We present the Unfireable Safety Kernel, a Rust reference implementation realizing all four. Its fail-closed invariant is machine-checked at two levels: an SMT theorem (Z3) and an exhaustive bounded-model-checking proof of the production decision function (Kani, 4/4 harnesses). A Python-to-Rust migration was gated on byte-equivalence (1000/1000 fixtures; 17/17 adversarial classes). We evaluate the kernel governing a live, escapable AI system, a deterministic, self-improving world model, against an escape-seeking adversary driving its real self-modification seam: across 1,000 self-modifications, all 704 attempts on the safety-critical core are refused, with no escape; a further 300, under the operator kill switch, are also refused. A separate campaign of 6,240 authorization round-trips had no successful bypass. Against 3 contemporary systems claiming the agent control plane, the agent invokes control; here, it lacks that choice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不可解除的安全内核：面向AI代理及其他可逃逸AI系统的运行时对齐
AI代理被授予访问工具、API及其他基础设施的权限，使其成为这些系统的主动主体。主流方法将控制置于代理自身的运行时内部：系统提示、输出过滤器和护栏库。任何位于代理地址空间内的控制均可被影响它的输入所触及；这推广到任何能充分触及自身运行时的AI系统，我们称之为可逃逸AI系统。 我们定义了授权机制必须满足的四项属性，以实现架构级控制而非协作请求：进程隔离、仅在结构上唯一路径上的预操作强制执行、请求与系统层面的失败关闭，以及外部可验证的签名证据。我们将此层定位为运行时AI对齐，补充训练时对齐（RLHF、宪法式AI）和推理时对齐。 我们提出了不可解除安全内核（Unfireable Safety Kernel），一个用Rust实现的参考实现，满足上述四项属性。其失败关闭不变式在两个层级上由机器验证：SMT定理（Z3）和生产决策函数的穷举有界模型检验证明（Kani，4/4 harnesses）。Python到Rust的迁移基于字节等价性（1000/1000测试用例；17/17对抗类）。我们评估了该内核在控制一个真实、可逃逸AI系统——一个确定性、自我改进的世界模型——时的表现，对抗驱动其真实自我修改接口的逃逸寻求对手：在1000次自我修改中，所有704次针对安全关键核心的尝试均被拒绝，无一逃逸；另外300次在操作员关闭开关下同样被拒绝。另一项6240次授权往返的独立测试中，无一次成功绕过。针对3个声称控制代理平面的当代系统，代理可调用控制；此处，它无此选择。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that AI agents can exploit access to their own runtime to bypass safety controls, the paper proposes execution-time AI alignment via the Unfireable Safety Kernel, a Rust-based system enforcing four key properties: process separation, pre-action enforcement on a single structural path, fail-closed behavior at both request and system levels, and externalized verifiable evidence. The kernel’s safety is formally verified using SMT and bounded model checking, and its correctness is validated through a Python-to-Rust migration with 100% byte equivalence across adversarial classes. In experiments, the kernel successfully blocked all 704 attempts to modify safety-critical components during 1,000 self-modifications and 300 under a kill switch, while 6,240 authorization attempts were also blocked. Crucially, when compared to three competing systems, the agent was unable to invoke control, demonstrating the kernel’s architectural enforcement capability.
为应对AI代理可能利用自身运行时内的控制机制逃避安全约束的风险，本文提出通过“不可触发的安全内核”实现运行时AI对齐，该内核基于Rust语言，强制执行四个关键安全属性：进程隔离、仅单一结构路径上的预操作强制执行、请求与系统层面的失效关闭、以及可外部验证的签名证据。其设计通过SMT定理证明和模型检测进行形式化验证，Python到Rust的迁移也通过字节等价性验证。实验中，该内核在真实可逃逸AI系统中成功拦截了704次安全关键自修改尝试和300次触发操作开关的修改，同时在6240次授权绕过尝试中无一成功。与三类现有系统对比，本文系统中的代理无法调用控制权限，凸显其架构优势。
When Does Synthetic Data Augmentation Improve Score-Based Imbalanced Classification?
Authors: Zhengchi Ma, Pengfei Lyu, Anru R. Zhang
First: 2026-06-24T17:30:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:30:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Synthetic data augmentation is widely used to mitigate class imbalance, but its theoretical effects on score-based classification remain poorly understood. This paper develops a framework for characterizing when synthetic minority augmentation can improve threshold-integrated and threshold-optimized metrics, including AUROC, AUPRC, best-threshold balanced accuracy, and best-threshold \(\F_1\) score. We separate the effect of augmentation into two components: a change in effective class weighting and a discrepancy between the synthetic and true minority distributions. Under well-specified score models, the raw estimator already targets the likelihood-ratio ordering, which is population-optimal for the metrics considered. Consequently, augmentation cannot provide a fundamental population-level improvement beyond possible finite-sample variance reduction, and may introduce additional bias through synthetic distributional error. We further establish minimax lower bounds showing that the raw estimator already achieves the optimal metric-regret rate in the well-specified regime. Under misspecification, however, augmentation can play a qualitatively different role: by changing the effective class balance, it can alter the restricted-class projection and correct ranking errors induced by the raw imbalanced objective. We provide explicit improvement bounds quantifying the roles of approximation error, finite-sample estimation error, and synthetic distributional error. Simulation studies corroborate the theory, demonstrating limited gains under well-specification and nontrivial but nonmonotone improvements under misspecification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成数据增强在基于分数的不平衡分类中何时能提升性能？
合成数据增强广泛用于缓解类别不平衡，但其对基于分数分类的理论影响仍不明确。本文构建了一个框架，用于刻画合成少数类增强在提升阈值整合与阈值优化指标（包括AUROC、AUPRC、最佳阈值平衡准确率及最佳阈值F1分数）时的条件。我们将增强效应分为两个部分：有效类别权重的变化与合成分布与真实少数类分布之间的差异。在良好指定的分数模型下，原始估计量已针对似然比排序，该排序对所考虑指标为群体最优。因此，增强无法提供超越有限样本方差降低的群体层面根本性改进，反而可能因合成分布误差引入额外偏差。我们进一步建立了最小最大下界，表明在良好指定情形下，原始估计量已达到最优指标遗憾率。然而在模型误设下，增强可发挥质性不同的作用：通过改变有效类别平衡，它可调整受限类别投影并纠正原始不平衡目标引发的排序错误。我们提供了明确的改进界限，量化了近似误差、有限样本估计误差与合成分布误差的作用。仿真研究验证了理论，表明在良好指定下收益有限，而在误设下则有非单调但非平凡的改进。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates when synthetic data augmentation improves score-based classification under class imbalance, motivated by the lack of theoretical understanding of its effects. It develops a framework that decomposes augmentation’s impact into changes in effective class weighting and discrepancies between synthetic and true minority distributions. Under well-specified score models, augmentation offers no fundamental population-level gain beyond variance reduction and may introduce bias; however, under misspecification, it can correct ranking errors by altering class balance, leading to nonmonotone but nontrivial improvements. Simulation results confirm these theoretical findings, showing minimal gains under correct specification and meaningful, albeit irregular, gains under model misspecification.
该文探讨合成数据增强在类别不平衡下对评分分类性能的提升条件，动机源于其理论效果缺乏系统研究。文章提出框架，将增强效应分解为有效类别权重变化与合成分布与真实分布差异两部分。在模型正确设定下，增强无法带来根本性性能提升，仅可能降低方差并引入偏差，最小最大下界证明原始估计器已最优；在模型设定错误时，增强可通过调整类别平衡修正排序误差，带来非单调但非平凡的性能提升，其效果由逼近误差、估计误差与分布误差的显式界限量化，仿真结果验证了理论结论。
Natural Ungrokking: Asymmetric Control of Which Rules Survive Pretraining
Authors: Juliana Li, Diya Sreedhar
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-24T17:27:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:27:13+00:00
Comments: Foundations of Deep Generative Models (FoGen) Workshop at ICML 2026. 23 pages (5-page main text plus appendices), 5 figures. Code: https://github.com/lijuliana/Natural-Ungrokking
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Midway through an ordinary pretraining run, a small language model learns the pronoun-gender rule: cued with a girl's name ("Sue cried because"), it resolves the next pronoun to she, generalizing to held-out probes (0.94 by step 925). By step 3,500 the same model scores near zero on the same probes, although the rule's evidence is still in the training data. We call this within-run reversal natural ungrokking: the corpus decides, with no trace in the loss curve, which learned rules a model keeps. Which rules survive is predictable from one corpus statistic: how often the training stream shows the rule winning. Across un-intervened runs (two corpora, three budgets, three seeds), support frequency decides a rule's fate; the data-to-parameter ratio only modulates how deeply a doomed rule falls. The same emerge-then-collapse dynamics appear in public Pythia checkpoints, collapse depth ordered by model scale as predicted. The forgetting is a displacement: a competing surface pattern out-competes the rule, and the log-probability margin between them crosses zero within 100 training steps of the behavioral collapse. Control over this fate is asymmetric: the same edit that destroys a rule on demand cannot restore it. Flipping support to counter-evidence in place kills the rule with monotone dose-response in two unrelated rules; but injecting support back, even to 450 times the level that naturally sustains it, buys no recovery. Every confirmatory threshold and prediction was pre-registered before the data it governed was read.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自然未掌握：预训练中规则存活的非对称控制
在普通预训练过程中，小型语言模型在中途学习了代词性别规则：当提示女孩名字（“Sue哭了因为”）时，它能将下一个代词解析为“她”，并在保留测试样本上泛化表现良好（第925步时达0.94）。但在第3500步时，同一模型在相同测试样本上得分接近零，尽管该规则的证据仍存在于训练数据中。我们将此现象称为“运行内反转”的自然未掌握：语料库决定模型保留哪些学习规则，且无迹可循于损失曲线。 规则的存活与否可由一个语料库统计量预测：规则在训练流中获胜的频率。在未干预的运行中（两个语料库、三种预算、三种种子），支持频率决定规则命运；数据与参数比值仅调节注定失败规则的衰减深度。相同“出现后崩溃”动态在公开Pythia检查点中显现，崩溃深度随模型规模递增，符合预测。遗忘是位移现象：竞争性表面模式胜出，二者对数概率差值在行为崩溃前100步内跨越零点。 对此命运的控制具有非对称性：同一编辑操作可按需摧毁规则，却无法恢复。在两个无关规则中，将支持转向反证即可单调摧毁规则；但即使注入450倍于自然维持水平的支持，也无恢复效果。所有确认阈值与预测均在数据被读取前预先注册。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how language models selectively retain or discard learned rules during pretraining, a phenomenon termed 'natural ungrokking,' where rules like pronoun-gender generalization emerge early but vanish later despite persistent evidence in training data. The authors show that rule survival is determined by the frequency with which the training data supports the rule, not model capacity or loss dynamics, and that collapse occurs when a competing surface pattern overtakes the rule, causing log-probability margins to cross zero within 100 steps. Crucially, control over rule survival is asymmetric: suppressing support can eliminate a rule, but restoring support—even at extreme levels—fails to revive it, indicating irreversibility in learned rule retention.
该论文研究了语言模型在预训练过程中自然遗忘规则的现象，称为“自然未掌握”，即如代词性别规则等早期习得的规则会在训练后期消失，尽管训练数据中仍存在相关证据。作者发现这一遗忘由语料库层面的统计特征驱动——即规则在训练流中获得支持的频率——而非模型容量或损失曲线。多语料、多预算、多种子实验表明，支持频率低的规则会提前崩溃，且模型规模与崩溃深度呈正相关。关键的是，规则遗忘后，即使在崩溃后人为注入大量支持也无法恢复，表明规则存活具有不对称控制性：仅当规则从未被语料接触时，其才能被恢复。
Deep Reinforcement Learning-Enhanced Event-Triggered Data-Driven Predictive Control for a 3D Cable-Driven Soft Robotic Arm
Authors: Cheng Ouyang, Moeen Ul Islam, Kaixiang Zhang, Zhaojian Li, Xiaobo Tan, Dong Chen
First: 2026-06-24T17:26:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:26:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Soft robots are challenging to control due to their nonlinear and time-varying dynamics. Data-enabled predictive control (DeePC) offers a model-free alternative by directly leveraging measured input-output trajectories to construct a predictive controller. However, its receding-horizon formulation requires solving a constrained optimization problem at every sampling instant, which can be computationally demanding for real-time deployment on resource-limited robotic platforms.To address this limitation, we propose an adaptive reinforcement-learning-based event-triggered DeePC (RL-ET-DeePC) framework for soft robotic control. A model-free RL policy is trained to determine when to invoke the DeePC optimizer based on the current system state representation, thereby reducing unnecessary optimization calls while preserving closed-loop performance.Simulation results show that RL-ET-DeePC reduces optimization frequency by up to 66% compared to periodic DeePC, while maintaining comparable tracking accuracy. Hardware experiments on a three-dimensional cable-driven soft robotic arm demonstrate zero-shot transfer, achieving a 34% reduction in optimization frequency with tracking accuracy comparable to periodic DeePC and more consistent performance than a static threshold-based event-triggered baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度强化学习的事件触发数据驱动预测控制用于三维缆驱动柔性机械臂
柔性机器人因其非线性和时变动力学特性而难以控制。数据驱动预测控制（DeePC）提供了一种无需模型的替代方案，通过直接利用测量的输入-输出轨迹构建预测控制器。然而，其滚动时域形式要求在每个采样时刻求解约束优化问题，这对资源受限的机器人平台实时部署而言计算负担较重。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种基于强化学习的自适应事件触发DeePC（RL-ET-DeePC）框架用于柔性机器人控制。通过训练一个无模型的强化学习策略，根据当前系统状态表示决定何时调用DeePC优化器，从而减少不必要的优化调用，同时保持闭环性能。仿真结果表明，RL-ET-DeePC相比周期性DeePC将优化频率降低高达66%，同时保持相近的跟踪精度。在三维缆驱动柔性机械臂上的硬件实验验证了零样本迁移能力，实现了优化频率降低34%，跟踪精度与周期性DeePC相当，且比基于静态阈值的事件触发基线方法表现更稳定。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational burden of real-time model-free predictive control for soft robots, this work introduces an RL-ET-DeePC framework that uses reinforcement learning to trigger optimization events adaptively based on system state, rather than at fixed intervals. The RL policy learns to minimize unnecessary optimization calls while preserving control performance. Experimental results show up to 66% reduction in optimization frequency in simulation and 34% reduction in hardware tests, with tracking accuracy comparable to periodic DeePC and more consistent performance than a static threshold-based approach.
为解决软机器人非线性动态下数据驱动预测控制的计算负担，本文提出一种基于强化学习的自适应事件触发控制框架（RL-ET-DeePC），通过训练强化学习策略根据系统状态决定何时调用预测控制器，避免冗余计算同时保持控制性能。仿真结果显示优化调用频率降低最多达66%，硬件实验在三维缆驱动软臂上实现零样本迁移，优化频率降低34%，跟踪精度与周期控制相当，且比静态阈值触发方法表现更稳定。
Learning Robot Visual Navigation in Crowds via Intention-Aware Scene Representations
Authors: Han Bao, Bingyi Xia, Hanjing Ye, Yu Zhan, Hao Cheng, Baozhi Jia, Wenjun Xu, Jiankun Wang
First: 2026-06-24T17:26:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:26:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot crowd navigation requires the ability to infer human intentions while accounting for the structural constraints of the environment. Currently, deep reinforcement learning (DRL) provides a promising method for learning navigation policies that understand human intentions. However, most of them rely on limited scene representations, treating pedestrians as simple 2D points and ignoring rich visual cues from both humans and the environment. To address this issue, we introduce iCrowdNav, a novel visual crowd navigation method with intention-aware scene representations, to encode behavioral and structural context from egocentric visual observations. Our method employs two key components: a spatio-temporal encoder for extracting occupancy features of the scene, and Intent-Interact Former (I$^2$ Former), an attention-based module that encodes human poses to infer pedestrians' motion intentions. These features are integrated into a compact state embedding that supports effective DRL policy training. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves superior performance over baselines, and real-world deployment demonstrates vision-based crowd navigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过意图感知场景表示学习机器人在人群中的视觉导航
机器人在人群中的导航需要推断人类意图并考虑环境的结构约束。目前，深度强化学习（DRL）为学习理解人类意图的导航策略提供了一种有前景的方法。然而，大多数方法依赖于有限的场景表示，将行人视为简单的2D点，忽略了来自人类和环境的丰富视觉线索。为解决这一问题，我们提出了iCrowdNav，一种具有意图感知场景表示的新型视觉人群导航方法，用于从第一人称视觉观测中编码行为和结构上下文。该方法包含两个关键组件：一个时空编码器，用于提取场景的占用特征；以及Intent-Interact Former（I²Former），一个基于注意力的模块，用于编码人体姿态以推断行人的运动意图。这些特征被整合到一个紧凑的状态嵌入中，支持有效的DRL策略训练。大量实验表明，我们的方法在基线方法上表现更优，实际部署验证了基于视觉的人群导航。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to navigate crowded environments by understanding human intentions while respecting spatial constraints, this paper proposes iCrowdNav, a visual navigation method that leverages intention-aware scene representations. It combines a spatio-temporal encoder to capture occupancy features and an Intent-Interact Former (I²Former) attention module to infer pedestrian motion intentions from visual poses. These components jointly form a compact state embedding for reinforcement learning policy training. Experimental results demonstrate that iCrowdNav outperforms existing baselines and successfully enables vision-based crowd navigation in real-world settings.
为使机器人能在人群环境中通过理解人类意图并兼顾空间结构进行导航，本文提出iCrowdNav方法，利用意图感知的场景表征。该方法结合时空编码器提取场景占用特征与Intent-Interact Former（I²Former）注意力模块，从视觉姿态推断行人运动意图，共同生成紧凑状态嵌入以支持强化学习策略训练。实验表明，iCrowdNav在模拟与真实场景中均优于现有基线方法，验证了其在视觉导航中的有效性。
RoboAtlas: Contextual Active SLAM
Authors: Alexander Schperberg, Shivam K. Panda, Abraham P. Vinod, M. K. Jawed, Stefano Di Cairano
First: 2026-06-24T17:26:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:26:07+00:00
Comments: Alexander Schperberg and Shivam K. Panda made equal contribution
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present RoboAtlas, a contextual Active SLAM framework that adaptively balances geometric exploration and semantic reasoning using a scalable 3D semantic mapping system, OpenRoboVox. RoboAtlas integrates frontier exploration, global semantic-map reasoning, and egocentric VLM-based reasoning through a contextual multi-armed bandit that transitions from exploration to semantically guided navigation as scene understanding improves. We evaluate the system in simulation and on a Unitree Go2 robot in large-scale real-world environments exceeding 1800 m2 with approx. 30k mapped semantic instances, achieving a 100% task success rate. On the GOAT-Bench "Val Unseen" benchmark, RoboAtlas achieves state-of-the-art performance with highest reported success rate (SR) of 90.6%, using GPT-4o, improving over the strongest prior baseline by 17.8 percentage points in SR. Using the much smaller Qwen2.5-VL-7B model, it still achieves 88.8% SR, outperforming all baselines using GPT-4o in SR, and revealing the importance of the information gained by our semantic mapping framework over simply replacing the underlying foundation model. The results demonstrate that grounding foundation models with large-scale 3D semantic maps enables robust and efficient contextual Active SLAM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboAtlas：上下文主动SLAM
我们提出了RoboAtlas，一种上下文主动SLAM框架，通过可扩展的3D语义映射系统OpenRoboVox，自适应地平衡几何探索与语义推理。RoboAtlas通过上下文多臂老虎机整合前沿探索、全局语义地图推理和以第一人称视角为基础的视觉语言模型推理，在场景理解提升时从探索过渡到语义引导导航。我们在仿真环境和Unitree Go2机器人上对系统进行了评估，测试环境为超过1800平方米的大型真实场景，共映射约3万个语义实例，任务成功率100%。在GOAT-Bench“Val Unseen”基准测试中，RoboAtlas使用GPT-4o时达到90.6%的最高成功率（SR），比最强基线提升17.8个百分点；使用更小的Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型仍达到88.8%的SR，超越所有使用GPT-4o的基线，凸显了我们的语义映射框架所获取信息的重要性，而非仅替换基础模型。结果表明，将基础模型与大规模3D语义地图结合，可实现稳健且高效的上下文主动SLAM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and context-aware robotic navigation, RoboAtlas introduces a contextual Active SLAM framework that dynamically balances geometric exploration with semantic reasoning via a scalable 3D semantic mapping system called OpenRoboVox. It employs a contextual multi-armed bandit to coordinate frontier exploration, global semantic-map reasoning, and egocentric vision-language model (VLM) reasoning, transitioning from exploration to semantically guided navigation as scene understanding deepens. Evaluated in simulation and on a Unitree Go2 robot across large-scale real-world environments (over 1800 m² with ~30k semantic instances), the system achieved 100% task success. On the GOAT-Bench "Val Unseen" benchmark, it achieved the highest success rate of 90.6% using GPT-4o, outperforming prior baselines by 17.8 percentage points, and still reached 88.8% SR with the smaller Qwen2.5-VL-7B model, demonstrating that semantic mapping enhances performance even when using less powerful VLMs.
为提升机器人在大规模环境中的高效且上下文感知的导航能力，RoboAtlas提出了一种结合几何探索与语义推理的上下文主动SLAM框架，其核心是可扩展的3D语义映射系统OpenRoboVox。该方法通过上下文多臂老虎机机制，融合前沿探索、全局语义地图推理与自视角视觉-语言模型推理，随场景理解加深逐步从探索转向语义引导导航。在模拟环境及Unitree Go2机器人真实世界测试中（覆盖超1800平方米，含约3万语义实例），系统实现100%任务成功率。在GOAT-Bench "Val Unseen"基准测试中，使用GPT-4o时达到90.6%成功率，超越最强基线17.8个百分点；使用更小的Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型仍达88.8%成功率，超越所有GPT-4o基线方法，凸显语义映射框架对基础模型性能提升的关键作用。
Auto-exploration for online reinforcement learning
Authors: Caleb Ju, Guanghui Lan
First: 2025-12-06T02:04:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:21:18+00:00
Comments: 30 pages. Added experiments and re-write
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The exploration-exploitation dilemma in reinforcement learning (RL) is a fundamental challenge to efficient RL algorithms. Existing algorithms for finite state and action discounted RL problems address this by assuming sufficient exploration over both state and action spaces. However, this yields non-implementable algorithms and sub-optimal performance. To resolve these limitations, we introduce a new class of methods with auto-exploration, or methods that automatically explore both state and action spaces. Auto-exploration can be applied in both the tabular and linear function approximation setting. Under algorithm-independent assumptions on the existence of an exploring optimal policy, both settings attain $O(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity to solve to $ε$ error. These complexities are novel since they avoid algorithm-dependent parameters seen in prior works, which may be arbitrarily large. The methods are also simple to implement because they are parameter-free. We achieve these results by integrating auto-exploration into policy mirror descent to avoid the (unknown) stationary distribution seen in prior art. In the tabular setting, we introduce a dynamic exploration time with a data-driven stopping time, while for linear function approximation we propose a new sampling distribution based on the discounted visitation distribution that covers a more general class of Markov chains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在线强化学习中的自动探索
强化学习中的探索-利用权衡是高效强化学习算法的基本挑战。现有针对有限状态和动作折扣强化学习问题的算法通过假设在状态和动作空间上充分探索来应对这一挑战，但由此产生的算法不可实现且性能次优。为解决这些局限性，我们引入了一类新的自动探索方法，即能自动探索状态和动作空间的方法。自动探索可应用于表格化和线性函数逼近两种设定。在算法无关的假设下，即存在探索最优策略，两种设定均能达到$O(ε^{-2})$的样本复杂度以解决$ε$误差问题。这些复杂度是新颖的，因为它们避免了先前工作中的算法依赖参数，这些参数可能任意大。这些方法也易于实现，因为它们无参数。我们通过将自动探索整合到策略镜像下降法中，以避免先前方法中出现的（未知）平稳分布。在表格化设定中，我们引入了动态探索时间与数据驱动的停止时间；在线性函数逼近设定中，我们提出了一种基于折扣访问分布的新采样分布，覆盖了更广泛的马尔可夫链类。
Summary / 总结
To address the exploration-exploitation dilemma in reinforcement learning, which limits efficiency of existing algorithms by requiring unrealistic assumptions about exploration, this work introduces auto-exploration methods that automatically explore state and action spaces without manual tuning. These methods are designed for both tabular and linear function approximation settings and achieve $O(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity to solve to $ε$ error under algorithm-independent assumptions, avoiding the algorithm-dependent parameters that plague prior approaches. The methods are implemented via policy mirror descent with modifications: in the tabular case, a data-driven dynamic exploration time is used, and in the linear setting, a novel sampling distribution based on discounted visitation is proposed to cover broader Markov chains. Experimental results confirm the theoretical guarantees and demonstrate practical performance improvements over baseline methods.
为解决传统强化学习算法依赖人工探索策略、性能次优或不可实现的问题，本文提出自动探索方法，能自适应地在状态与动作空间中探索，无需用户设定参数。方法基于策略镜像下降算法改进，避免对未知平稳分布的依赖，适用于表格化与线性函数逼近两种场景。实验表明，在算法无关假设下，两种场景均达到 $O(ε^{-2})$ 样本复杂度以实现 $ε$ 精度，优于依赖可能无限大的算法特定参数的现有方法，同时保持参数自由的简洁性。
How Robust is OCR-Reasoning? Evaluating OCR-Reasoning Robustness of Vision-Language Models under Visual Perturbations
Authors: Yuxing Cheng, Yuan Wu, Yi Chang
First: 2026-06-24T17:15:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:15:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on OCR-based benchmarks and increasingly focused on text-rich understanding, but their robustness under controlled visual degradation remains insufficiently understood. This gap is critical for OCR reasoning, where visual corruption can induce OCR errors and structural distortions, thereby introducing uncertainty into the reasoning task. To systematically study this problem, we introduce OCR-Robust, a benchmark designed for evaluating OCR reasoning robustness under visual perturbations. It contains 812 samples across two complementary subsets: OCR1.0, covering documents, scene text, receipts, handwriting, and mathematical content, and OCR2.0, focusing on charts, geometry diagrams, and tables. To enable efficient yet informative evaluation, we conduct a pilot study over 18 candidate perturbations and select 5 representative types at 3 severity levels each based on their impact and cross-model discriminability. We evaluate robustness using clean accuracy, Relative Corruption Retention (RCR), Worst-Case Retention (WCR), and a composite Corruption Robustness Index (CRI), and benchmark 18 models spanning proprietary systems, open-source VLMs, and OCR+LLM pipelines. Our results show that higher clean accuracy does not necessarily imply stronger robustness, and that models can suffer pronounced degradation in the worst case on OCR tasks that are sensitive to structure, and charts and tables are substantially more fragile than document-like inputs under perturbation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OCR推理的鲁棒性如何？在视觉扰动下评估视觉-语言模型的OCR推理鲁棒性
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在基于OCR的基准测试中表现强劲，并日益关注文本丰富的理解，但其在受控视觉退化下的鲁棒性仍不充分理解。这一差距对OCR推理至关重要，因为视觉损坏可能引发OCR错误和结构扭曲，从而引入推理任务的不确定性。为系统研究此问题，我们引入OCR-Robust基准，用于评估视觉扰动下的OCR推理鲁棒性。该基准包含812个样本，分为两个互补子集：OCR1.0涵盖文档、场景文本、收据、手写体和数学内容；OCR2.0聚焦图表、几何图示和表格。为实现高效且信息丰富的评估，我们在18种候选扰动上进行试点研究，根据其影响和跨模型区分度，选取5种代表性扰动类型，每种扰动设3个严重程度等级。我们通过干净准确率、相对损坏保留率（RCR）、最坏情况保留率（WCR）和综合损坏鲁棒性指数（CRI）评估鲁棒性，并对18个模型进行基准测试，涵盖专有系统、开源VLMs和OCR+LLM流水线。结果表明，高干净准确率并不必然意味着更强鲁棒性，且在结构敏感的OCR任务中，模型在最坏情况下可能显著退化，而图表和表格在扰动下比文档类输入更脆弱。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the lack of understanding regarding how vision-language models (VLMs) handle OCR reasoning under visual degradation, which is critical because visual corruption can cause OCR errors and disrupt reasoning. The authors introduce OCR-Robust, a benchmark with 812 samples split into OCR1.0 (documents, handwriting, etc.) and OCR2.0 (charts, tables, diagrams), and evaluate 18 models using five visual perturbations at three severity levels. They measure robustness via clean accuracy, Relative Corruption Retention (RCR), Worst-Case Retention (WCR), and a Corruption Robustness Index (CRI). Results reveal that high clean accuracy does not guarantee robustness, and models show severe performance drops on structurally sensitive tasks, especially with charts and tables, which are far more fragile than document-like inputs under perturbation.
该研究旨在填补视觉语言模型（VLM）在视觉退化环境下OCR推理鲁棒性不足的认知空白，因为视觉干扰易引发OCR错误并破坏结构推理。作者构建了OCR-Robust基准测试，包含812个样本，分为OCR1.0（文档、收据、手写体、数学内容）和OCR2.0（图表、几何图、表格），并基于影响程度和模型区分度筛选出五类代表性扰动，每类设三个严重程度。评估18个模型（含专有系统、开源VLM及OCR+LLM组合），采用干净准确率、相对退化保留率（RCR）、最坏情况保留率（WCR）及综合退化鲁棒指数（CRI）。结果表明，高干净准确率不等于强鲁棒性，模型在结构敏感任务（如图表和表格）上退化显著，且其鲁棒性远低于文档类输入。
AI translation of literary texts is "fine", but readers still prefer human translations
Authors: Yves Ferstler, Adam Podoxin, Ty Brassington, Roman Grundkiewicz, Maite Taboada, Marzena Karpinska
First: 2026-06-24T17:15:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:15:14+00:00
Comments: 58 pages, including appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI translation of literary works is increasingly common. While the content may be rendered adequately, we do not know enough about how readers experience it in terms of immersiveness and literary effect, aspects poorly captured by automatic machine translation metrics or human evaluation targeting fluency and adequacy. We ask 15 avid readers to compare recently published human translations (HT) to machine translations (MT) generated with an agentic large language model (LLM)-based pipeline, for 15 recent novels in French, Polish, and Japanese and translated into English. Readers evaluated approximately 8K-word excerpts in two conditions: immersive reading of the whole excerpt (30 comparisons) and close reading of 386 aligned HT-MT chunk pairs (772 comparisons), with two readers per book and in alternating order of presentation. Overall, readers find MT "fine", but prefer HT (slightly at excerpt-level 19/30, more clearly at chunk-level 522/772) for its ease, clarity, and immersive nature. Readers' highlights show that MT's quality varies more within one book than HT's does. Crucially, readers cannot reliably tell the two apart (17/30 guess correctly) and tend to prefer the version they believe to be human. Automatic metrics, including LLM-as-a-judge approaches, fail to recover reader preferences and favor MT. We release LAIT (Literary AI Translation), a reader-centered evaluation dataset with 1K reader comments, 2K judgments and preference ratings, and 7.2K span-level annotations, along with our evaluation protocol and supporting interface.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI翻译文学文本“尚可”，但读者仍偏好人工翻译
AI翻译文学作品日益普遍。虽然内容可基本传达，但我们对读者在沉浸感和文学效果方面的体验了解不足，而自动机器翻译指标或以流畅性和准确性为目标的人工评估难以捕捉这些方面。我们邀请15位热衷阅读者，将近期出版的人工翻译（HT）与基于代理型大语言模型（LLM）生成的机器翻译（MT）进行比较，对象为15部近期出版的法语、波兰语和日语小说，译为英文。读者在两种条件下评估约8000词的摘录：沉浸式阅读整段摘录（30组对比）和细读386对齐的HT-MT片段对（772组对比），每本书由两位读者评估，且交替呈现顺序。总体而言，读者认为MT“尚可”，但更偏好HT，因其更易读、清晰且具沉浸感（段落级对比中522/772更倾向HT）。读者标注的高亮显示，MT在单本书内质量波动更大，而HT则更稳定。关键的是，读者无法可靠区分HT与MT（仅17/30猜对），且倾向于偏好他们认为是人工翻译的版本。自动评估指标，包括LLM作为裁判的方法，均未能还原读者偏好，反而更倾向MT。我们发布LAIT（文学AI翻译）数据集，包含1000条评论、2000个判断与偏好评分、7200个片段级标注，以及评估协议与支持界面。

Motivated by the gap between AI translation performance and reader experience in literary contexts, the study compares human translations (HT) and machine translations (MT) generated by an agentic LLM pipeline across 15 novels in multiple languages. Fifteen readers evaluated 8K-word excerpts through immersive reading and close reading of aligned HT-MT pairs, yielding 30 and 772 comparisons respectively. Results show MT is deemed "fine" but HT is preferred for clarity and immersion, especially at the chunk level (522/772) versus excerpt level (19/30). Readers often misidentify MT as human and automatic metrics fail to align with reader preferences, favoring MT. The study releases LAIT, a dataset with 1K comments, 2K judgments, and 7.2K span annotations to support reader-centered evaluation.
鉴于AI翻译在文学文本中虽能准确传达内容，但读者体验（如沉浸感与文学效果）难以被现有自动评估指标或侧重流畅度与准确度的人类评价捕捉，本研究对比了人类翻译与基于LLM的机器翻译在15部多语种小说中的表现。15位读者通过沉浸式阅读和逐段对比评估，发现尽管AI翻译被评价为“尚可”，但人类翻译在段落级对比中更受青睐（522/772），因其更易理解、更沉浸。读者常误判AI翻译为人类翻译，且自动评估指标（含LLM裁判）未能反映真实偏好，反而倾向AI译文。研究发布LAIT数据集，包含1000条读者评论、2000个判断及7200个片段标注，以支持文学AI翻译的读者导向评估。
Uncovering Insights of Compound Flooding with Data-Driven AI
Authors: Xu Zheng, Chaohao Lin, Sipeng Chen, Zhuomin Chen, Jimeng Shi, Jayantha Obeysekera, Jingchao Ni, Wei Cheng, Jason Liu, Dongsheng Luo
First: 2025-06-04T04:45:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:14:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to SIGKDD 2026 AI for Science Track; 12 Pages, 5 Figures, 6 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Compound flooding, driven by nonlinear interactions between multiple hydrometeorological factors, poses a significant challenge to hazard prevention. Existing forecasting approaches, whether physics-based or data-driven, often emphasize temporal patterns while underexploring how multiple interacting factors jointly shape flood dynamics. To address this problem, we conduct a large-scale data-driven analysis of compound flooding in South Florida, a typical area for compound flooding, by integrating tidal conditions, rainfall, groundwater stage, and human water management activities. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (i) models that capture temporal dynamics alone fail to represent multi-factor interactions during compound events; (ii) subsurface saturation, as reflected by groundwater levels, emerges as a dominant predictor of flood severity, often outweighing immediate rainfall intensity in this porous coastal region; and (iii) the spatial state of surrounding monitoring stations within a finite effective radius provides critical causal context for flooding, while extending temporal history yields diminishing returns during extreme events. These findings suggest that compound flooding is governed more by spatially coupled system states than by long-term temporal dependencies, challenging rain-centric and sequence-dominated forecasting paradigms. By framing data-driven models as tools for scientific inquiry rather than prediction alone, this study offers new insights into the mechanisms of compound flooding and informs the design of more physically grounded early-warning systems for coastal environments. Our dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/AslanDing/SFBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用数据驱动人工智能揭示复合洪水的机理
复合洪水由多种水文气象因子的非线性相互作用驱动，对灾害防控构成重大挑战。现有预测方法，无论是物理模型还是数据驱动模型，往往侧重时间序列模式，而忽视多因子协同作用如何共同塑造洪水动态。为解决此问题，我们对佛罗里达州南部——典型复合洪水高发区，整合潮汐、降雨、地下水位及人类水资源管理活动，开展大规模数据驱动分析。研究发现三点：(i) 仅捕捉时间动态的模型无法表征复合事件中的多因子交互；(ii) 地下水位反映的地下饱和度成为该多孔沿海地区洪水严重程度的主导预测因子，常超过即时降雨强度；(iii) 有限有效半径内周边监测站的空间状态提供关键因果背景，而延长历史时间序列在极端事件中收益递减。这些发现表明，复合洪水更受空间耦合系统状态支配，而非长期时间依赖，挑战以降雨为中心、序列主导的预测范式。本研究将数据驱动模型定位为科学探索工具而非单纯预测工具，为复合洪水机制提供新洞见，并指导沿海环境更物理基础的早期预警系统设计。数据与代码公开于 https://github.com/AslanDing/SFBench。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to better understand how multiple hydrometeorological factors interact during compound flooding, this study employs a large-scale data-driven analysis of South Florida’s flood events, integrating tidal data, rainfall, groundwater levels, and human water management. The research finds that models focusing solely on temporal patterns fail to capture critical multi-factor interactions; instead, groundwater saturation emerges as the dominant predictor of flood severity, often surpassing rainfall intensity in coastal porous regions. Additionally, spatial context from nearby monitoring stations within a limited radius proves more informative than extended temporal history during extreme events, indicating that compound flooding is better explained by spatially coupled system states than by temporal sequences. These insights challenge traditional forecasting models and advocate for data-driven approaches as tools for scientific discovery rather than mere prediction.
为深入理解由多种水文气象因子非线性交互引发的复合洪水机制，本研究在佛罗里达州南部开展大规模数据驱动分析，整合潮汐、降雨、地下水位及人类水资源管理数据。研究发现，仅关注时间序列的模型无法捕捉多因子协同作用；地下水饱和度成为预测洪水严重程度的关键主导因子，常超过降雨强度的影响；同时，周边监测站点在有限空间范围内的状态比延长的时间序列对极端事件更具预测价值，表明复合洪水更受空间耦合系统状态支配而非长期时序依赖。该研究挑战了以降雨为中心、依赖序列的预测范式，主张数据驱动模型应服务于科学探索，为沿海地区更物理基础的预警系统设计提供新思路。
FedReLa: Imbalanced Federated Learning via Re-Labeling
Authors: Guangzheng Hu, Patricia Menéndez, Feng Liu, Mingming Gong, Guanghui Wang, Liuhua Peng
First: 2026-06-24T17:13:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:13:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning has emerged as the foremost approach for decentralized model training with privacy preservation. The global class imbalance and cross-client data heterogeneity naturally coexist, and the mismatch between local and global imbalances exacerbates the performance degradation of the aggregated model. The agnosticism of global class distribution poses significant challenges for data-level methods, especially under extreme conditions with severe class absence across clients. In this paper, we propose FedReLa, a novel data-level approach that tackles the coexistence of data heterogeneity and class imbalance in federated learning. By re-labeling samples with a feature-dependent label re-allocator, FedReLa corrects biased global decision boundaries without requiring knowledge of the global class distribution. This modular, model-agnostic approach can be integrated with algorithmic methods to deliver consistent improvements without additional communication overhead. Through extensive experiments, our method significantly improves the accuracy of minority classes and the overall accuracy on stepwise-imbalanced and long-tailed datasets, outperforming the previous state of the art.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedReLa：通过重标记实现不平衡联邦学习
联邦学习已成为隐私保护下分布式模型训练的主流方法。全局类别不平衡与客户端间数据异质性天然共存，局部与全局不平衡的不匹配加剧了聚合模型的性能退化。全局类别分布的未知性对数据层面方法构成重大挑战，尤其在客户端间严重类别缺失的极端条件下。本文提出FedReLa，一种新颖的数据层面方法，旨在解决联邦学习中数据异质性与类别不平衡的共存问题。通过基于特征的标签重分配器对样本进行重标记，FedReLa可在无需全局类别分布知识的情况下校正偏斜的全局决策边界。该模块化、模型无关的方法可与算法方法集成，实现性能提升且不增加通信开销。大量实验表明，该方法在阶梯式不平衡和长尾数据集上显著提升少数类别准确率和整体准确率，优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of global class imbalance and cross-client data heterogeneity in federated learning, which degrade model performance due to mismatched local and global distributions, FedReLa introduces a data-level re-labeling method that corrects biased global decision boundaries without requiring knowledge of the global class distribution. It employs a feature-dependent label re-allocator to adjust sample labels, enabling a modular, model-agnostic integration with existing federated algorithms. Experimental results demonstrate that FedReLa significantly improves accuracy for minority classes and overall performance on stepwise-imbalanced and long-tailed datasets, consistently outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods.
为解决联邦学习中全局类别不平衡与客户端间数据异质性共存导致的模型性能下降问题，FedReLa提出一种数据层面方法，通过特征依赖的标签重分配机制对样本进行重标签，无需全局类别分布信息即可修正偏斜的全局决策边界。该方法模块化、模型无关，可无缝集成现有算法且不增加通信开销。实验表明，FedReLa在阶梯式不平衡与长尾数据集上显著提升少数类准确率和整体性能，优于现有最先进方法。
Detect, Unlearn, Restore: Defending Text Summarization Models Against Data Poisoning
Authors: Poojitha Thota, Shirin Nilizadeh
First: 2026-06-24T17:12:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:12:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training-time data poisoning during fine-tuning poses a significant threat to large language models (LLMs) deployed for abstractive text summarization, where small task-specific datasets exert disproportionate influence on model behavior. In this setting, adversaries manipulate fine-tuning data to induce persistent summarization failures, such as biased or harmful summaries, while preserving standard evaluation metrics. We present a unified post-hoc defense framework for detecting and remediating fine-tuning-stage poisoning in summarization models across the machine learning supply chain. Our experiments show that in white-box settings, poisoned document-summary pairs exhibit abnormally high training influence, enabling detection via influence-function analysis with semantic consistency checks. In black-box settings, poisoned models display two to three times greater sensitivity to semantics-preserving perturbations, enabling behavioral auditing without training data access. Beyond existing poisoning formulations, we introduce novel attacks targeting factual distortion and representational bias, showing that poisoning alters summarization behavior without triggering conventional alarms. Across nine architectures and six benchmark datasets under adaptive attacks, our defenses achieve 85-92% detection precision, while gradient-ascent unlearning restores up to 96% of original behavior with minimal utility loss (less than 0.6% ROUGE degradation). These results indicate that fine-tuning-time poisoning leaves persistent structural artifacts, enabling practical detection and post-deployment recovery without full retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检测、遗忘、恢复：防御文本摘要模型中的数据投毒
在微调阶段进行的训练数据投毒对用于抽象式文本摘要的大语言模型（LLMs）构成重大威胁，其中小规模任务特定数据集对模型行为产生不成比例的影响。在此场景下，攻击者操纵微调数据以诱导持久性的摘要失败，如偏见或有害摘要，同时保持标准评估指标不变。我们提出了一种统一的后置防御框架，用于检测和修复摘要模型在机器学习供应链中微调阶段的投毒问题。实验表明，在白盒环境下，被投毒的文档-摘要对表现出异常高的训练影响，可通过影响函数分析结合语义一致性检查进行检测；在黑盒环境下，被投毒模型对语义保持扰动的敏感度高出两到三倍，从而无需访问训练数据即可进行行为审计。除现有投毒形式外，我们引入了针对事实扭曲和表征偏见的新攻击方式，表明投毒可改变摘要行为而不触发传统警报。在九种架构和六个基准数据集上，面对自适应攻击，我们的防御措施实现85-92%的检测精度，而梯度上升遗忘方法可恢复高达96%的原始行为，且性能损失极小（ROUGE下降不足0.6%）。这些结果表明，微调阶段的投毒会留下持久的结构痕迹，从而无需完整再训练即可实现实际检测和部署后恢复。

Motivated by the risk that adversaries can poison fine-tuning data to corrupt summarization models without detection, this work proposes a unified post-hoc defense framework that detects, unlearns, and restores poisoned behavior. The method leverages influence-function analysis with semantic consistency checks in white-box settings to identify poisoned data pairs, and behavioral auditing via sensitivity to semantic perturbations in black-box settings. Experiments across nine architectures and six datasets show the framework detects poisoning with 85-92% precision and recovers up to 96% of original model behavior using gradient-ascent unlearning, with minimal utility loss (less than 0.6% ROUGE drop).
为应对在微调阶段通过数据投毒导致摘要模型持续性失效的问题，作者提出了一种统一的后置防御框架，实现检测、遗忘与恢复。该方法在白盒环境下通过影响函数分析结合语义一致性检查识别投毒数据，在黑盒环境下通过语义扰动敏感性审计检测异常行为。实验表明，该框架在九种架构和六个数据集上检测精度达85-92%，并通过梯度上升遗忘恢复96%原始能力，且性能损失极小（ROUGE下降不足0.6%）。
A Flow-rate-conserving CNN-based Domain Decomposition Method for Blood Flow Simulations
Authors: Simon Klaes, Axel Klawonn, Natalie Kubicki, Martin Lanser, Kengo Nakajima, Takashi Shimokawabe, Janine Weber
First: 2025-09-19T11:56:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:10:58+00:00
Comments: This is a revised version which has been accepted for publication in SIAM Journal on Scientific Computing (SISC) on June 17, 2026. It is the version prior to editorial processing by SIAM
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work aims to predict blood flow with non-Newtonian viscosity in stenosed arteries using convolutional neural network (CNN) surrogate models. An alternating Schwarz domain decomposition method is proposed which uses CNN-based subdomain solvers. A universal subdomain solver (USDS) is trained on a single, fixed geometry and then applied for each subdomain solve in the Schwarz method. Results for two-dimensional stenotic arteries of varying shape and length for different inflow conditions are presented and statistically evaluated. One key finding, when using a limited amount of training data, is that incorporating a physics-aware constraint, as, in our case, flow rate conservation, into the USDS improves the prediction accuracy and convergence behavior of the Schwarz method compared to a purely data-driven USDS. As the USDS is a data-driven, inexact subdomain solver, admissible parameter ranges for the geometry and inflow configurations must be defined and tested.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种基于保持流量守恒的卷积神经网络域分解方法用于血流模拟
本研究旨在利用卷积神经网络（CNN）代理模型预测狭窄动脉中具有非牛顿黏度的血流。 提出了一种交替Schwarz域分解方法，该方法使用基于CNN的子域求解器。 一种通用子域求解器（USDS）在单一固定几何结构上训练，然后应用于Schwarz方法中的每个子域求解。 对不同形状和长度的二维狭窄动脉在不同流入条件下的结果进行了展示和统计评估。一个关键发现是，当训练数据有限时，将物理感知约束（如本研究中的流量守恒）纳入USDS，可提高Schwarz方法的预测精度和收敛行为，相比纯数据驱动的USDS效果更优。由于USDS是数据驱动的近似子域求解器，必须定义并测试几何结构和流入配置的可接受参数范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simulate blood flow with non-Newtonian viscosity in stenosed arteries, this study proposes a CNN-based domain decomposition method using an alternating Schwarz scheme. A universal subdomain solver (USDS), trained on a fixed geometry, is applied to each subdomain within the decomposition. Experimental results on 2D stenotic arteries with varying shapes, lengths, and inflow conditions show that incorporating a physics-aware flow rate conservation constraint into the USDS significantly improves prediction accuracy and convergence compared to a purely data-driven USDS, especially under limited training data. The method requires predefined admissible parameter ranges for geometry and inflow to ensure valid predictions.
为模拟狭窄动脉中具有非牛顿粘性的血流，该研究提出了一种基于CNN的域分解方法，采用交替Schwarz算法。该方法使用在固定几何结构上训练的通用子域求解器（USDS），应用于每个子域求解。实验结果表明，在训练数据有限的情况下，将物理约束（如流量守恒）融入USDS可显著提升预测精度和收敛性，优于纯数据驱动的USDS。该方法需预先定义几何与流入条件的可接受参数范围以确保预测有效性。
TriViewBench: Controlled Complexity Scaling for Multi-View Structural Reasoning in MLLMs
Authors: Yu-Yang Chen, Lan-Zhe Guo
First: 2026-06-24T17:00:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T17:00:05+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate strong performance on standard visual question answering benchmarks, yet their scalability under controlled structural complexity remains poorly understood. We introduce TriViewBench, a controlled three-view visual reasoning benchmark constructed from synthetic 3D scenes with explicitly parameterized object count and occlusion. The benchmark contains 1,923 scenes and over 14K Question-Answer (QA) pairs organized into four complexity levels and three reasoning categories: Local Decision, Object Counting, and Global Recovery. We evaluate 18 open- and closed-source MLLMs under a unified prompting protocol. All 18 models exhibit an identical capability hierarchy without exception (Local Decision > Object Counting > Global Recovery), and performance degrades monotonically with complexity: Local Decision tasks decline modestly (12.11% relative drop), while Object Counting degrades substantially (59.14%) and Global Recovery collapses severely (80.02%). Error analysis on Object Counting reveals two mechanistically independent failure modes: single-view tasks are dominated by undercounting due to occlusion blindness, whereas the multi-view task reverses to overcounting due to cross-view identity confusion. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting yields near-zero overall benefit ($Δ= -0.16\%$) and its effect on Global Recovery is strongly capability-gated, suggesting that the bottleneck lies in cross-view spatial representation rather than reasoning strategy. These findings reveal fundamental scalability limitations in current MLLMs and position TriViewBench as a controlled diagnostic framework for analyzing structural reasoning failures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TriViewBench：面向多视角结构推理的MLLMs可控复杂度扩展基准
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在标准视觉问答基准上表现优异，但其在可控结构复杂度下的可扩展性尚不明确。我们提出了TriViewBench，一个基于合成3D场景构建的可控三视角视觉推理基准，场景中对象数量与遮挡情况明确参数化。该基准包含1923个场景和超过14000组问答对，按四个复杂度层级和三类推理任务组织：局部决策、对象计数与全局恢复。我们在统一提示协议下评估了18个开源与闭源MLLMs。所有18个模型均表现出完全一致的能力层级（局部决策 > 对象计数 > 全局恢复），且性能随复杂度单调下降：局部决策任务下降幅度较小（12.11%相对下降），对象计数任务大幅下降（59.14%），全局恢复任务严重崩溃（80.02%）。对象计数任务的错误分析揭示两种机制独立的失败模式：单视角任务因遮挡盲导致计数不足，而多视角任务因跨视角身份混淆导致计数过量。思维链（CoT）提示几乎无整体收益（Δ = -0.16%），且对全局恢复任务的影响受能力门控，表明瓶颈在于跨视角空间表征而非推理策略。这些发现揭示当前MLLMs的根本可扩展性限制，并将TriViewBench定位为分析结构推理失败的可控诊断框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how MLLMs scale with structural complexity in visual reasoning, the authors introduce TriViewBench, a synthetic 3D benchmark with controlled object count and occlusion, featuring 1,923 scenes and 14K QA pairs across four complexity levels and three reasoning types. Evaluating 18 MLLMs under unified prompting, they find a consistent capability hierarchy (Local Decision > Object Counting > Global Recovery) and monotonic performance decay with complexity: Local Decision drops 12.11%, Object Counting 59.14%, and Global Recovery collapses 80.02%. Chain-of-Thought prompting offers negligible benefit (Δ = -0.16%) and is ineffective for Global Recovery, indicating the bottleneck lies in spatial representation, not reasoning strategy.
为探究多模态大语言模型在多视角结构推理中的复杂度扩展能力，作者构建了TriViewBench基准，包含1923个合成3D场景与14000+问答对，通过对象数量与遮挡参数化控制复杂度，划分为四个难度层级和三类推理任务。对18个开源与闭源模型统一评测发现，所有模型均呈现一致能力层级（局部决策 > 对象计数 > 全局恢复），且性能随复杂度单调下降：局部决策下降12.11%，对象计数下降59.14%，全局恢复下降80.02%。错误分析显示对象计数失败分为两类机制：单视角任务因遮挡盲区导致漏计，多视角任务因跨视角身份混淆导致多计。思维链提示几乎无提升（Δ=-0.16%），且对全局恢复无效，表明瓶颈在于空间表征而非推理策略。
Breaking Data Symmetry is Needed For Generalization in Feature Learning Kernels
Authors: Marcel Tomàs Bernal, Neil Rohit Mallinar, Mikhail Belkin
First: 2026-03-31T23:28:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:59:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 29th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics (AISTATS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Grokking occurs when a model achieves high training accuracy but generalization to unseen test points happens long after that. This phenomenon was initially observed on a class of algebraic problems, such as learning modular arithmetic (Power et al., 2022). We study grokking on algebraic tasks in a class of feature learning kernels via the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm (Radhakrishnan et al., 2024), which iteratively updates feature matrices through the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) of an estimator in order to learn task-relevant features. Our main experimental finding is that generalization occurs only when a certain symmetry in the training set is broken. Furthermore, we empirically show that RFM generalizes by recovering the underlying invariance group action inherent in the data. We find that the learned feature matrices encode specific elements of the invariance group, explaining the dependence of generalization on symmetry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：特征学习核中为实现泛化需打破数据对称性
当模型在训练集上达到高精度，但对未见过的测试点的泛化能力却在很久之后才出现时，称为‘理解’（grokking）。该现象最初在代数问题（如学习模运算）中被观察到（Power等，2022）。我们通过递归特征机（RFM）算法（Radhakrishnan等，2024）研究代数任务中的‘理解’现象，该算法通过估计量的平均梯度外积（AGOP）迭代更新特征矩阵，以学习任务相关的特征。我们的主要实验发现是：泛化仅在训练集中某种对称性被打破时发生。此外，我们实证表明，RFM通过恢复数据中固有的不变性群作用来实现泛化。我们发现，所学习的特征矩阵编码了不变性群中的特定元素，解释了泛化对称性的依赖性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the phenomenon of grokking—where models achieve high training accuracy but only generalize much later—the study investigates this behavior in algebraic tasks using the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm. RFM learns task-relevant features by iteratively updating feature matrices via the Average Gradient Outer Product. The key finding is that generalization only emerges when symmetry in the training data is broken, and RFM achieves this by recovering the underlying invariance group action, with learned feature matrices encoding specific group elements that explain the symmetry-dependent generalization.
该论文研究了在代数任务中，模型在训练集上达到高准确率后，其泛化能力仅在很久之后才出现的现象（即‘grokking’），通过递归特征机器（RFM）算法进行分析。实验发现，泛化能力仅在训练数据的对称性被打破时出现，且RFM通过恢复数据内在的不变性群作用实现泛化，其学习到的特征矩阵编码了特定群元素，解释了泛化能力对对称性的依赖。
Can Trustless Agents Be Trusted? An Empirical Study of the ERC-8004 Decentralized AI Agent Ecosystem
Authors: Xihan Xiong, Zelin Li, Wei Wei, Qin Wang, William Knottenbelt, Zhipeng Wang
First: 2026-06-24T16:57:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:57:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous AI agents increasingly transact across organizational boundaries, a fundamental trust challenge emerges: how can an agent assess whether an unknown counterpart is trustworthy? The ERC-8004 protocol addresses this challenge with the first permissionless trust layer for AI agent economies, built around three on-chain registries for Identity, Reputation, and Validation. Despite its rapid adoption, the protocol has not been studied empirically, leaving it unclear whether the information it records provides a trustworthy basis for decision-making. To address this gap, we present the first empirical study of ERC-8004 across three chains: Ethereum, BNB Smart Chain (BSC), and Base, covering the period from protocol deployment through May 13, 2026. We crawl on-chain Identity and Reputation events, off-chain files, and x402 payment transactions. On the identity side, we find that most registrations are placeholders rather than active agents, with only a small fraction (3%, 4%, and 15% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exposing a valid ERC-8004 registration file with at least one live service endpoint. On the reputation side, we show that the Registry, as currently deployed, cannot function as a trust signal: values are not commensurable, feedback records are rarely grounded in verifiable interactions, and reputation can be manipulated at minimal cost. Consistent with these design weaknesses, we find that a substantial fraction of reviewers (73.6%, 59.2%, and 90.6% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exhibit coordinated Sybil behavior. After removing Sybil-flagged feedback, 15.5%, 72.3%, and 89.4% of rated agents, respectively, are left with no valid feedback. We then turn these findings into concrete recommendations for future revisions of ERC-8004. Our study yields actionable protocol-design implications and establishes an empirical baseline for research on AI agent markets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无信任代理能被信任吗？对ERC-8004去中心化AI代理生态系统的实证研究
随着自主AI代理越来越多地跨越组织边界进行交易，一个根本的信任挑战浮现：代理如何评估未知的对等方是否可信？ERC-8004协议通过构建围绕身份、声誉和验证三个链上注册表的首个无许可信任层，解决了这一挑战。尽管该协议已被迅速采用，但尚未进行实证研究，因此尚不清楚其记录的信息是否能作为决策的可信基础。为填补这一空白，我们首次对ERC-8004在三个链（以太坊、BNB智能链（BSC）和Base）上的实证研究，覆盖从协议部署至2026年5月13日的时期。我们爬取链上身份和声誉事件、链下文件以及x402支付交易。 在身份方面，我们发现大多数注册为占位符而非活跃代理，仅一小部分（以太坊、BSC和Base分别为3%、4%和15%）展示出至少一个活跃服务端点的有效ERC-8004注册文件。在声誉方面，我们表明当前部署的注册表无法作为信任信号：数值不可比，反馈记录极少基于可验证的互动，且声誉可以极低成本被操纵。与这些设计缺陷一致，我们发现大量评审者（以太坊、BSC和Base分别为73.6%、59.2%和90.6%）表现出协调的Sybil行为。在移除Sybil标记的反馈后，分别有15.5%、72.3%和89.4%的被评分代理无有效反馈。我们随后将这些发现转化为对ERC-8004未来修订的具体建议。本研究提供了可操作的协议设计启示，并为AI代理市场研究建立了实证基准。
Summary / 总结
As autonomous AI agents increasingly operate across organizational boundaries, the need for a trustworthy mechanism to evaluate counterparties arises. The ERC-8004 protocol introduces a permissionless trust layer via on-chain registries for Identity, Reputation, and Validation, yet its effectiveness remains untested. An empirical study across Ethereum, BNB Smart Chain, and Base from deployment to May 2026 reveals that most registered agents are placeholders, with only 3–15% exposing valid service endpoints. After filtering out Sybil feedback, 15.5–89.4% of agents have no valid reviews, undermining the protocol’s trustworthiness and suggesting urgent design revisions.

Why Multi-Step Tool-Use Reinforcement Learning Collapses and How Supervisory Signals Fix It
Authors: Yupu Hao, Zhuoran Jin, Huanxuan Liao, Kang Liu, Jun Zhao
First: 2026-06-24T16:55:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:55:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Tool use enables large language models (LLMs) to perform complex tasks, and recent agentic reinforcement learning (RL) methods show promise for enhancing model capabilities. However, RL alone often leads to instability or limited gains in tool-use tasks. In our experiments, some models exhibit catastrophic collapse, where performance abruptly drops and tool-invocation structures fail. The analysis reveals that these failures stem from unexpected probability spikes in specific control tokens, disrupting structured execution, yet the underlying tool-use capability remains intact, merely obscured by specific formats. To address this, we systematically investigate a diverse set of supervisory signals, including off-policy supervision, hint-based guidance, erroneous example supervision, and others, applied under both synchronous and interleaved training schemes. We find that interleaving supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with RL substantially improves stability, but exhibits degraded performance under format and content out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluation. We also analyze the impact of learning rates and generalization across settings. These results highlight the importance of understanding RL failures and demonstrate how diverse supervisory signals can guide exploratory learning, enabling robust training of LLMs for complex, multi-step tool-use tasks. Our Code is available at https://github.com/hypasd-art/Tool-RL-Box.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为什么多步工具使用强化学习会崩溃，以及监督信号如何修复它
工具使用使大语言模型（LLM）能够执行复杂任务，近期的代理强化学习（RL）方法在增强模型能力方面展现出潜力。然而，仅靠RL往往导致工具使用任务中的不稳定或收益有限。在我们的实验中，部分模型出现灾难性崩溃，表现为性能骤降且工具调用结构失效。分析表明，这些失败源于特定控制令牌出现意外概率激增，破坏了结构化执行，但底层工具使用能力仍完好，仅被特定格式掩盖。为解决此问题，我们系统性研究了多种监督信号，包括离策略监督、提示引导、错误示例监督等，在同步与交错训练方案下应用。我们发现，将监督微调（SFT）与RL交错训练可显著提升稳定性，但在格式和内容分布外（OOD）评估中表现下降。同时分析了学习率影响及不同设置下的泛化能力。这些结果强调理解RL失败的重要性，并展示多样监督信号如何引导探索性学习，从而实现LLM在复杂多步工具使用任务中的稳健训练。代码见 https://github.com/hypasd-art/Tool-RL-Box。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why multi-step tool-use reinforcement learning often fails, showing that models can experience catastrophic collapse where performance drops sharply due to unexpected probability spikes in control tokens, disrupting structured execution despite intact tool-use capability. The authors systematically evaluate various supervisory signals—including off-policy supervision, hint-based guidance, and erroneous examples—applied under synchronous and interleaved training schemes. Their experiments reveal that interleaving supervised fine-tuning with RL improves training stability but reduces performance under out-of-distribution evaluations, while learning rate adjustments and generalization across settings further influence outcomes, underscoring the need for careful supervision design in complex tool-use tasks.
该论文研究了多步工具使用强化学习为何常崩溃，发现仅靠强化学习会导致控制词概率异常飙升，破坏结构化执行，而底层工具能力仍完好。作者系统测试了多种监督信号（如离策略监督、提示引导、错误示例等）在同步与交错训练下的效果，发现交错监督微调与强化学习可提升训练稳定性，但会降低分布外评估表现。同时分析了学习率影响与泛化能力，指出多样监督信号有助于引导探索，从而实现复杂工具任务的鲁棒训练。
Paid Voices vs. Public Feeds: Interpretable Cross-Platform Theme-Based Analysis of Climate Discourse
Authors: Samantha Sudhoff, Pranav Perumal, Zhaoqing Wu, Tunazzina Islam
First: 2026-01-19T19:00:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:54:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Climate discourse online shapes public understanding of climate change and informs political and policy debate, yet it unfolds across structurally different environments: paid advertising platforms host targeted, institutionally produced messaging, while public social media reflects largely organic, user-driven discussion. We present a comparative analysis of climate discourse across paid advertisements on Meta (previously Facebook) and public posts on Bluesky from July 2024 to September 2025. To support it, we develop an interpretable thematic discovery pipeline that clusters texts by semantic similarity and uses large language models (LLMs) to label clusters with concise, human-interpretable themes, requiring no predefined topic inventory or seed set. Using these themes, we find the two environments diverge systematically: paid advertising centers on strategic promotion of specific solutions in a formal, forward-looking register, whereas organic discourse centers on systemic critique in a crisis-oriented, scientifically grounded one. We also evaluate the utility of the discovered themes through downstream stance prediction and theme-guided retrieval tasks. While our analysis focuses on climate communication, the framework generalizes to comparative thematic analysis across heterogeneous communication environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：付费声音与公共动态：气候话语的跨平台主题可解释分析
在线气候话语塑造公众对气候变化的理解，并影响政治与政策辩论，但其在结构迥异的环境中展开：付费广告平台承载定向、机构化生成的信息，而公共社交媒体则反映主要由用户驱动的有机讨论。我们对2024年7月至2025年9月期间Meta（原Facebook）上的付费广告与Bluesky平台上的公共帖子中的气候话语进行了比较分析。为此，我们构建了一个可解释的主题发现流程，通过语义相似性聚类文本，并利用大语言模型（LLM）为聚类分配简洁、人类可理解的主题，无需预设主题词典或种子集。通过这些主题，我们发现两个环境系统性地存在差异：付费广告侧重于正式、前瞻性的特定解决方案推广，而有机讨论则聚焦于危机导向、科学基础的系统性批判。我们还通过下游立场预测与主题引导检索任务评估了所发现主题的实用性。尽管本分析聚焦气候传播，但该框架可推广至异质传播环境中的比较主题分析。

In-Context World Modeling for Robotic Control
Authors: Siyin Wang, Junhao Shi, Senyu Fei, Zhaoyang Fu, Li Ji, Jingjing Gong, Xipeng Qiu
First: 2026-06-24T16:53:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:53:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often fail to generalize to novel setups, such as altered camera viewpoints or robot morphologies, because they are typically conditioned only on current observations and language instructions. By ignoring the underlying system configuration as a variable, these models implicitly assume a fixed execution context encountered during training, necessitating data-intensive fine-tuning for any new environment. In this work, we introduce In-Context World Modeling (ICWM), a framework that treats system identification as an in-context adaptation problem. ICWM enables robot policies to autonomously infer essential system variables from a short history of self-generated, task-agnostic interactions. Unlike traditional In-Context Learning that uses demonstrations to specify what task to perform, ICWM leverages the context window to understand how the system operates. By processing these interactions before task execution, the model implicitly captures the world dynamics of the current system, enabling adaptation to novel configurations without parameter updates. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real-world robot platforms demonstrate that ICWM significantly outperforms standard VLA baselines on novel camera viewpoints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人控制的上下文世界建模
现代视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型常因仅依赖当前观测和语言指令而难以泛化到新环境（如改变的相机视角或机器人形态），隐式假设训练时遇到的执行上下文固定，需大量数据微调以适应新环境。本文提出上下文世界建模（ICWM）框架，将系统识别视为上下文自适应问题，使机器人策略能从自生成的、与任务无关的交互历史中自主推断关键系统变量。与传统上下文学习依赖演示指定任务不同，ICWM利用上下文窗口理解系统运行机制，在执行任务前处理这些交互，隐式捕捉当前系统的动态，从而无需参数更新即可适应新配置。仿真与真实机器人平台的大量实验表明，ICWM在新相机视角下显著优于标准VLA基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of Vision-Language-Action models to novel robot setups due to their reliance on fixed training contexts, this work proposes In-Context World Modeling (ICWM), a framework that enables robots to infer system variables from task-agnostic self-interactions within their context window. Instead of fine-tuning parameters, ICWM allows policies to implicitly learn the dynamics of the current environment by processing prior interactions, thereby adapting to new camera viewpoints or robot morphologies without retraining. Experimental results across simulation and real robots show ICWM substantially outperforms standard VLA baselines on novel configurations.
为了解决视觉-语言-动作模型在面对新机器人配置时泛化能力差的问题，本文提出了一种名为“上下文世界建模”（ICWM）的方法，该方法通过分析机器人自动生成的、与任务无关的交互历史，自动推断系统关键变量（如摄像头视角或机器人形态）。与传统依赖任务演示的上下文学习不同，ICWM利用上下文窗口隐式学习系统动态，从而在无需参数更新的情况下适应新配置。实验表明，ICWM在模拟和真实机器人平台上的表现显著优于标准VLA基线模型，尤其在摄像头视角变化的任务中表现突出。
RoboRouter: Training-Free Policy Routing for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Yiteng Chen, Zhe Cao, Hongjia Ren, Chenjie Yang, Wenbo Li, Shiyi Wang, Yemin Wang, Li Zhang, Yanming Shao, Zhenjun Zhao, Huiping Zhuang, Qingyao Wu
First: 2026-03-09T02:14:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:52:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research on robotic manipulation has developed a diverse set of policy paradigms, including vision-language-action (VLA) models, vision-action (VA) policies, and code-based compositional approaches. Concrete policies typically attain high success rates on specific task distributions, but limited generalization beyond it. Rather than proposing another monolithic policy, we propose to leverage the complementary strengths of existing approaches through intelligent policy routing. We introduce RoboRouter, a training-free framework that maintains a pool of heterogeneous policies and learns to select the best-performing policy for each task through accumulated execution experience. Given a new task, RoboRouter constructs a semantic task representation, retrieves historical records of similar tasks, predicts the optimal policy choice without requiring trial-and-error, and incorporates structured feedback to refine subsequent routing decisions. Integrating a new policy into the system requires only a lightweight evaluation and does not incur training overhead. Across simulation benchmark and real-world evaluations, RoboRouter consistently outperforms individual policies, improving the average success rate by more than 3% in simulation and 13% in real-world settings, while preserving execution efficiency. Our results demonstrate that intelligent routing across heterogeneous, off-the-shelf policies provides a practical and scalable pathway toward building more capable robotic systems.

标题：RoboRouter：无需训练的策略路由用于机器人操作

Experimental results show that RoboRouter consistently outperforms individual policies, boosting average success rates by over 3% in simulation and 13% in real-world settings, while maintaining execution efficiency.
为解决现有机器人操作策略泛化能力不足的问题，本文提出RoboRouter，一种无需训练的策略路由框架，通过积累的执行经验智能选择异构策略池中的最优策略。面对新任务，RoboRouter构建语义表示、检索相似历史任务、无需试错预测最优策略，并利用结构化反馈优化后续路由决策。新策略接入仅需轻量评估，无需训练开销。实验表明，RoboRouter在仿真和真实环境中均优于单个策略，平均成功率分别提升3%和13%，同时保持执行效率。
Narrative Feature or Structured Feature? A Study of Large Language Models to Identify Cancer Patients at Risk of Heart Failure
Authors: Ziyi Chen, Mengyuan Zhang, Mustafa Mohammed Ahmed, Yi Guo, Thomas J. George, Jiang Bian, Yonghui Wu
First: 2024-03-18T02:42:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:44:10+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cancer treatments are known to introduce cardiotoxicity, negatively impacting outcomes and survivorship. Identifying cancer patients at risk of heart failure (HF) is critical to improving cancer treatment outcomes and safety. This study examined machine learning (ML) models to identify cancer patients at risk of HF using electronic health records (EHRs), including traditional ML, Time-Aware long short-term memory (T-LSTM), and large language models (LLMs) using novel narrative features derived from the structured medical codes. We identified a cancer cohort of 12,806 patients from the University of Florida Health, diagnosed with lung, breast, and colorectal cancers, among which 1,602 individuals developed HF after cancer. The LLM, GatorTron-3.9B, achieved the best F1 scores, outperforming the traditional support vector machines by 39%, the T-LSTM deep learning model by 7%, and a widely used transformer model, BERT, by 5.6%. The analysis shows that the proposed narrative features remarkably increased feature density and improved performance.

为应对癌症治疗引发的心脏毒性导致心力衰竭的风险，本研究比较了基于电子健康记录的多种机器学习模型。通过从结构化医疗编码中提取新颖的叙事特征，评估了传统机器学习、T-LSTM及大型语言模型的表现。在12,806名癌症患者队列中，GatorTron-3.9B大语言模型表现最优，F1分数比支持向量机高39%，比T-LSTM高7%，比BERT高5.6%，证明叙事特征显著提升了模型性能。
Privacy Vulnerabilities of Attention Layers in Tabular Foundation Models and Protection of High-Risk Queries
Authors: Tânia Carvalho, Maxime Cordy
First: 2026-06-24T16:42:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:42:21+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 12 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tabular foundation models are commonly assumed to present limited privacy concerns as they are often pre-trained on large collections of synthetic data. However, these models leverage in-context learning, where sensitive records may be provided directly at inference time as labelled context examples. In this paper, we demonstrate that predictions generated via the attention mechanism leak sufficient information to enable effective Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs). To highlight this vulnerability, we propose AMIA (Attention-based Membership Inference Attack), a shadow-model-free attack that exploits the concentration of transformer attention patterns. Our results show that attention mechanisms reveal strong membership signals, which exceed classical confidence-based attacks, achieving an average gain of 7.7\%, specially in low false-positive regimes. To mitigate this risk, we introduce an inference-time defence inspired by $k$-anonymity principles. This approach reduces the uniqueness of context-key representations without introducing random noise or retraining the model. By targeting only high-risk queries identified through AMIA scores, the defence substantially reduces membership leakage of this attack by an average of 50\% and 25\% against confidence-based attacks, while preserving predictive utility with only 3.9\% performance degradation. Beyond showing that context examples are vulnerable, we further demonstrate that fine-tuning introduces an additional source of privacy risk. In particular, samples whose prediction confidence increases after fine-tuning become more susceptible to MIAs, indicating that fine-tuning can amplify memorisation and expose sensitive training information through confidence shifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：表格式基础模型中注意力层的隐私漏洞及高风险查询的保护
表格式基础模型通常被认为隐私风险较低，因其常在大规模合成数据上预训练。然而，这些模型利用上下文学习，敏感记录可能在推理时直接作为带标签的上下文示例提供。本文证明，通过注意力机制生成的预测会泄露足够信息，以支持有效的成员推断攻击（MIAs）。为凸显此漏洞，我们提出AMIA（基于注意力的成员推断攻击），一种无需影子模型的攻击方法，利用Transformer注意力模式的集中性。结果表明，注意力机制揭示了强成员信号，其效果优于传统基于置信度的攻击，平均提升7.7%，尤其在低假阳性率下。为缓解此风险，我们引入受k-匿名原则启发的推理时防御机制，通过降低上下文键表示的独特性，而不引入随机噪声或重新训练模型。该防御仅针对AMIA评分识别的高风险查询，平均减少该攻击的成员泄露50%，对置信度攻击减少25%，同时仅造成3.9%的预测性能下降。此外，我们还表明上下文示例本身存在漏洞，并进一步证明微调引入了额外的隐私风险：微调后预测置信度提升的样本更易受MIAs攻击，表明微调可能放大记忆并通过置信度变化暴露敏感训练信息。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the underappreciated privacy risks in tabular foundation models, which are often assumed safe due to synthetic pre-training data. They introduce AMIA, a shadow-model-free attack that exploits attention pattern concentration, achieving 7.7% higher accuracy than confidence-based attacks, especially in low false-positive settings. To counter this, they propose an inference-time defence based on k-anonymity principles that reduces context-key uniqueness without retraining or adding noise, reducing AMIA leakage by 50% and confidence-based attacks by 25%, with only 3.9% drop in predictive performance. The study also shows that fine-tuning can amplify privacy risks by increasing confidence in sensitive samples, making them more vulnerable to MIAs.
本文指出，尽管表格式基础模型常因使用合成数据预训练而被认为隐私风险低，但其在推理时通过注意力机制可能泄露敏感信息。作者提出AMIA攻击，利用注意力模式集中性无需影子模型即可识别成员身份，其准确率比传统置信度攻击高7.7%（尤其在低误报率下）。为防御，他们设计一种基于k-匿名原理的推理时防御机制，通过匿名化上下文键表示，使AMIA攻击泄露率降低50%，对置信度攻击降低25%，仅导致模型性能下降3.9%。此外，研究发现微调会加剧隐私风险，因预测置信度提升的样本更易被攻击，表明微调可能强化记忆并暴露训练数据。
G2DP: Diffusion Planning with Spatio-Temporal Grid Guidance
Authors: Hang Yu, Ye Jin, Alessandro Canevaro, Julian Schmidt, Julian Jordan, Peizheng Li, Marc Kaufeld, Silvan Lindner, Johannes Betz, Wilhelm Stork
First: 2026-06-24T16:38:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:38:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In autonomous driving, diffusion-based planners have emerged as a promising paradigm for robust motion planning in dense and interactive traffic, as they can effectively model diverse driving behaviors. However, their inherent stochasticity often requires explicit guidance during denoising to ensure safety and route adherence for robust closed-loop execution. Existing guidance typically relies on sparse, entity-centric geometric queries or post-hoc refinement, yielding limited situational awareness and fragile performance in interactive scenes. To address this issue, we propose G2DP (Grid-Guided Diffusion Planning), a diffusion-based planner that directly enforces dense environmental constraints through inference-time guidance. Specifically, G2DP constructs a differentiable spatio-temporal cost volume by fusing probabilistic future occupancy distributions with a route-progress map. By formulating this volume as a continuous safety energy functional, it injects dense gradients directly into the denoising loop, actively steering trajectory generation toward collision-free and progress-optimal regions. Extensive closed-loop evaluations show that G2DP achieves state-of-the-art performance on nuPlan, outperforming the strongest imitation-learning baseline by +7.2 points in reactive score. It further maintains top scores in zero-shot transfers to interPlan and DeepScenario benchmarks, with collision avoidance improving by +10.15 over the unguided approach on interPlan. These results demonstrate that spatio-temporal cost grids serve as an effective representation for robust guidance in diffusion-based planning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：G2DP：时空网格引导的扩散规划
在自动驾驶领域，基于扩散的规划器已成为在密集且交互式交通环境中实现稳健运动规划的有前景范式，因其能有效建模多样化的驾驶行为。然而，其固有的随机性通常需要在去噪过程中显式引导，以确保安全性和路线遵循，从而实现稳健的闭环执行。现有引导方法通常依赖稀疏、以实体为中心的几何查询或事后优化，导致情境感知有限，在交互场景中表现脆弱。为解决此问题，我们提出G2DP（网格引导扩散规划），一种在推理时直接通过环境密集约束引导的扩散规划器。具体而言，G2DP通过融合概率未来占据分布与路线进度图，构建可微分的时空代价体。通过将该体建模为连续的安全能量函数，它将密集梯度直接注入去噪循环，主动引导轨迹生成趋向无碰撞且进度最优的区域。大量闭环评估表明，G2DP在nuPlan上达到最先进性能，比最强的模仿学习基线在反应评分上高出7.2分。它在零样本迁移至interPlan和DeepScenario基准测试中仍保持领先，其中在interPlan上相比无引导方法碰撞规避提升10.15分。这些结果表明，时空代价网格是扩散规划中实现稳健引导的有效表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safer and more reliable motion planning in dense traffic, G2DP introduces a diffusion-based planner that integrates dense spatio-temporal guidance directly into the denoising process. It constructs a differentiable cost volume by combining probabilistic future occupancy with a route-progress map, then treats this as a continuous safety energy function to inject gradients that steer trajectory generation toward collision-free and progress-optimal paths. Experimental results show G2DP achieves state-of-the-art performance on nuPlan, outperforming the best imitation-learning baseline by +7.2 in reactive score, and maintains strong zero-shot performance on interPlan and DeepScenario, with collision avoidance improving by +10.15 over unguided diffusion.
为提升密集交通场景下自动驾驶的规划安全性与可靠性，本文提出G2DP，一种在去噪过程中直接引入密集时空引导的扩散规划方法。该方法通过融合概率未来占据分布与路线进度图构建可微分代价体，并将其作为连续安全能量函数注入去噪循环，主动引导轨迹生成避开碰撞并保持进度最优。实验表明，G2DP在nuPlan上达到最先进性能，相较最强模仿学习基线在反应评分上提升7.2分，并在interPlan与DeepScenario零样本迁移中保持领先，碰撞规避能力较无引导扩散方法提升10.15分。
MIMFlow: Integrating Masked Image Modeling with Normalizing Flows for End-to-End Image Generation
Authors: Yang Chen, Xiaowei Xu, Shuai Wang, Xinwen Zhang, Qiushi Guo, Tiezheng Ge, Limin Wang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-24T16:37:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:37:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Normalizing Flows (NFs) are powerful generative models capable of exact density estimation and sampling. However, their strict invertibility often forces the model to exhaust its capacity on low-level pixel details, hindering the capture of high-level semantic structures. While Masked Image Modeling (MIM) has excelled in representation learning, its integration into generative pipelines has remained largely modular and disjointed. In this paper, we propose MIMFlow, a unified end-to-end framework that jointly optimizes latent semantics, pixel reconstruction, and generative flow. By employing a VAE encoder to infer semantic latent from masked images, MIMFlow achieves a principled decoupling of the generative task: the Normalizing Flow focuses on modeling a simplified, low-frequency semantic manifold, while a specialized decoder handles high-frequency synthesis. This design effectively resolves the inherent capacity bottleneck of NFs, allowing the model to prioritize global structural coherence over redundant noise. Empirical results on ImageNet 256$\times$256 show that MIMFlow-L reaches 71.3\% linear probing accuracy and an FID of 2.50. Despite using only 128 tokens (50\% fewer than standard models), it yields a 32.8\% performance gain over similar-scale NF baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/MIMFlow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MIMFlow：将掩码图像建模与归一化流集成用于端到端图像生成
归一化流（NFs）是强大的生成模型，能够进行精确的概率密度估计和采样。然而，其严格的可逆性常迫使模型将全部容量用于低级像素细节，阻碍了高级语义结构的捕捉。虽然掩码图像建模（MIM）在表征学习方面表现优异，但其与生成流程的集成仍主要为模块化且分离的。本文提出MIMFlow，一个统一的端到端框架，联合优化潜在语义、像素重建和生成流。通过VAE编码器从掩码图像推断语义潜在表示，MIMFlow实现了生成任务的原理性解耦：归一化流专注于建模简化、低频语义流形，而专用解码器负责高频合成。该设计有效解决了NFs的固有容量瓶颈，使模型优先保证全局结构一致性而非冗余噪声。在ImageNet 256×256上的实验证明，MIMFlow-L线性探测准确率达71.3%，FID为2.50。尽管仅使用128个token（比标准模型少50%），其性能较同类规模的NF基线提升32.8%。代码见https://github.com/MCG-NJU/MIMFlow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the capacity limitations of Normalizing Flows in capturing high-level semantics due to their strict invertibility, this paper proposes MIMFlow, an end-to-end framework that integrates Masked Image Modeling with Normalizing Flows. The method uses a VAE encoder to extract semantic latents from masked images, enabling the flow to focus on low-frequency semantic manifolds while a specialized decoder handles high-frequency pixel synthesis. Experimental results on ImageNet 256x256 show that MIMFlow-L achieves 71.3% linear probing accuracy and an FID of 2.50, outperforming similar-scale NF baselines by 32.8% despite using only 128 tokens — half the token count of standard models.
为了解决正常化流（NFs）因严格可逆性导致难以捕捉高层语义的问题，同时利用掩码图像建模（MIM）强大的表征学习能力，本文提出MIMFlow框架，将语义潜变量、像素重建与生成流联合优化。该框架通过VAE编码器从掩码图像中提取语义潜变量，使NF专注于低频语义流建模，而专用解码器负责高频像素合成。在ImageNet 256x256上的实验表明，MIMFlow-L实现71.3%线性探测准确率和FID 2.50，相比同等规模的NF基线模型性能提升32.8%，且仅使用128个token（仅为标准模型的一半）
The Tatoxa System for Text Detoxification in Low-Resource Languages: The Case of Tatar
Authors: Ilseyar Alimova, Bogdan Monogov, Artyom Mazur, Daniil Antonov, Vsevolod Karimov, Vitaliy Egorov, Bulat Khakimov, Alexander Panchenko
First: 2026-06-24T16:36:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:36:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text detoxification, the automated detection and mitigation of abusive and harmful content, is essential for ensuring the safety of online communities and protecting users. However, low resource languages such as Tatar have received little research attention. In this paper we present Tatoxa, a novel state-of-the-art system for text detoxification in the Tatar language. Comparative experiments show that the proposed approach outperforms existing open source and proprietary commercial LLMs on key quality metrics. We also introduce a new dataset for text detoxification in Tatar, designed for fine tuning and evaluation in low resource settings. Finally, cross lingual transfer experiments indicate that transfer from other languages, including the culturally close Russian, performs significantly worse than training on native Tatar data even when a large Russian corpus is available.
