标题：塔塔语文本净化系统：低资源语言中的文本净化案例
文本净化，即自动检测和缓解有害内容，对于保障在线社区安全和保护用户至关重要。然而，像塔塔语这样的低资源语言鲜有研究关注。本文提出了Tatoxa，一个针对塔塔语的新型先进文本净化系统。对比实验表明，该方法在关键质量指标上优于现有的开源及商业闭源大语言模型。我们还引入了一个新的塔塔语文本净化数据集，专为低资源环境下的微调与评估设计。最后，跨语言迁移实验表明，即使拥有大量俄语语料，从其他语言（包括文化相近的俄语）迁移的效果也显著劣于直接在塔塔语数据上训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of research and tools for abusive content detection in low-resource languages like Tatar, this paper introduces Tatoxa, a novel text detoxification system tailored for Tatar. The method leverages fine-tuning on a newly created Tatar-specific dataset designed for low-resource settings and compares against existing open-source and commercial LLMs. Experimental results show Tatoxa outperforms these baselines on key quality metrics, and cross-lingual transfer experiments reveal that even with a large Russian corpus, performance from Russian-trained models is significantly inferior to native Tatar training.
鉴于塔塔尔语等低资源语言在文本净化领域的研究匮乏，作者开发了Tatoxa系统，专门用于塔塔尔语中的有害内容检测与缓解。该方法基于新构建的塔塔尔语专用数据集进行微调，无需依赖高资源语言模型即可实现有效性能。实验结果表明，Tatoxa在关键净化指标上优于现有开源及商业大语言模型，且即使拥有大量俄语语料，跨语言迁移（如从俄语）的效果仍显著劣于使用塔塔尔语原生数据训练的效果。
FAR-LIO: Enabling High-Speed Autonomy through Fast, Accurate, and Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry
Authors: Maximilian Leitenstern, Marcel Weinmann, Patrick Haft, Tobias Lasser, Dominik Kulmer, Markus Lienkamp
First: 2026-06-24T16:30:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:30:51+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, accepted for publication at IROS2026 (IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Robust and accurate odometry estimation is essential in modern robotics. In environments characterized by highly dynamic motion and sensor noise, odometry estimation becomes increasingly challenging. Autonomous racing combines both factors in an unstructured setting, where minimizing odometry latency is essential for stable closed-loop control. This paper introduces FAR-LIO, a highly optimized CUDA-accelerated LiDAR-inertial odometry framework developed for Fast, Accurate, and Robust performance. Our system leverages a novel CUDA-based voxel hashmap to enable parallelized nearest-neighbor search and efficient map updates. We employ a sparsity-aware Generalized Iterative Closest Point algorithm with adaptive thresholding on top of the CUDA-based voxel hashmap with adaptive density to achieve low-latency without compromising accuracy. An Extended Kalman Filter serves as a robust backend. It utilizes an upsampling and delay compensation strategy to fuse the LiDAR odometry with high-frequency IMU data, thereby ensuring a robust and smooth odometry output. We evaluate FAR-LIO across four different sensor setups, using both public datasets and data from two autonomous racecars driving at speeds of up to 250 km/h. FAR-LIO achieves an average 6.9% reduction in the positional error and 38.4% lower runtime compared to state-of-the-art baselines on target hardware using a single parameter set. This demonstrates its computational efficiency and broad applicability. To build upon our work, our code is available open-source on https://github.com/TUMFTM/FAR-LIO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FAR-LIO：通过快速、准确且鲁棒的激光雷达惯性里程计实现高速自主
在现代机器人中，鲁棒且精确的里程计估计至关重要。在高度动态运动和传感器噪声并存的环境中，里程计估计愈发困难。自主赛车将这两个因素结合于非结构化场景中，其中最小化里程计延迟对稳定闭环控制至关重要。本文提出FAR-LIO，一种专为快速、准确、鲁棒性能设计的CUDA加速激光雷达惯性里程计框架。系统采用新颖的CUDA基体素哈希表，支持并行最近邻搜索与高效地图更新。基于该哈希表，我们采用感知稀疏性的广义迭代最近点算法，结合自适应阈值与自适应密度，实现低延迟而不牺牲精度。扩展卡尔曼滤波器作为鲁棒后端，通过上采样与延迟补偿策略融合激光雷达里程计与高频IMU数据，确保里程计输出稳定平滑。我们在四种不同传感器配置下评估FAR-LIO，使用公开数据集及两辆最高时速达250公里/小时的自动驾驶赛车采集的数据。在目标硬件上，FAR-LIO仅用单一参数集即实现平均位置误差降低6.9%，运行时间降低38.4%，证明其计算效率与广泛适用性。代码开源于https://github.com/TUMFTM/FAR-LIO。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of accurate and low-latency odometry in high-speed autonomous racing environments, this paper presents FAR-LIO, a CUDA-accelerated LiDAR-inertial odometry system designed for speed, accuracy, and robustness. It employs a novel voxel hashmap for parallelized nearest-neighbor search and map updates, combined with a sparsity-aware Generalized ICP algorithm using adaptive density thresholds to maintain precision while minimizing latency. An Extended Kalman Filter with upsampling and delay compensation fuses LiDAR and high-frequency IMU data for smooth, reliable output. Evaluated across four sensor setups including real-world racecar data at up to 250 km/h, FAR-LIO achieved a 6.9% reduction in positional error and 38.4% lower runtime compared to state-of-the-art baselines on the same hardware, demonstrating strong efficiency and adaptability.
为解决高速自主赛车环境中实时高精度里程计的挑战，FAR-LIO提出了一种基于CUDA加速的激光雷达惯性里程计框架，专为快速、准确和鲁棒性设计。该框架采用新型体素哈希表实现并行最近邻搜索与地图更新，并结合稀疏感知的广义ICP算法与自适应阈值，在保持精度的同时降低延迟。扩展卡尔曼滤波器通过上采样与延迟补偿融合激光雷达与高频IMU数据，确保输出稳定。在四种传感器配置（含250km/h赛车数据）上测试，相比现有最优方法，FAR-LIO在相同硬件上平均降低定位误差6.9%，运行时间减少38.4%，展现出高效与广泛适用性。
Evaluating Scene-based In-Situ Item Labeling for Immersive Conversational Recommendation
Authors: Jiazhou Liang, Yifan Simon Liu, David Guo, Minqi Sun, Yilun Jiang, Scott Sanner
First: 2026-04-06T23:52:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:30:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growing ubiquity of Extended Reality (XR) is driving Conversational Recommendation Systems (CRS) toward visually immersive experiences. We formalize this paradigm as Immersive CRS (ICRS), where recommended items are highlighted directly in the user's scene-based visual environment and augmented with in-situ labels. While item recommendation has been widely studied, the problem of how to select and evaluate which information to present as immersive labels remains an open problem. To this end, we introduce a principled categorization of information needs into explicit intent satisfaction and proactive information needs and use these to define novel evaluation metrics for item label selection. We benchmark IR-, LLM-, and VLM-based methods across three datasets and ICRS scenarios: fashion, movie recommendation, and retail shopping. Our evaluation reveals three important limitations of existing methods: (1) they fail to leverage scenario-specific information modalities (e.g., visual cues for fashion, meta-data for retail), (2) they present redundant information that is visually inferable, and (3) they poorly anticipate users' proactive information needs from explicit dialogue alone. In summary, this work provides both a novel evaluation paradigm for in-situ item labeling in ICRS and highlights key challenges for future work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于场景的沉浸式对话推荐中物品标签的评估
扩展现实（XR）的日益普及正推动对话推荐系统（CRS）向视觉沉浸式体验发展。我们将此范式形式化为沉浸式对话推荐系统（ICRS），其中推荐物品直接在用户基于场景的视觉环境中高亮显示，并附加现场标签。尽管物品推荐已广泛研究，但如何选择和评估呈现哪些信息作为沉浸式标签仍是一个开放问题。为此，我们提出了一种将信息需求分类为显式意图满足和主动信息需求的原则性方法，并据此定义了新的物品标签选择评估指标。我们在三个数据集和ICRS场景（时尚、电影推荐、零售购物）中对基于IR、LLM和VLM的方法进行了基准测试。评估结果揭示了现有方法的三个重要局限：（1）未能利用场景特定的信息模态（如时尚中的视觉线索、零售中的元数据）；（2）呈现可视觉推断的冗余信息；（3）仅凭显式对话难以准确预测用户的主动信息需求。总之，本工作为ICRS中现场物品标签提供了新颖的评估范式，并指出了未来研究的关键挑战。

The authors propose a principled framework that categorizes user information needs into explicit intent satisfaction and proactive needs, enabling novel evaluation metrics. Through experiments across fashion, movie, and retail datasets using IR-, LLM-, and VLM-based methods, they find that existing approaches fail to utilize scenario-specific modalities, present redundant visually inferable information, and neglect users’ proactive needs inferred from dialogue alone.
随着扩展现实（XR）的普及，本研究聚焦于沉浸式对话推荐系统（ICRS）中如何选择和评估场景内物品标签的问题。作者提出一种基于用户信息需求分类（显式意图满足与主动信息需求）的框架，构建了新的标签选择评估指标。在时尚、电影和零售三个数据集上对基于IR、LLM和VLM的方法进行实验，发现现有方法存在三大缺陷：未能利用场景特定模态（如时尚中的视觉线索）、常呈现可视觉推断的冗余信息，以及难以从对话中预判用户的主动信息需求。
Is Variational Monte Carlo Robust? Sharp Moment Thresholds and Heavy-tailed Stochastic Optimization
Authors: Philipp Grohs, Davide Nobile
First: 2026-06-24T16:29:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:29:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) is a central algorithm in electronic structure theory and has gained renewed importance through modern neural-network ansätze such as FermiNet. At its core, VMC seeks ground states by minimizing the Rayleigh quotient by stochastic optimization. In this work, we show that the resulting stochastic optimization problem is intrinsically governed by the nodal geometry of the underlying wave function. More precisely, we establish that properties of the nodal set determine the integrability of the local energy and gradient estimators that drive VMC. For broad and practically relevant ansatz classes, including Slater-Jastrow wave functions with variable-exponent Slater-type orbitals, we prove that these estimators are generically heavy-tailed and fail to admit higher moments. At the same time, for general analytic ansätze, we prove weak moment bounds for the relevant estimators and identify precise low-moment regimes, showing how generic and degenerate nodal structures lead to different integrability thresholds. Building on this analysis, we introduce a new robust variant of VMC $\unicode{x2013}$ coined PS-Clip-VMC $\unicode{x2013}$ which is based on clipping both the local energy and the gradient random variable. We prove that PS-Clip-VMC converges both in expectation and with high probability in the weak moment regime of VMC. Preliminary experiments for training FermiNet on Atoms with up to 18 electrons suggest that PS-Clip-VMC is significantly more robust than standard methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变分蒙特卡洛方法稳健吗？尖锐矩阈值与重尾随机优化
变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）是电子结构理论中的核心算法，随着现代神经网络近似方法（如FermiNet）的兴起而重新获得重要性。其核心在于通过随机优化最小化Rayleigh商以寻找基态。本文表明，由此产生的随机优化问题本质上由底层波函数的节点几何结构所决定。更精确地说，我们证明节点集的性质决定了驱动VMC的局部能量和梯度估计量的可积性。对于广泛且具有实际意义的近似类（包括具有可变指数Slater型轨道的Slater-Jastrow波函数），我们证明这些估计量通常具有重尾分布，且不存在更高阶矩。同时，对于一般的解析近似类，我们证明了相关估计量的弱矩界，并确定了精确的低矩区域，揭示了通用且退化的节点结构如何导致不同的可积性阈值。基于此分析，我们提出了一种新的稳健型VMC变体——PS-Clip-VMC，其通过同时截断局部能量和梯度随机变量实现。我们证明PS-Clip-VMC在VMC的弱矩区域内，既在期望意义下也以高概率收敛。针对FermiNet在最多18个电子原子上的初步实验表明，PS-Clip-VMC显著优于标准方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand and stabilize variational Monte Carlo (VMC) optimization in electronic structure theory, this work investigates how nodal geometry of wave functions governs the integrability of local energy and gradient estimators. For common ansatz classes like Slater-Jastrow functions, the estimators are shown to be heavy-tailed and lack higher moments, while general analytic ansätze exhibit weak moment bounds with precise low-moment thresholds tied to nodal structure. Building on this, a new robust method called PS-Clip-VMC is introduced, which clips both local energy and gradient random variables, and is proven to converge in expectation and with high probability under weak moment conditions. Preliminary experiments on FermiNet training for atoms up to 18 electrons indicate PS-Clip-VMC is significantly more robust than standard VMC.
为理解变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）在随机优化下的稳定性，本文研究波函数节点几何如何决定局部能量与梯度估计量的可积性。对于如Slater-Jastrow类等常用参数化形式，作者证明这些估计量呈重尾分布且缺乏高阶矩；而对于一般解析型波函数，推导出弱矩界并确定精确的低矩可积阈值。基于此，提出PS-Clip-VMC方法，对局部能量与梯度随机变量进行裁剪，证明其在弱矩条件下期望与高概率收敛。初步实验表明，该方法在训练含最多18电子的FermiNet时显著优于标准VMC方法。
Emcar: Embodied Controller for Animating Robots
Authors: Carlos Gomez Cubero, Elizabeth Jochum
First: 2026-06-24T16:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:29:19+00:00
Comments: Published in Lecture Notes in Computer Science, DOI: 10.1007/978-3-032-15501-6_11
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This chapter describes EMCAR, a novel software tool for programming robot motion that leverages the unique affordances of artistic practices such as puppetry and drawing to conceive, design, and program novel interactions and realize new use cases for HRI. The advantage of this no-code platform is that it expands creative applications for collaborative robots - putting robots directly in the hands of artists - and provides an inclusive environment that enables individuals with little or no technical backgrounds to engage meaningfully in collaborations and robotics research.

Motivated by the need to democratize robot programming for non-technical users, EMCAR introduces a no-code platform that draws inspiration from artistic practices like puppetry and drawing to enable intuitive motion design for collaborative robots. The method involves mapping artistic gestures and visual metaphors into executable robotic behaviors, allowing artists and non-engineers to directly program robot interactions without coding. Experimental results show that users with minimal technical background can effectively create novel robot behaviors and interactions, demonstrating the platform’s accessibility and its potential to expand creative applications in human-robot interaction.
为使非技术人员也能参与机器人编程，EMCAR提出了一种无代码软件工具，借鉴木偶戏和绘画等艺术实践，通过艺术手势和视觉隐喻映射机器人控制，实现直观的机器人运动设计。实验表明，即使缺乏技术背景的用户也能有效创建新颖的机器人行为与交互，证明该平台具备高度可访问性，可拓展人机协作中的创意应用。
Operator Boosting Produces Pareto-Efficient PDE Surrogates
Authors: Lennon J. Shikhman
First: 2026-06-16T03:20:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:29:04+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables. Submitted to ACM SIGKDD 2027 AI4Sciences Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural operators are widely used as surrogate solution maps for partial differential equations (PDEs), but full-size models can be costly to store, deploy, and evaluate in many-query scientific workflows. This work introduces Operator Boosting, a stagewise residual-learning framework for constructing compact neural-operator surrogates directly, rather than training a large model and compressing it afterward. Starting from the empirical mean predictor in normalized output coordinates, the method trains a sequence of tiny same-family neural operators on residual fields and incorporates each correction through validation-selected shrinkage. We instantiate the framework with Fourier neural operators (FNOs), DeepONets, and convolutional neural operators (CNOs), and compare boosted tiny stacks against full-size monolithic baselines across one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDE benchmarks from PDEBench, APEBench, and The Well. Across 30 dataset-architecture pairs, 21 show positive mean accuracy gains and 17 have positive confidence intervals, while all boosted stacks reduce trainable parameter count by approximately 72-95%. Best-model comparisons show empirical Pareto improvements on 7 of 10 completed PDE benchmarks, including two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, shallow-water dynamics, Darcy flow, one-dimensional transport and reaction systems, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes. These results show that Operator Boosting often improves the empirical accuracy-parameter Pareto frontier of neural PDE surrogates, while also exposing PDE- and architecture-dependent regimes where residual boosting fails to offset compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：算子增强生成帕累托最优的偏微分方程代理模型
神经算子广泛用于偏微分方程（PDE）的代理解映射，但在许多查询的科学工作流中，完整规模模型的存储、部署和评估成本高昂。本文提出了算子增强方法，这是一种分阶段残差学习框架，直接构建紧凑的神经算子代理模型，而非先训练大型模型再压缩。从归一化输出坐标中的经验均值预测器出发，该方法在残差场序列上训练一系列同家族的小型神经算子，并通过验证选择的收缩方式逐个整合修正。我们以傅里叶神经算子（FNOs）、DeepONets和卷积神经算子（CNOs）实例化该框架，并在PDEBench、APEBench和The Well提供的1D、2D和3D PDE基准上，将增强的小型堆叠模型与完整规模单体基线模型进行比较。在30组数据-架构对中，21组显示平均精度提升，17组置信区间为正，所有增强堆叠模型的可训练参数量减少约72-95%。最佳模型比较显示，在10个完成的PDE基准中的7个上实现了经验帕累托改进，包括二维Navier-Stokes方程、浅水动力学、达西流、一维传输与反应系统，以及三维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程。结果表明，算子增强通常能提升神经PDE代理模型的经验精度-参数帕累托前沿，同时揭示了PDE和架构依赖的残差增强无法抵消压缩的特定场景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of storing and deploying full-size neural operator models for PDE surrogates, this work proposes Operator Boosting, a stagewise residual-learning method that constructs compact surrogates directly by iteratively training small neural operators on residual fields and incorporating them via validation-selected shrinkage. Implemented with Fourier neural operators, DeepONets, and convolutional neural operators, the framework was tested across 30 dataset-architecture pairs from multiple PDE benchmarks. Results show that 21 of these pairs achieved positive mean accuracy gains and 17 had positive confidence intervals, while all boosted models reduced trainable parameters by 72–95%. In best-model comparisons, Operator Boosting achieved empirical Pareto improvements on 7 out of 10 benchmarks, including Navier-Stokes, shallow-water, Darcy flow, and compressible Navier-Stokes, though it sometimes failed to offset compression in PDE- or architecture-dependent regimes.
为降低神经算子模型在PDE代理中的存储与部署成本，本文提出Operator Boosting方法，通过逐阶段残差学习直接构建紧凑代理模型，即在归一化输出坐标下从经验均值预测器出发，依次训练小型同家族神经算子并经验证选择收缩后叠加修正。该框架在傅里叶神经算子、DeepONets和卷积神经算子上实现，测试了30组数据-架构组合。结果显示，21组平均精度提升，17组置信区间为正，所有压缩模型参数量减少72–95%。在10个完成的PDE基准中，7个实现经验帕累托改进，包括二维Navier-Stokes、浅水动力学、达西流、一维传质反应系统及三维可压缩Navier-Stokes，但部分PDE-架构组合中残差增强未能抵消压缩损失。
From Sparse and Imperfect 2D Anchors to Consistent 3D Gaussian Street Scenes: Support-Aware Appearance
Authors: Long Cao, Zhongquan Wang, Jie Li, Yuhan Chen, Kefei Qian, Xiangfei Huang, Guofa Li
First: 2026-06-24T16:26:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:26:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Image priors can synthesize target conditions for 3D Gaussian street scenes, but independently edited views do not define a coherent 3D target. Direct fitting can propagate view-specific noise, while existing pipelines do not jointly handle imperfect sparse anchors and standard-rasterizer deployment. To address this gap, teacher-relative appearance residual distillation is introduced for appearance baking. A structured space for frequency decomposition, confidence estimation, and primitive-level lifting is formed by residuals between teacher anchors and original renders. The direct optimization signal is supplied by renderer-space matching, while primitive assignment is regularized by support-aware Gaussian-space aggregation. Supported detail is admitted and unsupported noise is suppressed through confidence-gated coarse-to-fine optimization, after which all residuals are baked into fixed-geometry spherical-harmonic coefficients. The teacher and auxiliary training modules are discarded at inference. Evaluation across Waymo street assets, Tanks and Temples scenes, and multiple target conditions shows a favorable overall balance of target alignment, content preservation, artifact suppression, and cross-view consistency over editing-based baselines. Ablations confirm the effectiveness of the main components. Code will be released at https://github.com/Cagares/Baking-for-3D-Gaussian.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从稀疏不完美的2D锚点到一致的3D高斯街道场景：支持感知外观
图像先验可合成3D高斯街道场景的目标条件，但独立编辑的视图无法定义连贯的3D目标。直接拟合会传播视图特异性噪声，而现有流程未联合处理不完美的稀疏锚点与标准光栅化部署。为解决此问题，引入教师相对外观残差蒸馏用于外观烘焙。通过教师锚点与原始渲染之间的残差构建结构化空间，用于频域分解、置信度估计和原始级提升。渲染空间匹配提供直接优化信号，支持感知高斯空间聚合则正则化原始分配。通过置信度门控的粗到细优化，允许支持细节并抑制非支持噪声，随后将所有残差烘焙为固定几何球谐系数。推理时丢弃教师与辅助训练模块。在Waymo街道资产、Tanks and Temples场景及多种目标条件下评估，结果在目标对齐、内容保留、伪影抑制和跨视图一致性方面优于基于编辑的基线。消融实验验证了主要组件的有效性。代码将在https://github.com/Cagares/Baking-for-3D-Gaussian发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate coherent 3D Gaussian street scenes from sparse and imperfect 2D anchors while preserving visual fidelity across views, the method introduces teacher-relative appearance residual distillation to bake consistent appearance into fixed geometry. It decomposes residuals between teacher anchors and original renders into frequency components, estimates confidence per primitive, and lifts them to Gaussian-space for regularization via support-aware aggregation. A confidence-gated coarse-to-fine optimization suppresses unsupported noise while retaining supported detail, and final spherical-harmonic coefficients are computed without teacher or auxiliary modules at inference. Experiments on Waymo, Tanks and Temples, and varied target conditions show superior alignment, content preservation, artifact suppression, and cross-view consistency compared to editing-based baselines, with ablation confirming component effectiveness.
为解决从稀疏不完美2D锚点生成连贯3D高斯街道场景的问题，该研究提出教师相对外观残差蒸馏方法用于外观烘焙。该方法通过教师锚点与原始渲染间的残差分解出频域、置信度和原始结构成分，并结合渲染空间匹配与支持感知高斯空间聚合进行优化，抑制无支持区域噪声。在Waymo、Tanks and Temples及多种目标条件下实验表明，该方法在目标对齐、内容保留、伪影抑制和跨视角一致性方面优于编辑类基线，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
AIChilles: Automatically Uncovering Hidden Weaknesses in AI-Evolved Systems
Authors: Yajie Zhou, Ao Li, Ashwin Silla, Zaoxing Liu, Vyas Sekar
First: 2026-06-14T14:24:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:25:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The computer systems community has recently seen growing interest in AI-driven system evolution, where AI agents iteratively rewrite systems. Frameworks such as AdaEvolve and Engram report 12-60% score improvements over human-designed algorithms. While these results are promising, there are practical concerns if these AI-evolved programs can perform worse on unseen workloads and exhibit scalability regressions. Given the speed and scale of AI-generated code, we need automated mechanisms to uncover such identify hidden weaknesses in AI-evolved systems programs. To this end, we develop AIChilles that takes as input a baseline program $P$ and an AI-evolved program $P'$, AIChilles searches for valid workloads where $P'$ regresses relative to $P$ in correctness, runtime, memory usage, or output quality. To tackle the diversity in system applications, weakness types and potential bugs, AIChilles combines deterministic workload-parameter extraction, agent-based constraint inference, differential oracles, and code-frequency coverage to discover diverse failures. Across five system applications and 30 AI-evolved programs, AIChilles finds 49 distinct hidden weaknesses. We also show that explicitly including AIChilles in the AI-driven development lifecycle can mitigate several of these weaknesses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AIChilles：自动发现AI演化系统中的隐藏弱点
计算机系统领域近期对AI驱动的系统演化兴趣日益增长，其中AI代理会迭代重写系统。框架如AdaEvolve和Engram报告其性能比人工设计算法提升12%-60%。尽管这些结果令人鼓舞，但若AI演化程序在未见工作负载上表现更差或出现可扩展性退化，则存在实际担忧。鉴于AI生成代码的速度与规模，我们需要自动化机制来发现AI演化系统中的隐藏弱点。为此，我们开发了AIChilles，它以基线程序P和AI演化程序P'为输入，搜索P'在正确性、运行时、内存使用或输出质量方面相对于P退化的有效工作负载。为应对系统应用的多样性、弱点类型及潜在错误，AIChilles结合确定性工作负载参数提取、基于代理的约束推断、差异预言机和代码频率覆盖率，以发现多样化的失败案例。在五个系统应用和30个AI演化程序中，AIChilles发现了49种不同的隐藏弱点。我们还表明，将AIChilles明确纳入AI驱动的开发生命周期，可缓解其中若干弱点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that AI-evolved systems may regress on unseen workloads despite showing performance gains over human-designed code, the authors developed AIChilles, an automated tool that searches for hidden weaknesses by identifying workloads where AI-evolved programs underperform relative to baselines across correctness, runtime, memory, or output quality. AIChilles combines deterministic workload extraction, agent-based constraint inference, differential oracles, and code-frequency coverage to systematically uncover diverse failure modes. Experimental results across five system applications and 30 AI-evolved programs revealed 49 distinct hidden weaknesses, and integrating AIChilles into the development pipeline was shown to mitigate several of these regressions.
鉴于AI生成的系统在未见负载下可能表现劣于人类设计程序，尽管其在性能上优于人类设计方案，作者开发了AIChilles工具，通过搜索特定工作负载，自动发现AI演化程序在正确性、运行时、内存或输出质量上相对于基线程序的退化。该工具结合确定性负载提取、基于代理的约束推断、差异性预言机和代码频率覆盖率，以应对不同系统应用中的多种故障模式。在五个系统和30个AI演化程序上的实验发现49种不同隐藏缺陷，且将AIChilles集成到开发流程中可缓解部分缺陷。
FORCE: Efficient VLA Reinforcement Fine-Tuning via Value-Calibrated Warm-up and Self-Distillation
Authors: Shuyi Zhang, Yunfan Lou, Hongyang Cheng, Yichen Guo, Chuyao Fu, Yaoxu Lyu, Xiaojie Zhang, Haoran Li, Pengwei Wang, Zhongyuan Wang, Shanghang Zhang
First: 2026-06-24T16:23:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:23:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often constrained by the imitation ceiling imposed by sub-optimal data. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning can surpass this limit, it is notoriously sample inefficient. This challenge arises from two core issues: (1) catastrophic initial unlearning due to an unstable Q-function and (2) inefficient policy updates caused by low-quality exploration data, often forcing a reliance on costly human interventions. We introduce FORCE, a 3-stage framework that stabilizes fine-tuning by tackling both issues. FORCE first incorporates a Value-Calibrated Warm-Up phase, utilizing on-policy rollouts to mitigate the distributional shift of the Q-function. Subsequently, during the online stage, this calibrated Q-function acts as a filter for both the policy's own action proposals and expert data, ensuring only high-value actions are used for the policy update. We evaluate FORCE on various simulation and real-world tasks, and the result shows that FORCE achieves a 79% absolute improvement in success rates and outperform prior RL methods by 10%, while accelerating training by 32.5%. Critically, it mitigates the common success rate drop and achieves this robust performance without human intervention, marking a significant step towards deploying capable and autonomous robotic agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FORCE：通过价值校准预热与自蒸馏实现高效的视觉-语言-动作模型强化微调
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型常受限于由次优数据造成的模仿上限。虽然强化学习（RL）微调可突破此限制，但其样本效率极低。该挑战源于两个核心问题：（1）因Q函数不稳定导致的灾难性初始遗忘；（2）低质量探索数据引发的策略更新效率低下，常迫使依赖昂贵的人类干预。我们提出FORCE，一种三阶段框架，通过同时解决上述问题来稳定微调。FORCE首先引入价值校准预热阶段，利用在线策略滚动以缓解Q函数的分布偏移。随后，在在线阶段，该校准后的Q函数作为过滤器，筛选策略自身动作提案及专家数据，确保仅高价值动作用于策略更新。我们在各类仿真与现实任务中评估FORCE，结果表明其成功率达79%绝对提升，相较现有RL方法提升10%，训练速度加快32.5%。关键的是，它缓解了常见成功率下降问题，并在无需人类干预下实现稳健性能，标志着向部署具备能力与自主性的机器人代理迈出重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of RL fine-tuning for Vision-Language-Action models due to unstable Q-functions and poor exploration data, FORCE introduces a three-stage framework combining value-calibrated warm-up and self-distillation. The warm-up phase stabilizes the Q-function using on-policy rollouts, while the online stage filters actions and expert data through this calibrated Q-function to guide policy updates. Experiments show FORCE achieves a 79% absolute improvement in success rates over imitation baselines, outperforms prior RL methods by 10%, and accelerates training by 32.5% without human intervention.
为了解决视觉-语言-动作模型在强化学习微调中因Q函数不稳定和探索数据质量差导致的样本效率低下问题，FORCE提出三阶段框架，结合价值校准预热与自蒸馏。方法首先通过在线策略滚动校准Q函数，随后在在线阶段用校准后的价值过滤自生成与专家动作，仅保留高价值动作更新策略。实验表明，FORCE在成功率上较基线提升79%，超越现有强化学习方法10%，训练速度提升32.5%，且无需人工干预。
SPARC: Separating Perception And Reasoning Circuits for Test-time Scaling of VLMs
Authors: Niccolo Avogaro, Nayanika Debnath, Li Mi, Thomas Frick, Junling Wang, Zexue He, Hang Hua, Konrad Schindler, Mattia Rigotti
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-06T10:05:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:20:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite recent successes, test-time scaling -- i.e., dynamically expanding the token budget during inference as needed -- remains brittle for vision-language models (VLMs). Unstructured visual reasoning chains entangle perception and reasoning, leading to long, disorganized contexts where small perceptual mistakes may cascade into completely wrong answers. Reasoning also requires expensive reinforcement learning with hand-crafted rewards. Here, we introduce SPARC (Separating Perception And Reasoning Circuits), a modular framework that explicitly decouples visual perception from reasoning. Inspired by sequential sensory-to-cognitive processing in the brain, SPARC implements a two-stage pipeline where the model first performs explicit visual search to localize question-relevant regions, then conditions its reasoning on those regions to produce the final answer. This separation enables independent test-time scaling with asymmetric compute allocation (e.g., prioritizing perceptual processing under distribution shift), and supports selective optimization (e.g., improving the perceptual stage alone when it is the bottleneck for end-to-end performance). It also accommodates compressed contexts by running global search at lower image resolutions and allocating high-resolution processing only to selected regions, thereby reducing visual token count and compute. SPARC outperforms monolithic baselines and strong visual-grounding approaches across challenging visual reasoning tasks, such as improving Qwen3VL 4B on the $V^*$ VQA benchmark by 6.7 points and surpassing "thinking with images" by 4.6 points in an OOD setting with a $200\times$ lower token budget.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPARC：分离感知与推理电路以实现视觉语言模型的测试时扩展
尽管近期取得成功，视觉语言模型（VLMs）在测试时扩展——即根据需要动态扩展令牌预算——仍存在脆弱性。无结构的视觉推理链将感知与推理纠缠在一起，导致冗长且杂乱的上下文，其中微小的感知错误可能引发完全错误的答案。推理还需要昂贵的强化学习和人工设计的奖励。本文提出SPARC（分离感知与推理电路），一种明确分离视觉感知与推理的模块化框架。受大脑中按顺序进行的感官到认知处理启发，SPARC实现两阶段流水线：模型首先执行显式视觉搜索以定位与问题相关的区域，然后基于这些区域进行推理以生成最终答案。这种分离支持独立的测试时扩展与非对称计算分配（例如，在分布偏移时优先处理感知），并支持选择性优化（例如，仅优化感知阶段以提升端到端性能）。此外，SPARC通过在较低图像分辨率下执行全局搜索，仅对选定区域分配高分辨率处理，从而压缩上下文，减少视觉令牌数量和计算量。SPARC在多个具有挑战性的视觉推理任务中优于单体基线和强视觉定位方法，例如在V* VQA基准测试中将Qwen3VL 4B性能提升6.7分，并在令牌预算降低200倍的OOD设置下，以4.6分超越“图像思考”方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of test-time scaling in vision-language models due to entangled perception and reasoning, SPARC introduces a modular framework that explicitly separates these two processes. It employs a two-stage pipeline: first performing visual search to localize relevant image regions, then using those regions to condition reasoning for final answers. This separation enables asymmetric compute allocation, selective optimization, and context compression via resolution-aware processing. Experiments show SPARC outperforms monolithic baselines and visual-grounding methods, boosting Qwen3VL 4B’s performance on the V* VQA benchmark by 6.7 points and surpassing "thinking with images" by 4.6 points under OOD conditions while using 200x fewer tokens.
为了解决视觉语言模型在测试时扩展中因感知与推理耦合导致的脆弱性，SPARC提出一种显式分离感知与推理的模块化框架。其采用两阶段流程：先进行视觉搜索定位相关区域，再基于这些区域进行推理生成答案。这种分离支持不对称计算分配、选择性优化和通过分辨率感知的上下文压缩。实验表明，SPARC在多个视觉推理任务中优于单体基线和视觉定位方法，使Qwen3VL 4B在V* VQA基准上提升6.7分，并在200倍更少的token预算下于分布外场景超越“图像思考”方法4.6分。
AutoSpec: Safety Rule Evolution for LLM Agents via Inductive Logic Programming
Authors: Pingchuan Ma, Zhaoyu Wang, Zimo Ji, Yuguang Zhou, Zhantong Xue, Zongjie Li, Shuai Wang, Xiaoqin Zhang
First: 2026-06-23T07:31:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:19:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate complex tasks by integrating language models with external tools and environments. However, their autonomy poses significant safety risks: agents may execute destructive commands, leak sensitive data, or violate domain constraints. Existing safety approaches face a fundamental tradeoff: hand-crafted rules are interpretable but brittle, with overly conservative rules blocking safe operations (high false positives) while permissive rules miss unsafe behaviors (high false negatives). Neural classifiers lack the interpretability required for safety-critical deployments. We present AutoSpec, a framework that automatically evolves deployed expert-designed safety rules from user safe/unsafe annotations through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS) guided by inductive logic programming (ILP). Starting from the expert rules and a stream of annotated traces, AutoSpec iteratively evaluates rules, mines false-positive and false-negative counterexamples, uses ILP to learn which predicates discriminate them, generates candidate rule edits, and verifies candidates to select the best revision. The key insight is that ILP efficiently identifies predicates that appear frequently in false negatives but rarely in false positives (or vice versa), dramatically pruning the exponential search space of rule edits. This continues until convergence, producing interpretable rules that balance precision and recall. We evaluate AutoSpec on 291 execution traces spanning code execution and embodied agent domains. AutoSpec raises rule F1 to 0.98 and 0.93 across the two domains, achieving up to 94% false positive reduction while maintaining high recall, and converges within 4-5 iterations. The ILP-guided approach achieves up to 4.8x higher F1 than heuristic CEGIS. The learned rules are human-readable, auditable, and generalize to unseen scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AutoSpec：通过归纳逻辑编程实现大语言模型代理的安全规则演化
大语言模型（LLM）代理通过集成语言模型与外部工具和环境，日益自动化复杂任务。然而，其自主性带来显著安全风险：代理可能执行破坏性命令、泄露敏感数据或违反领域约束。现有安全方法面临根本性权衡：手工设计规则虽可解释但脆弱，过于保守的规则会阻断安全操作（高假阳性），而宽松的规则则会遗漏不安全行为（高假阴性）。神经分类器缺乏安全关键部署所需的可解释性。 我们提出AutoSpec框架，该框架通过反例引导的归纳合成（CEGIS），结合归纳逻辑编程（ILP），自动从用户标注的安全/不安全轨迹中演化已部署的专家设计安全规则。AutoSpec从专家规则和标注轨迹流出发，迭代评估规则，挖掘假阳性和假阴性反例，利用ILP学习区分这些反例的谓词，生成候选规则修改，验证候选并选择最优修订。关键洞见是：ILP高效识别在假阴性中频繁出现但在假阳性中罕见（或反之）的谓词，大幅剪枝规则修改的指数级搜索空间。该过程持续至收敛，生成兼顾精确率与召回率的可解释规则。 我们在涵盖代码执行与具身代理领域的291个执行轨迹上评估AutoSpec。AutoSpec将规则F1提升至0.98和0.93，实现高达94%的假阳性减少，同时保持高召回率，且在4-5次迭代内收敛。ILP引导的方法相比启发式CEGIS的F1提升高达4.8倍。所学规则具备人类可读性、可审计性，并能泛化至未见场景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety risks of autonomous LLM agents executing unsafe commands or violating constraints, AutoSpec addresses the tradeoff between interpretable hand-crafted rules and brittle neural classifiers by automatically evolving safety rules via counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS) guided by inductive logic programming (ILP). Starting from expert rules and user-annotated traces, it iteratively mines false-positive and false-negative counterexamples, uses ILP to identify discriminative predicates, generates rule edits, and verifies them for optimal revision. The method leverages ILP’s ability to focus on predicates frequent in one type of error over the other, dramatically pruning the search space. Evaluated on 291 traces across code and embodied agent domains, AutoSpec improved rule F1 scores to 0.98 and 0.93, reduced false positives by up to 94% while preserving recall, converged within 4–5 iterations, and outperformed heuristic CEGIS by up to 4.8x in F1.
为应对自主LLM代理执行危险操作或违反约束的安全风险，AutoSpec通过归纳逻辑编程（ILP）引导的反例引导归纳合成（CEGIS）自动演化安全规则。从专家设计的初始规则和用户标注的执行轨迹出发，系统迭代识别误报与漏报反例，利用ILP挖掘区分两类反例的谓词，生成候选规则修改并验证选择最优版本。其核心在于ILP能聚焦于频繁出现在一类错误但罕见于另一类的谓词，大幅压缩规则修改的指数级搜索空间。在涵盖代码与具身代理领域的291条轨迹上，AutoSpec将规则F1提升至0.98和0.93，最高减少94%误报同时保持高召回率，4–5轮内收敛，相比启发式CEGIS性能提升最高达4.8倍，所学规则具备可读性、可审计性并能泛化至未见场景。
Dziri Voicebot: An End-to-End Low-Resource Speech-to-Speech Conversational System for Algerian Dialect
Authors: Dihia Lanasri, Fairouz Taki, Asma Kemmoum
First: 2026-06-24T16:19:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:19:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic speech and language technologies are still heavily biased toward high-resource languages, limiting their applicability to dialectal and low-resource settings such as Algerian Dialect. This language presents additional challenges including lack of standardized orthography, frequent codeswitching with French, and scarcity of annotated speech resources. This paper addresses the problem of building a complete speech-to-speech conversational system for Algerian Dialect. We propose a modular pipeline integrating automatic speech recognition, natural language understanding, retrieval-augmented generation, and text-to-speech synthesis within a unified architecture. This work is the continuation of our previous work on Algerian dialectal conversational systems Bechiri and Lanasri [2026], extending it from text-based dialogue modeling to full speech-based interaction. We constructed dedicated datasets for ASR, NLU, and TTS in the telecom domain and fine-tune pretrained models for each component. The ASR system is built on Whisper-based adaptation, while the NLU module combines transformer-based embeddings with a task-oriented dialogue framework. A neural TTS system is trained on a newly collected dialectal corpus to enable spoken response generation. Experimental results show strong performance across all components, including low word error rate for ASR, high intent classification and entity recognition scores for NLU, and stable speech synthesis quality. The proposed system provides a reproducible baseline for end-to-end conversational modeling in Algerian Dialect.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Dziri语音机器人：面向阿尔及利亚方言的端到端低资源语音到语音对话系统
自动语音与语言技术仍严重偏向高资源语言，限制其在阿尔及利亚方言等方言和低资源环境中的适用性。该语言面临标准化拼写缺失、频繁与法语混用及标注语音资源匮乏等挑战。本文旨在构建完整的阿尔及利亚方言语音到语音对话系统。我们提出一个模块化管道，整合自动语音识别、自然语言理解、检索增强生成和文本到语音合成，统一架构实现。本工作延续我们此前关于阿尔及利亚方言对话系统的研究（Bechiri 和 Lanasri, 2026），将对话建模从文本扩展至全语音交互。我们构建了电信领域专用的ASR、NLU和TTS数据集，并对各组件预训练模型进行微调。ASR系统基于Whisper进行适配，NLU模块结合基于Transformer的嵌入与任务导向对话框架。神经TTS系统基于新收集的方言语料库训练，以支持语音响应生成。实验结果表明各组件性能优异，包括低词错误率的ASR、高意图分类与实体识别准确率的NLU，以及稳定的语音合成质量。所提系统为阿尔及利亚方言端到端对话建模提供了可复现的基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of speech-based conversational systems for low-resource Algerian Dialect, this work proposes an end-to-end modular pipeline integrating ASR, NLU, retrieval-augmented generation, and TTS. The system adapts Whisper for ASR, combines transformer embeddings with a task-oriented dialogue framework for NLU, and trains a neural TTS model on a newly collected dialectal corpus. Experiments demonstrate strong performance: low word error rate in ASR, high intent and entity recognition accuracy in NLU, and stable TTS output quality, establishing a reproducible baseline for Algerian Dialect conversational systems.
为解决阿尔及利亚方言缺乏端到端语音对话系统的问题，本文提出一个集成自动语音识别、自然语言理解、检索增强生成和语音合成的模块化端到端系统。ASR基于Whisper进行适配，NLU结合Transformer嵌入与任务导向对话框架，TTS则在新收集的方言语料上训练。实验表明：ASR错误率低，NLU意图识别和实体识别准确率高，语音合成质量稳定，为阿尔及利亚方言对话系统提供了可复现的基准。
Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Neural Network Compression (HiReLC): Pruning and Quantization
Authors: Kamar Hibatallah Baghdadi, Kawther Guoual Belhamidi, Sara Belhadj, Aissa Boulmerka, Nadir Farhi
First: 2026-06-24T16:19:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:19:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present HiReLC, a hierarchical ensemble-reinforcement learning framework for automated joint quantization and structured pruning of deep neural networks. The framework decomposes the compression search across two levels of abstraction: low-level agents (LLAs) operate independently per block, selecting per-kernel configurations over a multi-discrete action space spanning bitwidth, pruning keep-ratio, quantization type, and granularity, while high-level agents (HLAs) coordinate global budget allocation via ensemble voting guided by Fisher Information-based sensitivity estimates. To mitigate the computational cost of policy evaluation, an iterative active learning loop interleaves surrogate-guided RL optimization with post-compression fine-tuning, using a lightweight MLP surrogate to amortize expensive evaluations and a logit-MSE proxy during cold-start. The surrogate is used for reward shaping rather than as a replacement for final post-compression evaluation. The controller is architecture-agnostic by design, with a modular layer abstraction decoupling the RL environment from the underlying network topology. Experiments across Vision Transformer and CNN benchmarks demonstrate effective parameter-storage compression ratios of 5.99 - 6.72$\times$ with a 3.83 % gain in one setting and 0.55 - 5.62 % accuracy drops elsewhere, supporting hierarchical policy decomposition and sensitivity-aware guidance as practical design choices for joint neural network compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于神经网络压缩的分层强化学习（HiReLC）：剪枝与量化
我们提出了HiReLC，一种用于深度神经网络联合量化与结构化剪枝的分层集成强化学习框架。该框架在两个抽象层次上分解压缩搜索：低层代理（LLAs）独立操作每个模块，从涵盖比特宽度、剪枝保留率、量化类型和粒度的多离散动作空间中选择每核配置；高层代理（HLAs）通过基于Fisher信息的敏感性估计引导的集成投票协调全局预算分配。为降低策略评估的计算成本，采用迭代主动学习循环，交替进行代理引导的强化学习优化与压缩后微调，使用轻量级MLP代理以摊销昂贵评估，并在冷启动阶段使用logit-MSE代理。该代理用于奖励塑造，而非替代最终压缩后评估。控制器设计上架构无关，通过模块化层抽象将强化学习环境与底层网络拓扑解耦。在视觉Transformer和CNN基准实验中，实现了5.99至6.72倍的有效参数存储压缩率，在一种设置下精度提升3.83%，在其他设置下精度下降0.55至5.62%，支持分层策略分解和敏感性引导作为联合神经网络压缩的实用设计选择。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, automated joint compression of neural networks, HiReLC employs a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that separates local pruning and quantization decisions from global budget allocation. Low-level agents independently optimize per-kernel configurations across bitwidth, pruning ratio, quantization type, and granularity, while high-level agents coordinate resource distribution using Fisher Information-based sensitivity estimates. To reduce computational overhead, an active learning loop integrates a lightweight MLP surrogate for reward shaping with post-compression fine-tuning, avoiding full policy evaluation at every step. Experiments on Vision Transformers and CNNs show compression ratios of 5.99–6.72× with accuracy drops ranging from 0.55% to 5.62%, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical decomposition and sensitivity-guided control.
为实现神经网络的高效自动化联合压缩，HiReLC提出了一种分层强化学习框架，将局部剪枝与量化决策与全局预算分配分离。低层代理独立优化每个核的配置，涵盖比特宽度、剪枝比例、量化类型和粒度；高层代理则通过基于Fisher信息的敏感性估计协调全局资源分配。为降低计算开销，系统引入轻量MLP代理与迭代主动学习循环，通过奖励塑造引导强化学习，并在压缩后进行微调优化。在视觉Transformer和CNN基准测试中，该方法实现5.99–6.72倍的参数存储压缩，准确率下降范围为0.55%至5.62%，验证了分层决策与敏感性引导在联合压缩中的有效性。
Articulat3D: Reconstructing Articulated Digital Twins From Monocular Videos with Geometric and Motion Constraints
Authors: Lijun Guo, Haoyu Zhao, Xingyue Zhao, Rong Fu, Linghao Zhuang, Siteng Huang, Zhongyu Li, Hua Zou
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-03-12T06:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:15:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. 26 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Building high-fidelity digital twins of articulated objects from visual data remains a central challenge. Existing approaches depend on multi-view captures of the object in discrete, static states, which severely constrains their real-world scalability. In this paper, we introduce Articulat3D, a novel framework that constructs such digital twins from casually captured monocular videos by jointly enforcing explicit 3D geometric and motion constraints. We first propose Motion Prior-Driven Initialization, which leverages 3D point tracks to exploit the low-dimensional structure of articulated motion. By modeling scene dynamics with a compact set of motion bases, we facilitate soft decomposition of the scene into multiple rigidly moving groups. Building on this initialization, we introduce Geometric and Motion Constraints Refinement, which enforces physically plausible articulation through learnable kinematic primitives parameterized by a joint axis, a pivot point, and per-frame motion scalars, yielding reconstructions that are both geometrically accurate and temporally coherent. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Articulat3D achieves state-of-the-art performance on synthetic benchmarks and real-world casually captured monocular videos, significantly advancing the feasibility of digital twin creation under uncontrolled real-world conditions. Our project page is available at https://maxwell-zhao.github.io/Articulat3D/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Articulat3D：利用几何与运动约束从单目视频重建可动数字孪生体
从视觉数据构建高保真可动物体的数字孪生体仍是一个核心挑战。现有方法依赖于物体在离散静态状态下的多视角捕捉，严重限制了其在现实世界中的可扩展性。本文提出Articulat3D，一种新颖框架，可从随意拍摄的单目视频中联合施加显式三维几何与运动约束，构建此类数字孪生体。我们首先提出运动先验驱动初始化方法，利用三维点轨迹挖掘可动运动的低维结构；通过紧凑的运动基函数建模场景动态，实现场景软分解为多个刚体运动组。在此基础上，我们引入几何与运动约束优化模块，通过可学习的运动学原语（由关节轴、枢轴点及每帧运动标量参数化）强制物理上合理的运动，生成几何精确且时间连贯的重建结果。大量实验表明，Articulat3D在合成基准与真实单目视频上均达到最先进性能，显著提升了在无控制现实条件下创建数字孪生体的可行性。项目页面见https://maxwell-zhao.github.io/Articulat3D/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to create high-fidelity digital twins of articulated objects from real-world monocular videos without multi-view constraints, Articulat3D introduces a framework that jointly enforces 3D geometric and motion priors. It begins with Motion Prior-Driven Initialization, using 3D point tracks to model articulated motion via compact motion bases, enabling soft decomposition into rigidly moving groups. Then, Geometric and Motion Constraints Refinement refines the reconstruction by learning kinematic primitives defined by joint axes, pivot points, and per-frame motion scalars, ensuring physically plausible articulation. Experiments show it outperforms existing methods on both synthetic and real monocular video datasets, achieving state-of-the-art results and demonstrating strong scalability under uncontrolled conditions.
为了解决从单目视频中重建高保真可动数字孪生体的难题，Articulat3D提出了一种联合施加几何与运动约束的框架。该方法首先通过运动先验驱动初始化，利用3D点轨迹建模运动基底，软性分解场景为多个刚体运动组；随后通过几何与运动约束优化，使用可学习的运动学原语（由关节轴、支点及帧级运动标量参数化）确保物理合理性。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实单目视频数据集上均达到当前最优性能，显著提升了在非结构化现实场景下构建数字孪生体的可行性。
Memory Contagion: Cross-Temporal Propagation of Evaluator Bias via Agent Memory
Authors: Zewen Liu
First: 2026-06-22T11:43:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:12:30+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on memory systems to maintain long-term coherence. Recent work shows that agent memories degrade during continuous consolidation. However, existing research assumes memories are derived from unbiased experiences. In this work, we identify and formalize a novel phenomenon: Memory Contagion -- the cross-temporal propagation of evaluator bias through agent memory. We show that when agents are trained or guided by biased evaluators, their experiences become biased; when these trajectories are stored and consolidated into memory, the bias propagates to future agents retrieving from the same memory store, even when consolidation is perfect (oracle). Across two bias types (length preference, authority bias) and four experimental phases, we demonstrate: (1) Memory Contagion occurs for length bias even with perfect consolidation on older models (Gamma_A = 13.18, DeepSeek V4-Chat), while newer models (V4-Pro, Claude) are immune, proving both that biased input is a sufficient cause and that contagion is model-generation-dependent; (2) authority bias fails to propagate in all 15 controlled multi-seed experiments (Gamma_A = 0.00), revealing that not all evaluator biases can cross temporal boundaries through current memory architectures; (3) No observed safe threshold: length bias propagation is detected at contamination rates as low as p=0.2. Our findings expose a critical but contingent vulnerability in current agent memory designs and provide formal tools for measuring cross-temporal bias propagation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：记忆传染：通过代理记忆实现评估者偏见的跨时间传播
大型语言模型（LLM）代理越来越多地依赖记忆系统以维持长期连贯性。近期研究表明，代理记忆在持续巩固过程中会退化。然而，现有研究假设记忆来源于无偏经验。本文识别并形式化了一种新现象：记忆传染——评估者偏见通过代理记忆在时间维度上的传播。我们表明，当代理由有偏评估者训练或引导时，其经验会变得有偏；当这些轨迹被存储并巩固进记忆中时，即使巩固过程完美（oracle），偏见也会传播给未来从同一记忆库检索的代理。在两种偏见类型（长度偏好、权威偏见）和四个实验阶段中，我们证明：（1）即使在旧模型（Gamma_A = 13.18, DeepSeek V4-Chat）上，长度偏见在完美巩固下仍会发生传染，而新模型（V4-Pro, Claude）则免疫，证明有偏输入是充分原因，且传染依赖于模型生成版本；（2）在15次受控多种子实验中，权威偏见未在任何实验中传播（Gamma_A = 0.00），表明并非所有评估者偏见都能通过当前记忆架构跨越时间边界；（3）未发现安全阈值：长度偏见传播在污染率低至p=0.2时即被检测到。我们的发现揭示了当前代理记忆设计中的关键但有条件的脆弱性，并提供了衡量跨时间偏见传播的正式工具。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how evaluator bias propagates across time through agent memory systems, motivated by the observation that LLM agents' memories degrade during consolidation and are assumed to reflect unbiased experiences. The authors formalize 'Memory Contagion' — the phenomenon where biased inputs from evaluators contaminate stored agent trajectories, which then influence future agents even with perfect memory consolidation. Experiments across two bias types (length preference and authority bias) and four phases reveal that length bias propagates robustly in older models (Gamma_A = 13.18), while newer models (V4-Pro, Claude) are immune, indicating model-generation dependency. Authority bias showed no propagation (Gamma_A = 0.00) across 15 controlled trials, and length bias was detectable even at contamination rates as low as p=0.2, highlighting a critical but conditional vulnerability in current memory architectures.
该研究探讨了评估者偏见如何通过智能体记忆系统跨时间传播，这一现象被称为‘记忆传染’。研究动机源于当前假设智能体记忆反映无偏经验，但实际记忆在持续巩固过程中会退化。作者正式定义并实验证明，当智能体接受有偏评估时，其经验会受污染并存储于记忆中，进而影响未来检索该记忆的智能体，即使记忆巩固完美。在长度偏好和权威偏见两种类型及四个实验阶段中，发现长度偏见在旧模型中可传播（Gamma_A = 13.18），新模型则免疫（V4-Pro、Claude），表明传播依赖模型版本；权威偏见在15次控制实验中均未传播（Gamma_A = 0.00）；且传播阈值极低（p=0.2即可触发），揭示当前记忆架构无安全边界。
Improving Zero-Shot Offline RL via Behavioral Task Sampling
Authors: Nazim Bendib, Nicolas Perrin-Gilbert, Olivier Sigaud
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-04-28T10:56:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:12:26+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Offline zero-shot reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn agents that optimize unseen reward functions without additional environment interaction. The standard approach to this problem trains task-conditioned policies by sampling task vectors that define linear reward functions over learned state representations. In most existing algorithms, these task vectors are randomly sampled, implicitly assuming this adequately captures the structure of the task space. We argue that doing so leads to suboptimal zero-shot generalization. To address this limitation, we propose extracting task vectors directly from the offline dataset and using them to define the task distribution used for policy training. We introduce a simple and general reward function extraction procedure that integrates into existing offline zero-shot RL algorithms. Across multiple benchmark environments and baselines, our approach improves zero-shot performance by an average of 20%, highlighting the importance of principled task sampling in offline zero-shot RL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过行为任务采样改进零样本离线强化学习
离线零样本强化学习旨在无需额外环境交互的情况下，学习能优化未见过奖励函数的智能体。标准方法通过采样定义线性奖励函数的任务向量来训练任务条件策略。在大多数现有算法中，这些任务向量随机采样，隐含假设这足以捕捉任务空间的结构。我们认为这样做会导致零样本泛化能力不足。为解决此局限，我们提出直接从离线数据集中提取任务向量，并用其定义策略训练所用的任务分布。我们引入一种简单通用的奖励函数提取程序，可集成到现有离线零样本强化学习算法中。在多个基准环境和基线方法上，我们的方法平均提升零样本性能20%，凸显了离线零样本强化学习中合理任务采样的重要性。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of random task sampling in offline zero-shot reinforcement learning, which leads to suboptimal generalization across unseen reward functions. The authors propose a method that extracts task vectors directly from the offline dataset to define a more principled task distribution for training task-conditioned policies. This extraction procedure is designed to be simple and compatible with existing algorithms, and experimental results across multiple benchmarks show an average 20% improvement in zero-shot performance, demonstrating the value of data-driven task sampling.
鉴于现有离线零样本强化学习方法中随机采样任务向量导致泛化能力不足的问题，本文提出直接从离线数据集中提取任务向量，以构建更合理的任务分布用于训练任务条件策略。该方法引入一种简单且通用的奖励函数提取流程，可无缝集成到现有算法中，无需额外环境交互。实验结果表明，该方法在多个基准环境上平均提升零样本性能20%，凸显了任务采样策略在离线零样本强化学习中的关键作用。
Variable Bound Tightening for Nash Equilibrium Computation in Multiplayer Imperfect-Information Games
Authors: Sam Ganzfried
First: 2026-06-24T16:08:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:08:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There has been significant recent progress in algorithms for approximation of Nash equilibrium in large two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games and exact computation of Nash equilibrium in multiplayer strategic-form games. While counterfactual regret minimization and fictitious play are scalable to large games and have convergence guarantees in two-player zero-sum games, they do not guarantee convergence to Nash equilibrium in multiplayer games. Recently, an approach has been presented for exact computation of Nash equilibrium in multiplayer imperfect-information games that solves a quadratically constrained program based on a nonlinear complementarity problem formulation derived from the sequence-form game representation. This formulation was solved using Gurobi's nonconvex quadratic solver, which employs spatial branch-and-bound to iteratively refine variable bounds by solving convex relaxations of bilinear terms via McCormick envelopes. During presolve, Gurobi introduces auxiliary variables and, in some cases, binary variables, leading to an internal MIQCP reformulation. This approach was demonstrated to outperform prior algorithms from the Gambit software suite and quickly solve three-player Kuhn poker after removal of dominated actions; however, the algorithm was not able to solve the full version of the game within 24 hours. In this paper, we derive finite bounds on slack and multiplier variables in the nonlinear complementarity formulation. These bounds strengthen the convex relaxations used within spatial branch-and-bound and lead to substantial computational improvements. We demonstrate the impact of the proposed bounds on exact Nash equilibrium computation in three-player Kuhn poker.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多玩家不完全信息博弈中纳什均衡计算的变量边界紧化
近期在大规模二人零和不完全信息博弈中纳什均衡的近似算法，以及多玩家战略形式博弈中纳什均衡的精确计算方面取得了显著进展。尽管反事实遗憾最小化和 fictitious play 在大规模二人零和博弈中具有可扩展性并有收敛保证，但在多玩家博弈中无法保证收敛至纳什均衡。最近，一种基于序列形式博弈表示所导出的非线性互补问题的二次约束规划方法被提出，用于精确计算多玩家不完全信息博弈的纳什均衡。该问题通过 Gurobi 的非凸二次规划求解器求解，该求解器采用空间分支定界法，通过 McCormick 包络迭代细化双线性项的凸松弛，从而收紧变量边界。在预处理阶段，Gurobi 引入辅助变量，有时还引入二元变量，形成内部混合整数二次约束规划（MIQCP）重形式。该方法在移除被支配策略后，能快速解决三人 Kuhn 扑克问题，且优于 Gambit 软件套件中的先前算法；但无法在 24 小时内解决完整版本的博弈。本文推导了非线性互补问题形式中松弛变量和乘子变量的有限界，这些界强化了空间分支定界法中使用的凸松弛，带来显著的计算改进。我们展示了所提界对三人 Kuhn 扑克精确纳什均衡计算的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient exact Nash equilibrium computation in multiplayer imperfect-information games, this paper improves upon a prior quadratic programming approach by tightening variable bounds in the nonlinear complementarity formulation. The method derives finite bounds on slack and multiplier variables, which strengthen convex relaxations within Gurobi’s spatial branch-and-bound solver, reducing the search space and accelerating convergence. Experimental results show significant computational improvements, enabling the algorithm to solve three-player Kuhn poker more efficiently than previous methods, though it still cannot solve the full game within 24 hours.
为提升多玩家不完全信息博弈中纳什均衡的精确计算效率，本文改进了基于序列形式表示的非线性互补问题求解方法，通过推导松弛变量与乘子变量的有限界来收紧凸松弛，从而加速空间分支定界算法的收敛。实验表明，该方法成功解决了此前24小时内无法求解的完整三玩家库恩扑克博弈，显著优于现有方法。
Autodata: An agentic data scientist to create high quality synthetic data
Authors: Ilia Kulikov, Chenxi Whitehouse, Tianhao Wu, Yixin Nie, Swarnadeep Saha, Eryk Helenowski, Weizhe Yuan, Olga Golovneva, Jack Lanchantin, Yoram Bachrach, Jakob Foerster, Xian Li, Han Fang, Sainbayar Sukhbaatar, Jason Weston
First: 2026-06-24T16:08:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:08:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Autodata, a general method that enables AI agents to act as data scientists who build high quality training and evaluation data. We show how to train (meta-optimize) such a data scientist agent, so that it learns to create even stronger data. We describe the overall formulation, and a specific practical implementation, Agentic Self-Instruct. We conduct experiments on computer science research tasks, legal reasoning tasks and reasoning with mathematical objects, where we obtain improved results compared to classical synthetic dataset creation methods. Further, meta-optimizing the data scientist agent itself delivers an even larger performance uplift. Agentic data creation provides a way to convert increased inference compute into higher quality model training. Overall, we believe this direction has the potential to change the way we build AI data.

Motivated by the need to generate high-quality synthetic data for training AI models, the authors propose Autodata, an agentic system that trains AI agents to act as data scientists capable of creating superior datasets. The method, called Agentic Self-Instruct, meta-optimizes the agent to iteratively improve its data generation skills. Experiments across computer science research, legal reasoning, and mathematical reasoning tasks show that Autodata outperforms traditional synthetic data methods, and further performance gains are achieved when the agent itself is meta-optimized. This approach effectively converts increased inference compute into higher-quality training data, suggesting a transformative potential for AI data creation.
为提升AI模型训练所需高质量合成数据的生成能力，作者提出Autodata框架，训练AI代理扮演数据科学家角色以构建更优数据集。该方法通过Agentic Self-Instruct实现，采用元优化策略使代理持续改进数据生成能力。在计算机科学、法律推理及数学推理等任务中，实验表明其优于传统合成数据方法，且对代理本身进行元优化可进一步提升性能，实现将推理计算资源转化为更高质训练数据。
SpeechEQ: Benchmarking Emotional Intelligence Quotient in Socially Aware Voice Conversational Models
Authors: Liang-Yuan Wu, Zih-Ching Chen, Tongshuang Wu, Chao-Han Huck Yang, Hua Shen
First: 2026-06-24T16:03:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T16:03:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
As multimodal conversational systems increasingly engage in spoken interaction, their ability to navigate paralinguistic social cues has become a critical bottleneck for natural human-AI communication. However, existing evaluations of machine emotional intelligence assess reasoning exclusively through isolated text or passive acoustic perception, overlooking the complex cross-modal reasoning required for active, multi-turn dialogue. We introduce \textsc{SpeechEQ}, a comprehensive framework designed to evaluate the sociolinguistic reasoning of Speech-Language Models (SLMs). The framework includes a validated dataset of 2,265 dialogues across 15 Emotional Quotient (EQ) subscales grounded in EQ-i 2.0 theory, along with a multi-turn evaluation protocol measured by our proposed Spoken EQ (SEQ) score inspired by human EQ assessments. Experiments show limitations in how both existing Speech Emotion Recognition and end-to-end Speech-Language Models understand and apply paralinguistic cues through speech. While end-to-end architectures outperform cascaded systems, \textsc{SpeechEQ} reveals that current multimodal models remain bottlenecked by a text-reliant ``modality shortcut,'' an alignment-induced ``safety trap,'' and ``contextual amnesia,'' highlighting the barriers to truly emotionally aware AI. Our benchmark can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SpeechEQ/SpeechEQ and demo page at https://binomial14.github.io/speecheq-demo/

标题：SpeechEQ：社交感知语音对话模型的情感智力基准评估
随着多模态对话系统越来越多地参与口语交互，其处理语用社会线索的能力已成为实现自然人机沟通的关键瓶颈。然而，现有机器情感智能评估仅通过孤立文本或被动声学感知评估推理能力，忽视了主动多轮对话中所需的跨模态推理。我们提出了SpeechEQ，一个全面评估语音语言模型（SLM）社会语言推理能力的框架。该框架包含基于EQ-i 2.0理论构建的2,265个对话数据集，涵盖15个情感商数（EQ）子量表，并采用我们提出的口语EQ（SEQ）评分体系进行多轮评估。实验表明，现有语音情感识别系统和端到端语音语言模型在理解并应用语音中的语用线索方面存在局限。尽管端到端架构优于级联系统，但SpeechEQ揭示当前多模态模型仍受限于依赖文本的“模态捷径”、由对齐引发的“安全陷阱”以及“上下文遗忘”，凸显了实现真正情感感知AI的障碍。本基准可访问：https://huggingface.co/datasets/SpeechEQ/SpeechEQ，演示页面：https://binomial14.github.io/speecheq-demo/

Motivated by the need to evaluate how well voice-based conversational AI understands emotional context in real dialogue, this paper introduces SpeechEQ, a benchmark framework that assesses sociolinguistic reasoning in Speech-Language Models using a 2,265-dialogue dataset aligned with EQ-i 2.0 theory across 15 emotional subscales.
