Experimental results show that both traditional Speech Emotion Recognition systems and end-to-end SLMs struggle with paralinguistic cue integration, revealing persistent bottlenecks such as modality shortcuts, safety traps from alignment, and contextual amnesia, indicating that current models lack true emotional awareness in dynamic conversations.
为评估语音对话AI在真实对话中处理情感社交线索的能力，SpeechEQ提出一套基准框架，使用2265个跨15个EQ子量表（基于EQ-i 2.0理论）的多轮对话数据，结合创新的Spoken EQ（SEQ）评分体系衡量社会语言推理能力。实验表明，即使先进的端到端模型也因过度依赖文本、对齐引发的安全限制及语境遗忘等问题而难以真正理解语音中的情感线索，揭示当前多模态模型在实现情感智能方面的关键瓶颈。
Taxonomy-aware deep learning for hierarchical marine species classification in underwater imagery
Authors: Dan Zimmerman, Dimitris A. Pados, George Sklivanitis
Venue: Proc. SPIE 14030 Machine Learning from Challenging Data 2026, 140300C (2026)
First: 2026-06-24T15:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:59:48+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Presented at SPIE Defense + Security 2026 (Machine Learning from Challenging Data conference), National Harbor, MD, April 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated classification of marine species from underwater imagery is essential for scalable ocean biodiversity monitoring and conservation policy. Existing approaches struggle with severe domain shift across collection platforms, fine-grained visual similarity between closely related species, and uneven annotation granularity, where many specimens can only be identified to genus or a coarser taxonomic rank. We present a taxonomy-aware deep learning framework that aligns both the training loss and the inference rule with the hierarchical structure of biological classification, combining a taxonomy-weighted loss, minimum-risk Bayesian inference, multi-scale feature encoding, and independent per-rank classification heads. Evaluated on the FathomNet 2025 dataset1 (79 marine classes across seven taxonomic ranks), the system achieves a mean taxonomic distance of 1.581, within 3% of the 1st-place solution (1.535), with the largest gains from metric-aligned inference and simple, decoupled components that generalize better than learned dependencies under distribution shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向分类学的深度学习在水下图像中分层海洋物种分类中的应用
从水下图像自动分类海洋物种，对可扩展的海洋生物多样性监测和保护政策至关重要。现有方法在跨采集平台的严重领域偏移、近缘物种间的精细视觉相似性以及标注粒度不均（许多标本仅能识别至属或更粗粒度分类阶元）方面存在困难。我们提出了一种面向分类学的深度学习框架，其训练损失和推理规则均与生物分类的层级结构对齐，结合分类学加权损失、最小风险贝叶斯推理、多尺度特征编码及独立的每阶分类头。在FathomNet 2025数据集（涵盖七个分类阶元的79个海洋类）上评估，系统达到平均分类距离1.581，接近第一名方案（1.535）的3%以内，主要收益来自与度量对齐的推理及简单解耦组件，在分布偏移下比学习依赖关系更具泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
To address challenges in automated marine species classification from underwater imagery—including domain shift, fine-grained visual similarity, and inconsistent annotation granularity—the paper proposes a taxonomy-aware deep learning framework that aligns both training loss and inference with biological taxonomy. The method employs a taxonomy-weighted loss, minimum-risk Bayesian inference, multi-scale feature encoding, and separate classification heads per taxonomic rank. Evaluated on FathomNet 2025 with 79 marine classes across seven ranks, the system achieved a mean taxonomic distance of 1.581, within 3% of the top-performing solution, demonstrating that metric-aligned inference and decoupled components outperform learned dependencies under distribution shift.
为解决水下图像中海洋物种自动分类面临的领域偏移、细粒度视觉相似性及标注粒度不一致等问题，本文提出一种与分类学结构对齐的深度学习框架，其训练损失和推理规则均结合生物分类层级。方法包括分类学加权损失、最小风险贝叶斯推理、多尺度特征编码及按分类阶次独立的分类头。在包含79个类别的FathomNet 2025数据集上，系统达到1.581的平均分类学距离，接近第一名方案（1.535），表明对齐度量的推理与解耦组件在分布偏移下优于学习依赖关系。
Weave of Formal Thought
Authors: Alexandre Bouayad
First: 2026-06-24T15:58:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:58:11+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/alexbouayad/formal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) attain remarkable surface fluency on code, yet they neither formally guarantee the syntactic validity of their output nor leverage the hierarchical structure defining the target language. While existing constrained-decoding frameworks address the former, they operate under rigid assumptions that preclude critical lexical mechanisms -- including context-sensitive lexing, maximal-munch tokenization, and keyword extraction -- and only approximate vocabulary masking, sacrificing completeness. For the latter, code LLMs typically inject grammatical structure via predetermined policies rather than learning which structural information to expose. In this work, we introduce Weave of Formal Thought (WoFT), a paradigm uniting rigorous syntactic validation with learned structural representations. First, we present a formal engine and constrained decoder that is sound and complete with respect to the full Tree-sitter specification. By augmenting generalized LR (GLR) parsing with a speculative-lexing construction that maintains concurrent lexer-state hypotheses synchronized with a GLR graph-structured stack, our decoder admits every subword token extending to a valid program prefix and rejects all others. Second, we present a latent-variable fine-tuning method training the language model to interleave non-terminal grammar symbols directly into generation. Utilizing the reweighted wake-sleep (RWS) algorithm to optimize the importance-weighted evidence lower bound (IW-ELBO) of the surface text, the model learns to selectively retain formal derivations as an adaptive structural scratchpad. For Python, fine-tuning StarCoder2-3B with our RWS objective reduces per-token cross-entropy by 14.3% relative to a text-only SFT baseline, demonstrating that discretionary latent syntax recovers critical structural information that flat autoregressive training discards.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式思维的编织
大型语言模型（LLMs）在代码生成方面表现出色，但它们既不能形式化保证输出的语法正确性，也不能利用目标语言的层次结构。现有约束解码框架虽能处理前者，却依赖严格假设，排除了上下文敏感词法分析、最大 munch 词法分析和关键词提取等关键词法机制，且仅近似词汇掩码，牺牲了完备性。对于后者，代码 LLM 通常通过预设策略注入语法结构，而非学习应暴露哪些结构信息。本文提出形式思维编织（WoFT），一种将严格语法验证与学习结构表示相结合的范式。首先，我们提出一个形式引擎和约束解码器，其相对于完整 Tree-sitter 规范是声效完备的。通过将通用 LR（GLR）解析与推测词法构造相结合，该解码器在 GLR 图结构栈上同步维护多个词法状态假设，允许所有可扩展为有效程序前缀的子词标记，拒绝其余标记。其次，我们提出一种潜在变量微调方法，训练语言模型在生成过程中直接插入非终结符语法符号。利用重加权醒睡（RWS）算法优化表面文本的重要性加权证据下界（IW-ELBO），模型学会选择性保留形式推导作为自适应结构草稿。以 Python 为例，使用我们的 RWS 目标对 StarCoder2-3B 进行微调，相较纯文本 SFT 基线，每 token 交叉熵降低 14.3%，证明了可选潜在语法能恢复扁平自回归训练所丢弃的关键结构信息。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLMs to produce syntactically valid code while preserving hierarchical language structure, this work introduces Weave of Formal Thought (WoFT), a framework that combines formal parsing with learned structural representations. It employs a sound and complete decoder based on generalized LR parsing augmented with speculative lexing to ensure every generated token extends to a valid program prefix. Additionally, it uses a latent-variable fine-tuning method with reweighted wake-sleep optimization to train models to interleave grammar symbols during generation, enabling selective retention of formal derivations. Experiments on Python show that fine-tuning StarCoder2-3B with this approach reduces per-token cross-entropy by 14.3% compared to a text-only supervised fine-tuning baseline, indicating that learned structural representations improve code generation quality.
为解决大语言模型在生成代码时缺乏语法正确性保障且无法有效利用语言层级结构的问题，本文提出Weave of Formal Thought（WoFT）框架，融合形式化解析与学习结构表示。该框架采用基于广义LR解析的声完备解码器，结合推测式词法分析，确保每个子词均能扩展为合法程序前缀；同时引入潜变量微调方法，通过重加权醒睡算法优化表面文本的证据下界，使模型在生成时嵌入非终结符语法符号，形成动态结构草稿。在Python任务上，对StarCoder2-3B进行该方法微调后，每token交叉熵较纯文本监督微调基线降低14.3%，证明学习结构信息可显著提升代码质量，超越单纯表面自回归训练。
Why Pool When You Can Flow? Active Learning with GFlowNets
Authors: Renfei Zhang, Mohit Pandey, Artem Cherkasov, Martin Ester
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2025-08-31T05:15:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:56:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2025 Workshop on AI Virtual Cells and Instruments: A New Era in Drug Discovery and Development (AI4D3 2025), San Diego, California, USA. 6 pages; 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The scalability of pool-based active learning is limited by the computational cost of evaluating large unlabeled datasets, a challenge that is particularly acute in virtual screening for drug discovery. While active learning strategies such as Bayesian Active Learning by Disagreement (BALD) prioritize informative samples, it remains computationally intensive when scaled to libraries containing billions samples. In this work, we introduce BALD-GFlowNet, a generative active learning framework that circumvents this issue. Our method leverages Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) to directly sample objects in proportion to the BALD reward. By replacing traditional pool-based acquisition with generative sampling, BALD-GFlowNet achieves scalability that is independent of the size of the unlabeled pool. In our virtual screening experiment, we show that BALD-GFlowNet achieves a performance comparable to that of standard BALD baseline while generating more structurally diverse molecules, offering a promising direction for efficient and scalable molecular discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何不流动而要池采样？基于GFlowNets的主动学习
基于池的主动学习的可扩展性受限于评估大规模未标注数据集的计算成本，这一挑战在药物发现的虚拟筛选中尤为突出。虽然诸如基于分歧的贝叶斯主动学习（BALD）等主动学习策略优先选择信息量大的样本，但在扩展到包含数十亿样本的库时仍计算成本高昂。本文提出BALD-GFlowNet，一种生成式主动学习框架，可规避此问题。该方法利用生成流网络（GFlowNets）直接按BALD奖励比例采样对象。通过将传统基于池的采样替换为生成式采样，BALD-GFlowNet实现了与未标注池大小无关的可扩展性。在虚拟筛选实验中，我们表明BALD-GFlowNet在性能上与标准BALD基线相当，同时生成结构更多样化的分子，为高效可扩展的分子发现提供了有前景的方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational bottleneck of pool-based active learning in large-scale virtual screening, this work proposes BALD-GFlowNet, a generative active learning framework that uses Generative Flow Networks to sample molecules directly proportional to the BALD reward, bypassing the need to evaluate entire unlabeled pools. Experimental results show that BALD-GFlowNet matches the performance of standard BALD while generating more structurally diverse molecules, demonstrating scalability independent of pool size and offering a viable path for efficient molecular discovery.
为解决大规模虚拟筛选中池式主动学习的计算瓶颈，本文提出BALD-GFlowNet框架，利用生成流网络直接按BALD奖励比例采样分子，避免传统方法需遍历整个未标注池的高成本。该方法实现与池大小无关的可扩展性。实验表明，BALD-GFlowNet在性能上与标准BALD相当，同时生成更具结构多样性的分子，为高效分子发现提供了可行路径。
A Hybrid TGN-SEAL Model for Dynamic Graph Link Prediction
Authors: Nafiseh Sadat Sajadi, Behnam Bahrak, Mahdi Jafari Siavoshani
Venue: EPJ Data Science (2026)
First: 2026-02-15T17:16:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:55:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting links in sparse, continuously evolving networks is a central challenge in network science. Conventional heuristic methods and deep learning models, including Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), are typically designed for static graphs and thus struggle to capture temporal dependencies. Snapshot-based techniques partially address this issue but often encounter data sparsity and class imbalance, particularly in networks with transient interactions such as telecommunication call detail records (CDRs). Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) model dynamic graphs by updating node embeddings over time; however, their predictive accuracy under sparse conditions remains limited. In this study, we improve the TGN framework by extracting enclosing subgraphs around candidate links, enabling the model to jointly learn structural and temporal information. Experiments on a sparse CDR, email, message dataset show that our approach increases average precision by at least 2% over standard TGNs, demonstrating the advantages of integrating local topology for robust link prediction in dynamic networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于动态图链接预测的混合TGN-SEAL模型
在稀疏且持续演化的网络中预测链接是网络科学的核心挑战。传统启发式方法和深度学习模型（包括图神经网络GNNs）通常针对静态图设计，难以捕捉时序依赖关系。基于快照的技术部分解决了这一问题，但在如电信通话详单（CDRs）等具有瞬态交互的网络中，常面临数据稀疏和类别不平衡问题。时序图网络（TGNs）通过随时间更新节点嵌入来建模动态图，但在稀疏条件下其预测精度仍有限。本研究通过提取候选链接周围的包围子图改进TGN框架，使模型能够联合学习结构与时序信息。在稀疏CDRs、邮件、消息数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法相比标准TGNs平均精度提升至少2%，证明了在动态网络中整合局部拓扑结构对鲁棒链接预测的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static graph models and snapshot-based methods in handling sparse, evolving networks, this study proposes a hybrid TGN-SEAL model that enhances temporal graph networks by incorporating local subgraph structures around candidate links. The method updates node embeddings over time while leveraging enclosing subgraphs to capture both structural and temporal dependencies, addressing data sparsity and class imbalance. Experimental results on sparse datasets including CDRs, email, and message networks show that the proposed model achieves at least a 2% improvement in average precision compared to standard TGNs, validating the effectiveness of integrating local topology for robust link prediction.
为解决稀疏且不断演化的网络中链接预测的难题，本文提出一种融合TGN与SEAL的混合模型，通过在候选链接周围提取局部子图，增强时序图网络对结构与时间信息的联合学习能力。该方法在更新节点嵌入的同时，利用邻域拓扑结构提升时序推理效果。在包含电信通话记录、邮件和消息日志的稀疏数据集上实验表明，该方法相较标准TGN平均精度提升至少2%，验证了局部拓扑结构对动态图中鲁棒链接预测的有效性。
Distilling Answer-Set Programming Rules from LLMs for Neurosymbolic Visual Question Answering
Authors: Thomas Eiter, Nelson Higuera Ruiz, Johannes Oetsch
First: 2026-06-02T07:35:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:55:37+00:00
Comments: Under consideration in Theory and Practice of Logic Programming (TPLP)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual Question Answering (VQA) is the task of answering questions about images, requiring the integration of multimodal input and reasoning. Modular approaches that incorporate logic-based representations into the reasoning component offer clear advantages over end-to-end trained systems, particularly in terms of interpretability. However, adapting or extending these representations when task requirements change can place a significant burden on developers. To address this challenge, we present an approach for distilling rules from Large Language Models (LLMs). Our method prompts an LLM to extend an initial VQA reasoning theory, expressed as an answer-set program, to meet new requirements of the task. Examples from VQA datasets guide the LLM, validate the results, and help correct erroneous rules by leveraging feedback from the ASP solver. We demonstrate that our approach is effective across diverse VQA datasets. Notably, only a few examples are needed to elicit correct rules from LLMs. Our experiments suggest that rule distillation from LLMs is a promising alternative to traditional data-driven rule learning approaches. Under consideration in Theory and Practice of Logic Programming (TPLP).
中文标题/摘要
标题：从大语言模型中蒸馏用于神经符号视觉问答的答案集编程规则
视觉问答（VQA）任务要求根据图像回答问题，需融合多模态输入与推理。将逻辑表示融入推理组件的模块化方法相比端到端训练系统具有更清晰的可解释性优势。然而，当任务需求变化时，调整或扩展这些表示会增加开发者的负担。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种从大语言模型（LLMs）中蒸馏规则的方法。该方法通过提示LLM扩展初始以答案集程序形式表达的VQA推理理论，以满足新任务需求。VQA数据集中的示例引导LLM，验证结果，并借助答案集编程求解器的反馈纠正错误规则。我们在多个VQA数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。值得注意的是，仅需少量示例即可从LLM中诱导出正确规则。实验表明，从LLM中蒸馏规则是传统数据驱动规则学习方法的有前景替代方案。目前正被《逻辑编程理论与实践》（TPLP）期刊审阅。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and adaptable reasoning in visual question answering, this work proposes distilling answer-set programming rules from large language models. The method prompts an LLM to extend an initial logic-based VQA theory, guided by example queries and answers from datasets, while using an ASP solver to validate and correct generated rules. Experimental results show that only a few examples are sufficient to elicit accurate rules, and the distilled rules perform effectively across multiple VQA benchmarks, demonstrating the viability of LLM-based rule distillation as an alternative to traditional rule learning methods.
为提升视觉问答中推理的可解释性和适应性，该研究提出从大语言模型中蒸馏答案集编程规则。方法通过提示模型扩展初始逻辑理论，结合示例引导与ASP求解器反馈进行错误修正。实验表明，仅需少量示例即可获得准确规则，验证了该方法在多个视觉问答数据集上的有效性，为传统规则学习提供了可行替代方案。
The Inference-Compute Frontier and a Latency-Efficient Architecture for Limit Order Book Prediction
Authors: C. Evans Hedges
First: 2026-06-24T15:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:54:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study whether a scaling-law-style inference-compute frontier appears in limit order book prediction. Using FI-2010 and a suite of models ranging from small decision trees to neural LOB architectures, we find that the realized empirical frontier of predictive loss versus structural forward work is well summarized by a power law. In particular, with MLPLOB held out as an architecture family, a power-law fit to the low- and mid-compute non-MLPLOB frontier extrapolates across multiple orders of magnitude and attains $R^2=0.941$ on the excluded high-compute MLPLOB target frontier. A similar exercise in latency space gives substantially weaker results, showing that latency is not merely noisy compute. We use this gap to motivate FastBiNLOB, a dense axis-separable LOB mixer built from hardware-friendly temporal and feature mixing operations. In a five-seed experiment, FastBiNLOB exceeds the published $y_{10}$ and $y_{100}$ macro-F1 targets at notably lower latency than existing published SOTA architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理-计算前沿与限价订单簿预测的低延迟架构
我们研究限价订单簿预测中是否存在类似缩放定律的推理-计算前沿。使用FI-2010数据集和从小型决策树到神经网络订单簿架构的一系列模型，我们发现预测损失与结构化前向计算量之间的实证前沿可由幂律良好概括。特别是，以MLPLOB作为架构家族保留，对低至中等计算量的非MLPLOB前沿进行幂律拟合，可跨多个数量级外推，并在排除的高计算量MLPLOB目标前沿上达到R²=0.941。 在延迟空间中进行类似实验得到显著较弱的结果，表明延迟并非仅仅是噪声计算。我们利用这一差距提出FastBiNLOB，这是一种基于硬件友好的时序与特征混合操作构建的密集轴分离式订单簿混合器。在五次种子实验中，FastBiNLOB在显著低于现有SOTA架构的延迟下，超越了已发表的y₁₀和y₁₀₀宏F1目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether a scaling-law-like frontier exists between predictive accuracy and computational cost in limit order book prediction, the study evaluates multiple model architectures on FI-2010 data and finds that predictive loss versus structural forward work follows a power law with high fidelity (R²=0.941). The analysis reveals that latency does not scale predictably with compute, prompting the design of FastBiNLOB—a hardware-efficient, axis-separable LOB mixer using temporal and feature mixing operations. In a five-seed experiment, FastBiNLOB achieves superior macro-F1 scores at y10 and y100 while operating at significantly lower latency than existing state-of-the-art models.
为探究限价订单簿预测中预测精度与计算成本之间的缩放规律，研究评估了多种模型架构，发现预测损失与结构前向计算量之间存在幂律关系，且在跨多个计算量级的外推中，对排除的高计算量MLPLOB目标前沿的R²达0.941。分析表明，延迟并非计算量的简单噪声，由此启发设计了FastBiNLOB架构，该架构基于硬件友好的时序与特征混合操作，实验表明其在五次种子实验中以更低延迟实现了优于现有最先进模型的宏F1得分。
Action ControlNet: A Lightweight Delay-Aware Adapter for Smooth Asynchronous Control in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Tiecheng Guo, Meng Guo
First: 2026-06-24T15:53:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:53:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation, but their inference latency remains a major obstacle to stable high-frequency control. Asynchronous execution mitigates this bottleneck by overlapping policy inference with action execution, yet the next action chunk is still predicted from stale observations while the robot continues to move. Direct chunk stitching therefore introduces handoff discontinuities, action jitter, and failures in contact-rich manipulation. Existing remedies typically require either full-policy retraining or architecture-specific runtime logic. This work proposes Action ControlNet (ACNet), a lightweight delay-aware adapter that uses the executed motion suffix as a residual condition for a mostly frozen action head. ACNet leaves the pretrained backbone unchanged, introduces few trainable parameters, and remains compatible with generative action heads such as diffusion and flow matching. On Kinetix, Meta-World MT50, and a real-world SO-ARM101 platform, ACNet improves robustness under inference delay and yields smoother asynchronous trajectories than direct chunk stitching, while remaining more lightweight than full delay-conditioned retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：动作控制网：一种轻量级延迟感知适配器，用于视觉-语言-动作模型中的平滑异步控制
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用机器人操作中展现出强大潜力，但其推理延迟仍是实现稳定高频控制的主要障碍。异步执行通过将策略推理与动作执行重叠来缓解这一瓶颈，但机器人持续移动时，下一动作块仍基于过时观测预测，直接拼接动作块会导致交接不连续、动作抖动及接触密集操作失败。现有解决方案通常需要全策略重训练或架构特定的运行时逻辑。本文提出动作控制网（ACNet），一种轻量级延迟感知适配器，利用已执行的运动后缀作为残差条件，对大部分冻结的动作头进行微调。ACNet保持预训练主干网络不变，仅引入少量可训练参数，且兼容扩散模型和流匹配等生成式动作头。在Kinetix、Meta-World MT50及真实SO-ARM101平台上，ACNet在推理延迟下提升鲁棒性，相比直接拼接动作块获得更平滑的异步轨迹，同时比全延迟条件重训练更轻量。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of inference latency hindering stable high-frequency control in vision-language-action models, this work introduces Action ControlNet (ACNet), a lightweight adapter that conditions action prediction on executed motion suffixes to compensate for stale observations during asynchronous execution. ACNet integrates into pretrained models with minimal trainable parameters and is compatible with generative action heads like diffusion models. Experiments on Kinetix, Meta-World MT50, and a real-world robotic platform show that ACNet significantly improves robustness under inference delay and produces smoother, more continuous action trajectories compared to direct chunk stitching, while being more computationally efficient than full delay-aware retraining.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型因推理延迟导致的高频率控制不稳定问题，本文提出轻量级延迟感知适配器Action ControlNet（ACNet），通过将已执行的动作后缀作为残差条件，补偿异步执行中因观察过时带来的预测偏差。ACNet在预训练主干网络上添加少量可训练参数，兼容扩散等生成式动作头。在Kinetix、Meta-World MT50及真实SO-ARM101平台实验中，ACNet在延迟环境下显著提升控制鲁棒性，生成轨迹比直接拼接更平滑连续，且无需全量重训练。
InvestPhilBench: A Multi-Layer Dynamic Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Procedural Reasoning in Expert Investment Philosophy
Authors: Mingguang Chen, Bo Qu
First: 2026-06-24T15:53:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:53:20+00:00
Comments: 57 pages, 6 figures, 26 tables. Benchmark, data, and code released. v0.6 release; preliminary empirical study (de-confounded multi-model leaderboard forthcoming)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly deployed as investment research assistants, yet no benchmark tests whether they can accurately reconstruct and apply the specific procedural decision frameworks of expert investors. We introduce InvestPhilBench, a multi-layer dynamic benchmark spanning eight cognitive tiers, from principle identification (L1) to novel framework extrapolation (L8). The v0.6 release comprises 118 primary-source-verified investment principle cards, 25 decision framework cards with explicit topology metadata, and 243 QA questions (197 dev / 46 held-out test). For reproducible scoring at scale we introduce the Benchmark Automated Scoring Pipeline (BASP) -- five algorithmic metrics (OGRS, KCCS, SAP@k, IVP, CKCA) -- the Failure Mode Detection Protocol (FMDP) with computable rules for six failure modes, and Gate Reconstruction Accuracy (GRA), a per-gate metric for questions with gold reasoning programs. In this release, InvestPhilBench is primarily a benchmark-and-methodology contribution. A four-model sanity wave on the 188-question development split shows a sharp provider-tier split (BASP 0.906 vs. 0.438); these mixed-judge numbers are confounded upper bounds. The central finding: the BASP composite saturates at the frontier (Claude L4 = 0.932) while GRA still exposes a procedural deficit (frontier L4 GRA approx. 0.77, L7 GRA 0.57-0.62) -- composite scoring rewards fluent prose and hides the procedural gap. v0.6 implements a unified judge and true model-in-the-loop retrieval/oracle conditions; the de-confounded multi-model leaderboard and full three-condition run are v1.0 deliverables. On a 100-item expert-annotated gold set the automated BASP composite tracks the human reference at Pearson r = 0.72 (MAE = 0.10), with attribution (SAP@3) the weakest sub-metric and the failure-mode detector running sensitive-but-over-flagging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InvestPhilBench：用于评估大型语言模型在专家投资哲学中程序性推理能力的多层次动态基准
大型语言模型正越来越多地被部署为投资研究助手，但尚无基准测试其能否准确重构并应用专家投资者的具体程序性决策框架。我们引入InvestPhilBench，这是一个涵盖八个认知层级的多层次动态基准，从原则识别（L1）到新框架外推（L8）。v0.6版本包含118张经原始来源验证的投资原则卡片、25张带有明确拓扑元数据的决策框架卡片，以及243个问答题（197个开发集/46个保留测试集）。为实现大规模可复现评分，我们引入基准自动化评分管道（BASP）——五项算法指标（OGRS、KCCS、SAP@k、IVP、CKCA）、包含六种故障模式可计算规则的故障模式检测协议（FMDP），以及针对具有黄金推理程序问题的门级准确率（GRA）。在本版本中，InvestPhilBench主要作为基准与方法论贡献。在188题开发集上的四模型基准测试显示显著的提供商层级差异（BASP 0.906 vs. 0.438）；这些混合评审得分是受干扰的上界。核心发现：BASP综合得分在前沿饱和（Claude L4 = 0.932），而GRA仍揭示程序性缺陷（前沿L4 GRA约0.77，L7 GRA 0.57-0.62）——综合评分奖励流畅文本，掩盖程序性差距。v0.6实现了统一评审员与真实模型在环检索/预言条件；去干扰多模型排行榜与完整三条件运行是v1.0交付内容。在100项专家标注黄金数据集上，自动化BASP综合得分与人类参考值相关系数为0.72（MAE=0.10），归因（SAP@3）为最弱子指标，故障模式检测器运行敏感但过度触发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate whether large language models can accurately reconstruct and apply expert investors' procedural reasoning frameworks, the authors introduce InvestPhilBench, a multi-layer dynamic benchmark covering eight cognitive tiers. The v0.6 release includes 118 verified principle cards, 25 framework cards with topology metadata, and 243 QA questions, scored via the Benchmark Automated Scoring Pipeline (BASP) using five metrics and a failure mode detector, plus Gate Reconstruction Accuracy (GRA) for reasoning program fidelity. Experimental results on 188 dev questions show a stark provider-tier gap (BASP 0.906 vs. 0.438), with the composite score saturating at the frontier (Claude L4 = 0.932) while GRA reveals persistent procedural deficits (L4 GRA ~0.77, L7 GRA 0.57–0.62), indicating that composite scoring rewards fluency over true reasoning. 为评估大语言模型能否准确重建并应用专家投资者的程序化决策框架，作者提出了多层动态基准InvestPhilBench，覆盖八个认知层级。v0.6版本包含118张经验证的投资原则卡片、25张带拓扑元数据的框架卡片及243道问答题，通过Benchmark Automated Scoring Pipeline（BASP）五项指标、失败模式检测协议及门控重建准确率进行评分。实验结果显示，顶级模型（BASP 0.906）与低级模型（0.438）表现差距显著，复合得分掩盖了程序化缺陷（如L7 GRA 0.57–0.62），而BASP与人类评分相关性中等（Pearson r = 0.72）。
Multi-Agent Goal Recognition with Team- and Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning and Factorized Branch-and-Bound
Authors: Thiago Thomas, Gabriel de Oliveira Ramos, Felipe Meneguzzi
First: 2026-06-24T15:50:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:50:39+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 1 figure, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent goal recognition asks an observer to jointly infer which agents act together and what each team is trying to achieve, so the hypothesis space grows combinatorially with the number of team partitions and goals per team. Real applications such as drone surveillance and collaborative robotics expose only the agents' trajectory, which forces the observer to rank team-goal hypotheses from behavior alone. Multi-Agent Goal Recognition with Branch-and-Bound (MAGR-BB) addresses this setting with a shared team- and goal-conditioned policy used as the scoring model inside a factorized branch-and-bound search. On a controlled multi-agent Blocksworld benchmark, MAGR-BB returns the same top-ranked hypothesis as exhaustive search throughout the trajectory while cutting hypothesis materialization by orders of magnitude and reducing cumulative recognition runtime substantially.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于团队与目标条件强化学习及因子化分支定界多智能体目标识别
多智能体目标识别要求观察者联合推断哪些智能体协同行动以及每个团队试图实现的目标，因此假设空间随团队划分数量和每个团队目标数量呈组合式增长。实际应用如无人机监控和协作机器人仅暴露智能体轨迹，迫使观察者仅依据行为对团队-目标假设进行排序。多智能体目标识别与分支定界（MAGR-BB）通过在因子化分支定界搜索中使用共享的团队与目标条件策略作为评分模型，解决该问题。在受控的多智能体Blocksworld基准测试中，MAGR-BB在整个轨迹中始终返回与穷举搜索相同的最高排名假设，同时将假设生成量降低多个数量级，并显著减少累积识别运行时间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to infer coordinated goals in multi-agent systems from limited observational data, this work proposes MAGR-BB, a method that combines team- and goal-conditioned reinforcement learning policies with a factorized branch-and-bound search to efficiently rank hypotheses. The approach leverages a shared policy to score candidate team-goal combinations, enabling scalable hypothesis pruning without exhaustive enumeration. Experimental results on a Blocksworld benchmark show that MAGR-BB consistently identifies the same top hypothesis as exhaustive search while reducing the computational cost of hypothesis generation by orders of magnitude and significantly cutting cumulative recognition runtime.
为了解决在无人机监控和协作机器人等实际场景中，仅通过轨迹数据推断多智能体团队目标的问题，本文提出MAGR-BB方法，结合团队与目标条件化的强化学习策略与因子化分支定界搜索，高效排序候选假设。该方法利用共享策略对团队目标组合进行评分，无需穷举所有可能性，大幅降低计算开销同时保持准确性。在Blocksworld基准测试中，MAGR-BB在整个轨迹中始终返回与穷举搜索相同的最优假设，同时将假设生成量降低多个数量级，显著缩短累计识别时间。
Spatial Transcriptomics as Images for Large-Scale Pretraining
Authors: Yishun Zhu, Jiaxin Qi, Jian Wang, Yuhua Zheng, Jianqiang Huang
First: 2026-03-13T05:12:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:48:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) profiles thousands of gene expression values at discrete spots with precise coordinates on tissue sections, preserving spatial context essential for clinical and pathological studies. With rising sequencing throughput and advancing platforms, the expanding data volumes motivate large-scale ST pretraining. However, the fundamental unit for pretraining, i.e., what constitutes a single training sample, remains ill-posed. Existing choices fall into two camps: (1) treating each spot as an independent sample, which discards spatial dependencies and collapses ST into single-cell transcriptomics; and (2) treating an entire slide as a single sample, which produces prohibitively large inputs and drastically fewer training examples, undermining effective pretraining. To address this gap, we propose treating spatial transcriptomics as croppable images. Specifically, we define a multi-channel image representation with fixed spatial size by cropping patches from raw slides, thereby preserving spatial context while substantially increasing the number of training samples. Along the channel dimension, we define gene subset selection rules to control input dimensionality and improve pretraining stability. Extensive experiments show that the proposed image-like dataset construction for ST pretraining consistently improves downstream performance, outperforming conventional pretraining schemes. Ablation studies verify that both spatial patching and channel design are necessary, establishing a unified, practical paradigm for organizing ST data and enabling large-scale pretraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将空间转录组学视为图像进行大规模预训练
空间转录组学（ST）在组织切片的离散位置上以精确坐标记录数千个基因表达值，保留对临床和病理研究至关重要的空间上下文。随着测序通量的提升和平台的进步，数据量的扩大推动了大规模ST预训练的需求。然而，预训练的基本单位——即单个训练样本的定义——仍不明确。现有方法分为两类：（1）将每个斑点视为独立样本，这会丢弃空间依赖性，使ST退化为单细胞转录组学；（2）将整张玻片视为单个样本，导致输入过大且训练样本数量急剧减少，难以实现有效的预训练。为解决这一问题，我们提出将空间转录组学视为可裁剪的图像。具体而言，通过从原始玻片中裁剪补丁，定义固定空间尺寸的多通道图像表示，从而在保留空间上下文的同时显著增加训练样本数量。在通道维度上，我们定义基因子集选择规则以控制输入维度并提升预训练稳定性。大量实验表明，所提出的图像式数据构建方法在ST预训练中持续提升下游性能，优于传统预训练方案。消融研究验证了空间补丁和通道设计的必要性，确立了一种统一、实用的数据组织范式，支持大规模预训练。

为有效利用大规模空间转录组（ST）数据进行预训练，本文解决了ST数据中训练样本定义模糊的问题——传统方法要么忽略空间结构，要么导致输入过大且样本过少。作者提出将ST数据视为可裁剪图像：从组织切片中提取固定大小的图像块，形成多通道图像，既保留空间上下文又增加训练样本数量。通过基因子集选择规则控制通道维度的输入维度，提升预训练稳定性。实验表明该方法显著优于传统预训练方案，消融实验验证空间分块与通道设计均不可或缺，为ST数据组织与大规模预训练提供统一实用范式。
Tensorion: A Tensor-Aware Generalization of the Muon Optimizer
Authors: Vladimir Bogachev, Vladimir Aletov, Alexander Molozhavenko, Sergei Kudriashov, Maxim Rakhuba
First: 2026-06-24T15:46:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:46:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Common first-order optimizers, such as Adam, implicitly treat each parameter block as an unstructured vector, which disregards the multilinear weight structure present in many modern machine learning models. Recent work has shown that exploiting matrix structure can improve optimization dynamics. A notable example is Muon, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm constraint. We take the next step and introduce Tensorion, a tensor-aware optimizer that extends Muon's constrained optimization perspective from matrices to higher-order tensors. Tensorion is built around a linear minimization oracle (LMO) over a tensor norm ball. The norm is carefully chosen to balance two objectives: tightly bounding the tensor spectral norm, while still keeping the LMO tractable. This LMO becomes computable because it reduces to operations on adaptively selected unfolding matrices. Notably, when restricted to order-2 tensors (i.e., matrices), Tensorion recovers Muon exactly. Experiments on tensor-based computer vision problems suggest that Tensorion can offer improved convergence behavior and more stable gradient updates compared with Adam-based and existing tensor-aware baselines in the evaluated settings.
中文标题/摘要

常见的第一阶优化器（如Adam）隐式地将每个参数块视为无结构向量，忽略了现代机器学习模型中普遍存在的多线性权重结构。近期研究表明，利用矩阵结构可改善优化动态。一个显著例子是Muon，它在谱范数约束下执行最陡下降。我们进一步提出Tensorion，一种面向张量的优化器，将Muon的约束优化视角从矩阵扩展到高阶张量。Tensorion基于张量范数球上的线性最小化算子（LMO）构建，其范数经过精心选择，以平衡两个目标：紧密约束张量谱范数，同时保持LMO可计算。该LMO之所以可计算，是因为它可归约为对自适应选择的展开矩阵的操作。值得注意的是，当限制在二阶张量（即矩阵）时，Tensorion精确恢复Muon。在基于张量的计算机视觉问题上的实验表明，Tensorion在评估设置中相比Adam类和现有张量感知基线方法，可提供更优的收敛行为和更稳定的梯度更新。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of standard first-order optimizers like Adam in handling the inherent multilinear structure of modern tensor-based models, the authors propose Tensorion, a tensor-aware generalization of Muon that extends constrained optimization from matrices to higher-order tensors. Tensorion employs a linear minimization oracle over a tensor norm ball, carefully designed to balance spectral norm control with computational tractability by reducing to operations on adaptively selected unfolding matrices. Experiments on tensor-based computer vision tasks show Tensorion achieves faster convergence and more stable gradient updates than Adam and existing tensor-aware baselines.
为了解决标准一阶优化器（如Adam）在处理现代张量模型中固有的多线性结构时效率低下问题，作者提出了Tensorion，它是Muon的张量感知扩展，将约束优化从矩阵推广到高阶张量。Tensorion基于张量范数球上的线性最小化算子，通过选择性展开矩阵运算实现计算可行性，同时平衡张量谱范数控制与计算效率。在张量计算机视觉任务上的实验表明，Tensorion相比Adam及现有张量感知基线方法，收敛更快、梯度更新更稳定。
Helpful or Harmful? Evaluating LLM-Assisted Vulnerability Patching via a Human Study
Authors: Giulian Biolo, Michael Tezza, Yuanjun Gong, Fabio Massacci
First: 2026-06-24T15:45:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:45:38+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Software vulnerability remediation is a cognitively demanding task that requires specialized security expertise often lacking in general developers. In the meantime, Large Language Models (LLMs) assisted tools show potential in vulnerability detection, location, and repair tasks. [Hypothesis:] While LLM-assistance is hypothesized to accelerate patching, it also risks introducing hallucinations or insecure code, leading to a higher likelihood of generating superficial repairs that bypass the standard functionality checks but fail the security validation. [Objective:] We aim to present an empirical experiment, unveiling the capability of LLM-assisted vulnerability patching compared to manual debugging on human participants in real-world scenarios. [Method:] We plan to conduct a controlled experiment using a Balanced Crossover design. For that, we have developed a WebApp for code execution and integrated hidden Ghost Tests to verify patch integrity beyond visible functional requirements. The experiment involves training and evaluation scenarios. The remediation speed, remediation efficacy for both standard functionality tests and security tests, and participant perception will be evaluated. [Pilot Study:] A pilot experiment with a small sample of participants has been conducted, providing insights for the following study.

Participants manually debug or use LLM-assisted tools to patch real-world vulnerabilities, with performance measured across remediation speed, functional correctness, and security validation via hidden Ghost Tests. Speech Codec Probing from Semantic and Phonetic Perspectives
Authors: Xuan Shi, Chang Zeng, Tiantian Feng, Shih-Heng Wang, Jianbo Ma, Shrikanth Narayanan
First: 2026-03-11T03:32:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:43:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Speech tokenizers are essential for connecting speech to large language models (LLMs) in multimodal systems. Speech tokenizers are expected to preserve both semantic and acoustic information for downstream understanding and generation tasks. However, emerging evidence suggests that the term "semantic" in speech processing does not align with linguistic lexical-semantic, leading to a mismatch between speech and text modality. In this paper, we systematically analyze the information encoded by several widely used speech tokenizers, evaluating their lexical-semantic and phonetic content through three tasks. Our results show that current tokenizers primarily capture phonetic rather than lexical-semantic structure, deriving practical implications for the design of next-generation speech tokenization methods. Code is released to public at https://github.com/Alexuan/codec_probing_release.

Improving Neural Network Training by Decoupling the Magnitude and Direction of Weight Vectors
Authors: Alexander Hägele, Alejandro Hernández-Cano, Atli Kosson, Martin Jaggi
First: 2026-06-24T15:40:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:40:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern neural network training relies on optimizers such as Adam and Muon which act on each weight matrix as a single object. Yet every weight matrix carries two distinct quantities -- a \emph{magnitude} and a \emph{direction} -- and all optimizers stepping in the matrix as a whole couple their dynamics: the directional change from an update depends on the current magnitude, while the magnitude drifts as a byproduct of learning the direction, so neither is governed directly by the learning rate. Typical training therefore leans on surrounding recipes such as weight decay and warmup to keep learning stable at scale, though these regulate the coupling only indirectly; other recent methods instead constrain the weight to a fixed-norm sphere, but add no learnable magnitude, leaving scale control to normalization layers alone. We propose \emph{Magnitude--Direction (MD) Decoupling}, an optimizer modification that factorizes each weight into a fixed-norm direction on a hypersphere and learnable per-row and per-column magnitude gains, updated at separate learning rates, all while the model still sees a single fused weight tensor. The method is agnostic to the base optimizer and removes the need for weight decay and warmup. Across both Adam and Muon, MD Decoupling improves on well-tuned baselines, transfers the optimal LR across model width without retuning, and continues to help at scale on large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. Treating magnitude and direction as separately controlled quantities thus yields more predictable training dynamics and a simple, broadly applicable improvement to modern optimizers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过解耦权重向量的幅度与方向改进神经网络训练
现代神经网络训练依赖于Adam和Muon等优化器，它们将每个权重矩阵视为单一对象进行操作。然而，每个权重矩阵包含两个独立量——幅度与方向——所有优化器在矩阵整体上进行更新时，会耦合其动态：方向变化依赖于当前幅度，而幅度则作为学习方向的副产物漂移，因此两者均未直接由学习率控制。典型训练依赖于权重衰减和预热等辅助方法以保持大规模学习稳定，但这些方法仅间接调节耦合；其他近期方法则约束权重位于固定范数球面上，但不引入可学习幅度，仅靠归一化层控制尺度。我们提出幅度-方向（MD）解耦，这是一种优化器修改方法，将每个权重分解为超球面上固定范数的方向与可学习的行/列幅度增益，分别以不同学习率更新，同时模型仍看到单一融合权重张量。该方法对基础优化器无依赖，无需权重衰减和预热。在Adam和Muon上，MD解耦优于调优基线，无需重新调参即可跨模型宽度迁移最优学习率，并在大型专家混合（MoE）模型中持续提供帮助。将幅度与方向视为独立可控量，从而获得更可预测的训练动态，并为现代优化器提供简单且广泛适用的改进。

This decoupling removes the need for weight decay and warmup, and is compatible with any base optimizer. Experiments show MD Decoupling consistently outperforms tuned baselines on both Adam and Muon, transfers optimal learning rates across model widths without retuning, and maintains benefits on large MoE models, yielding more predictable training dynamics.
为了解决当前优化器将权重矩阵视为单一对象导致幅度与方向更新耦合、学习率控制受限的问题，本文提出幅度-方向（MD）解耦方法：将每个权重分解为单位球面上的固定方向与可独立学习的行/列幅度增益，分别以不同学习率更新。该方法无需权重衰减或预热，可直接控制幅度。实验表明，MD解耦在Adam和Muon优化器上均优于调优基线，学习率可跨模型宽度迁移无需重新调参，并在大规模专家混合模型中持续有效。
A Sensorised Lattice Footplate for a Semi-Active Prosthetic Foot
Authors: Jinze Ge, Jingcheng Sun, Chengxu Zhou
First: 2026-06-24T15:35:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:35:49+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 7 figures, ICAC
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper investigates whether magnetic plantar sensing can be embedded directly inside the load-bearing compliant element of a low-cost semi-active prosthetic foot. We present a prototype integrating a sensorised 3D-printed lattice footplate, a servo-adjustable hydraulic damper, and a reduced-order ankle model. The damper is experimentally characterised to relate adjustment angle to damping coefficient. Controlled compression tests show tunable lattice stiffness, while cyclic normal loading shows that the embedded sensor tracks the testing-machine reference force, supporting plantar-force estimation without an external insole layer. Static-posture trials under approximately body-weight loading show that forefoot and rearfoot loading distributions are separable across four prescribed stance configurations, providing a preliminary check of the sensing pipeline. A feedforward damping schedule approximates the dorsiflexion trend of a reference ankle trajectory through early-to-mid stance, while exposing the expected limitation that a purely dissipative mechanism cannot generate active push-off. Together, these results demonstrate that sensing can be embedded inside the load-bearing compliant element of a prosthetic foot and used to drive semi-active damping.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于半主动假肢足部的传感格栅足板

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for cost-effective, embedded sensing in prosthetic feet, this study explores integrating magnetic plantar sensors directly into a 3D-printed lattice footplate within a semi-active prosthetic foot.
