为实现低成本假肢足部的嵌入式传感，本研究探索将磁性足底传感器直接集成于3D打印的格栅足板内。原型包含伺服调节液压阻尼器与简化踝关节模型，实验标定阻尼系数与调节角度的关系。压缩测试验证了格栅结构的可调刚度，循环载荷测试表明嵌入式传感器能准确追踪施加力，无需外置鞋垫。静态姿势试验在接近体重负载下，四种站姿配置中前足与后足受力分布可分离，验证了传感方案的有效性。前馈阻尼策略可模拟踝关节早期至中期的背屈趋势，但表明纯耗散机制无法产生主动蹬地，证实系统为半主动控制。
Mixture-of-Experts RL for Fault-Tolerant Legged Locomotion
Authors: Giulio Turrisi, Ozan Pali, Luca Oneto, Claudio Semini
First: 2026-06-24T15:35:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:35:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Legged robots deployed in planetary exploration and other remote environments must maintain reliable locomotion despite actuator failures and challenging terrain conditions. Although reinforcement learning has achieved strong results in legged locomotion, monolithic policies can struggle to efficiently represent the diverse control strategies required to compensate for different fault conditions. In this work, we propose a fault-aware modular control architecture that explicitly leverages fault-diagnosis information to activate specialized control experts associated with distinct actuator failure modes. Experimental results show that explicit fault-conditioned modular policies consistently outperform monolithic policies of comparable size, achieving higher locomotion performance across failure scenarios. Moreover, the proposed modular architecture retains competitive performance even under significantly reduced network capacity, highlighting its suitability for compute-constrained robotic platforms, such as those typically employed in space applications. The code associated with this work is available at: https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/fault-locomotion-isaaclab.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向容错腿式移动的专家混合强化学习
在行星探测等远程环境中部署的腿式机器人必须在执行器故障和复杂地形条件下保持可靠的移动能力。尽管强化学习在腿式移动方面已取得显著成果，但单一策略难以高效表示应对不同故障条件所需的多样化控制策略。本文提出一种故障感知的模块化控制架构，通过显式利用故障诊断信息激活与不同执行器故障模式相关的专用控制专家。实验结果表明，显式故障条件下的模块化策略在性能上持续优于同等规模的单一策略，且在各类故障场景下均实现更高的移动性能。此外，所提出的模块化架构即使在网络容量显著降低时仍能保持竞争力，凸显其适用于计算受限的机器人平台（如太空应用中常见的平台）。本工作的代码可访问：https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/fault-locomotion-isaaclab。
Summary / 总结
To enable legged robots to maintain locomotion in remote environments despite actuator failures, this work introduces a fault-aware modular control architecture using a mixture-of-experts reinforcement learning framework. The method dynamically activates specialized control experts based on diagnosed fault conditions, allowing the system to adaptively respond to different actuator failure modes. Experimental results demonstrate that this modular approach consistently outperforms monolithic policies of similar size across various failure scenarios, while also preserving strong performance even under reduced network capacity, making it suitable for resource-constrained robotic platforms.
为使腿式机器人在远程环境中即使遭遇执行器故障仍能保持可靠移动，该研究提出了一种基于故障诊断信息的模块化控制架构，采用专家混合强化学习方法，根据诊断出的故障类型激活对应的专用控制策略。实验表明，该模块化方法在多种故障场景下持续优于同等规模的单体策略，且在网络容量显著受限时仍能保持竞争力，适用于计算资源受限的机器人平台，如太空任务中使用的机器人。
WinDOM: Self-Family Distillation for Small-Model GUI Grounding
Authors: Chengheng Li-Chen, Zhiqian Zhou, Hao Chen, Nicolas Chauvin
First: 2026-06-24T15:35:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:35:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Small ($\sim$2B) GUI-grounding agents are attractive for on-device deployment, accessibility tooling, and low-cost iteration, but at this scale they face two open recipe questions: how to obtain bounding-box training data without expensive human annotation, and how to combine supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. We address both, with the explicit goal of pushing small-model performance rather than scaling up. WinDOM is a $54{,}425$-record grounding corpus harvested by driving an open-source Windows 11 web reimplementation under headless Playwright, with bounding boxes read directly off the DOM and no OCR or human annotation. Self-Family Distillation (SFD) is a single rejection-sampling cold-start parameterised only by the teacher choice: either an EMA of the student (no external model) or a frozen larger same-family teacher. We then treat the saturation depth of the SFD cold-start as an explicit GRPO hyperparameter. On a Qwen3.5-2B student, the under-saturated cold-start is a better GRPO initialiser than the converged one: SFD-4B with Early-init RL gains $+5.4$ OOD-mean ($+3.5$ ScreenSpot-Pro, $+7.0$ OSWorld-G, $+5.8$ ScreenSpot-V2) over the base. The same-size EMA mode lands within roughly one OOD-mean point of the cross-size $4$B variant ($65.2$ vs $66.3$) without an external teacher.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WinDOM：面向小模型GUI定位的自家族蒸馏方法
小规模（约2B参数）GUI定位代理适用于设备端部署、辅助工具及低成本迭代，但在该规模下面临两个开放性问题：如何在无需昂贵人工标注的情况下获取边界框训练数据，以及如何结合监督微调与强化学习。我们同时解决这两个问题，目标是提升小模型性能而非扩大模型规模。WinDOM是一个包含54,425条记录的定位语料库，通过在无头Playwright环境下驱动开源Windows 11网页重实现获取，边界框直接从DOM读取，无需OCR或人工标注。自家族蒸馏（SFD）是一种仅由教师选择参数化的冷启动方法：教师可为学生模型的指数移动平均（EMA）或冻结的同家族大模型。我们将SFD冷启动的饱和深度视为显式GRPO超参数。在Qwen3.5-2B学生模型上，未饱和的冷启动比收敛后的冷启动更优：SFD-4B结合早期强化学习初始化，相比基线模型在OOD均值上提升+5.4（ScreenSpot-Pro提升+3.5，OSWorld-G提升+7.0，ScreenSpot-V2提升+5.8）。相同规模的EMA模式在无外部教师情况下，其OOD均值（65.2）与跨规模4B变体（66.3）相差约1个点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, on-device GUI grounding with small models, this work tackles two key challenges: generating bounding-box training data without human annotation and integrating supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. WinDOM, a 54,425-record corpus, is built by automating GUI interactions via Playwright on a Windows 11 web reimplementation, extracting bounding boxes directly from the DOM. The authors introduce Self-Family Distillation (SFD), a cold-start method that uses either an EMA of the student or a frozen larger teacher from the same family, parameterized only by teacher choice. Treating SFD saturation depth as a GRPO hyperparameter, they find that early-init RL with SFD-4B outperforms converged SFD, achieving +5.4 OOD-mean across benchmarks, with notable gains on ScreenSpot-Pro, OSWorld-G, and ScreenSpot-V2. The EMA-only mode performs nearly as well as the cross-size 4B variant without external teachers.
为实现小模型在设备端高效运行的GUI定位，该研究提出WinDOM数据集，通过无OCR、无人标注的无头Playwright自动化生成54,425条带框数据。其核心方法Self-Family Distillation（SFD）利用学生模型自身EMA或同家族冻结大模型作为冷启动初始化器，将SFD饱和深度作为GRPO超参数。实验表明，采用未饱和冷启动（SFD-4B+Early-init RL）比收敛状态初始化效果更优，平均OOD得分提升5.4，其中ScreenSpot-Pro提升3.5、OSWorld-G提升7.0、ScreenSpot-V2提升5.8；仅用EMA模式的性能（65.2）与跨规模4B模型（66.3）相差不到1点，无需外部教师模型。
A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Stereo Deblurring with Physically- and Epipolar-constrained Cross Attention
Authors: Hoju Shin, Jiah Kim, Seung-Wook Kim, Seowon Ji
First: 2026-06-24T15:34:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:34:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern stereo-capable smartphones enable immersive XR content capture. However, hardware heterogeneity across camera modules often causes severe asymmetric blur artifacts. Existing methods and benchmarks largely assume homogeneous stereo setups and therefore do not explicitly address such asymmetric degradation. To bridge this gap, we present a dedicated framework for heterogeneous stereo deblurring. First, we introduce the heterogeneous stereo deblurring (HSD) dataset, constructed from real smartphone stereo captures via multi-frame integration. Second, we propose physically- and epipolar-constrained cross attention (PECA), a lightweight module that restricts cross-view matching to an epipolar search window bounded by a optics-derived disparity upper bound. By enforcing physically valid disparity constraints, PECA enables efficient and reliable cross-view feature fusion. Moreover, our confidence-weighted attention with residual fusion emphasizes cross-guided deblurring when correspondences are reliable, while naturally falling back to self-deblurring in occluded or unreliable regions. PECA is architecture-agnostic and consistently improves CNN-, Transformer-, and NAFNet-based baselines. Extensive experiments on HSD show that PECA-enhanced models achieve improved restoration performance with favorable efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理与极线约束交叉注意力的异构立体去模糊基准
现代具备立体拍摄功能的智能手机可实现沉浸式XR内容采集。然而，不同相机模组间的硬件异构性常导致严重的非对称模糊伪影。现有方法与基准大多假设同构立体设置，因而未明确处理此类非对称退化问题。为弥合此差距，我们提出专用于异构立体去模糊的框架。首先，我们构建了异构立体去模糊（HSD）数据集，通过多帧融合真实智能手机立体拍摄数据。其次，我们提出物理与极线约束交叉注意力（PECA），一种轻量模块，将跨视图匹配限制在由光学推导出的视差上限界定的极线搜索窗口内。通过施加物理有效视差约束，PECA实现高效可靠的跨视图特征融合。此外，我们的置信度加权注意力结合残差融合，在对应关系可靠时强调跨引导去模糊，而在遮挡或不可靠区域自然退化为自去模糊。PECA架构无关，且持续提升CNN、Transformer及NAFNet基线模型性能。在HSD上的大量实验表明，PECA增强模型在保持良好效率的同时实现更优的恢复效果。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to address asymmetric blur artifacts in heterogeneous smartphone stereo captures, this work introduces the HSD dataset and the PECA module, which uses physically and epipolar constraints to guide cross-view attention. PECA restricts feature matching to a disparity-bounded epipolar window, enabling efficient cross-view fusion while falling back to self-deblurring in unreliable regions. Experiments show PECA consistently boosts performance across CNN, Transformer, and NAFNet architectures on the HSD benchmark, achieving better restoration quality with improved efficiency.
为解决异构智能手机立体相机系统中出现的不对称模糊问题，本文提出了一种新的基准数据集和名为PECA的轻量级跨视图注意力模块。PECA结合物理光学约束与极线几何，限定跨视图特征融合在由光学推导出的视差上限所界定的极线搜索窗口内，从而实现可靠匹配并自动回避遮挡或不可靠区域。在新构建的HSD数据集上的实验表明，PECA可显著提升CNN、Transformer和NAFNet等架构的去模糊性能，且保持高效计算。
Agentic System as Compressor: Quantifying System Intelligence in Bits
Authors: Zihan Qin, Hongrui Zhang
First: 2026-06-24T15:32:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:32:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are turning from isolated predictors into agentic systems: they call tools, retrieve evidence, obey environment constraints, use verifiers, and complete tasks through search and multi-turn interaction. We adopts an analytical viewpoint based on "compression is intelligence": under a fixed task distribution, interface, and compute budget, a stronger agentic system lets a target object be reconstructed with fewer bits. We operationalize the measure with arithmetic coding, seed coding, and a fallback, and evaluate it in five settings: reversed text, chess moves, protein sequences, retrieval-augmented question answering, and semantic story compression; in all of them agentic components reduce codelength. These small, controlled experiments cover component types typical of real agentic systems, show that codelength can analyze how components, observers, and budgets change residual uncertainty, and offer guidance for evaluating real agent systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理系统作为压缩器：以比特量化系统智能
大语言模型正从孤立预测器转变为代理系统：它们调用工具、检索证据、遵守环境约束、使用验证器，并通过搜索和多轮交互完成任务。我们采用“压缩即智能”的分析视角：在固定任务分布、接口和计算预算下，更强的代理系统能用更少的比特重构目标对象。我们通过算术编码、种子编码及备用方法操作化该度量，并在五个场景中评估：逆向文本、国际象棋走法、蛋白质序列、检索增强问答和语义故事压缩；在所有场景中，代理组件均减少编码长度。这些小型可控实验涵盖了真实代理系统中典型的组件类型，表明编码长度可分析组件、观察者和预算如何改变残余不确定性，并为评估真实代理系统提供指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the idea that intelligence equals compression, this paper quantifies agentic system intelligence by measuring how efficiently a target object can be reconstructed under fixed task, interface, and compute constraints. Using arithmetic coding, seed coding, and fallback methods, the study evaluates five agentic scenarios—reversed text, chess moves, protein sequences, retrieval-augmented QA, and semantic story compression—and consistently finds that agentic components reduce codelength. These controlled experiments demonstrate that codelength serves as a viable metric to analyze how system components, observers, and budgets influence residual uncertainty, offering a principled approach to evaluating real-world agentic systems.
基于‘智能即压缩’的分析视角，该研究通过在固定任务、接口与计算预算下测量目标对象的重建所需比特数，量化了代理系统的智能水平。采用算术编码、种子编码及备用方法，在反转文本、棋局走法、蛋白质序列、检索增强问答和语义故事压缩五个场景中进行评估，结果均显示代理组件显著降低编码长度。这些受控实验表明，编码长度可作为衡量系统组件、观察者及资源预算如何影响残余不确定性的敏感指标，为真实代理系统的评估提供指导。
SE-AGCNet: An End-to-End Framework for Joint Speech Enhancement and Loudness Control in Meeting Scenarios
Authors: Jinming Zhang, Wei Rao, Xionghu Zhong, Eng Siong Chng
First: 2026-06-24T15:32:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:32:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conventional audio pipelines typically treat speech enhancement (SE) and automatic gain control (AGC) as discrete modules, which often limits overall performance. For instance, applying AGC before SE may inadvertently amplify background noise, while prioritizing SE tends to over-suppress low-volume speech. To address these limitations, we propose SE-AGCNet, an end-to-end framework that jointly optimizes SE and AGC. Tailored for meeting scenarios with significant volume variations, SE-AGCNet leverages the synergy between the two tasks: SE preserves quiet speech, thereby facilitating effective volume adjustment by the AGC component. Furthermore, we propose a specialized data simulation pipeline, SE-AGC-DataGen, and incorporate standardized loudness evaluation metrics: integrated loudness (LUFS), short-term loudness (St LUFS), and LRA. Experiments show that SE-AGCNet consistently achieves target loudness while improving speech quality and ASR accuracy over competitive baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SE-AGCNet：一种面向会议场景的语音增强与响度控制联合端到端框架
传统音频处理流程通常将语音增强（SE）与自动增益控制（AGC）视为独立模块，常限制整体性能。例如，先应用AGC再进行SE可能无意放大背景噪声，而优先SE则易过度压制低音量语音。为解决此问题，我们提出SE-AGCNet，一种联合优化SE与AGC的端到端框架。该框架专为存在显著音量变化的会议场景设计，利用两项任务的协同效应：SE保留安静语音，从而便于AGC模块有效调整音量。此外，我们提出专用数据模拟流程SE-AGC-DataGen，并引入标准化响度评估指标：综合响度（LUFS）、短时响度（St LUFS）和LRA。实验表明，SE-AGCNet在实现目标响度的同时，相较竞争基线显著提升语音质量和ASR准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the suboptimal performance of treating speech enhancement and automatic gain control as separate stages, SE-AGCNet proposes an end-to-end framework that jointly optimizes both tasks for meeting scenarios with varying volume levels. The method leverages the complementary nature of the two processes: SE preserves quiet speech, enabling AGC to adjust volume more effectively. A custom data simulation pipeline, SE-AGC-DataGen, is used to generate training data, and standardized loudness metrics (LUFS, St LUFS, LRA) are employed for evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that SE-AGCNet achieves target loudness levels while simultaneously improving speech quality and automatic speech recognition accuracy compared to existing methods.
为解决传统方法将语音增强与自动增益控制作为独立模块处理导致性能受限的问题，SE-AGCNet提出了一种端到端联合优化框架，专为会议场景中音量变化大的情况设计。该方法利用语音增强与增益控制任务的协同效应：语音增强保留低音量语音，使增益控制更有效。通过自研数据模拟管道SE-AGC-DataGen进行训练，并采用LUFS、St LUFS和LRA等响度评估指标进行验证。实验表明，SE-AGCNet能稳定实现目标响度，同时在语音质量和ASR准确率上优于现有基线方法。
SC-TauPath: A Structural Connectivity Attribution Framework for Mapping Tau Propagation Pathways in Alzheimer's Disease
Authors: Jing Zhang, Norman Scheel, Minheng Chen, Tong Chen, Yanjun Lyu, David C. Zhu, Rong Zhang, Dajiang Zhu
First: 2026-06-02T13:57:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:31:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how structural connections are associated with tau propagation in Alzheimer's disease (AD) remains a central open question, yet existing computational models either rely heavily on biophysical assumptions or lack neurobiologically interpretable pathway maps. We present SC-TauPath, a structural connectivity (SC) attribution framework that maps tau propagation pathways from in vivo neuroimaging data. SC-TauPath combines a Network Diffusion Model (NDM)-augmented multilayer perceptron with gradient $\times$ input attribution to score each SC edge's contribution to tau prediction, then translates these attribution scores into multi-scale pathway maps (backbone edges, high-traffic routes, and hub ROIs), which validates established Braak staging anatomy. Applied to 234 ADNI participants with paired DTI SC and 18F-Flortaucipir PET, SC-TauPath achieves strong cross-validated tau prediction and yields attribution-based pathway maps consistent with established Braak staging anatomy, demonstrating that SC encode spatially specific information about regional tau distribution in AD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SC-TauPath：一种用于绘制阿尔茨海默病中tau传播路径的结构连接性归因框架
理解结构连接性与阿尔茨海默病（AD）中tau传播之间的关联仍是核心未解问题，但现有计算模型要么依赖大量生物物理假设，要么缺乏神经生物学可解释的路径图谱。我们提出SC-TauPath，一种结构连接性（SC）归因框架，可从活体神经影像数据中绘制tau传播路径。SC-TauPath结合了增强型网络扩散模型（NDM）的多层感知机与梯度×输入归因方法，评估每条SC边对tau预测的贡献，并将这些归因分数转化为多尺度路径图谱（主干边、高流量路线和枢纽脑区），验证了已确立的Braak分期解剖学。应用于234名ADNI参与者（配对DTI结构连接性与18F-Flortaucipir PET数据），SC-TauPath实现了强交叉验证的tau预测，并生成与已确立Braak分期解剖学一致的归因路径图谱，表明SC编码了AD中区域tau分布的空间特异性信息。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to link structural brain connectivity with tau propagation in Alzheimer's disease without relying on biophysical assumptions or interpretable pathway maps, SC-TauPath introduces a framework that combines a Network Diffusion Model-augmented multilayer perceptron with gradient × input attribution to quantify each structural connection's contribution to tau prediction. Applied to 234 ADNI participants with DTI-derived structural connectivity and 18F-Flortaucipir PET data, the method generates multi-scale pathway maps—backbone edges, high-traffic routes, and hub regions—that align with Braak staging anatomy. The model achieves strong cross-validated tau prediction and demonstrates that structural connectivity encodes spatially specific information about regional tau distribution in AD.
为了解决阿尔茨海默病中结构连接如何影响tau传播的问题，且避免依赖生物物理假设或缺乏神经生物学可解释的路径图，SC-TauPath提出一种结合网络扩散模型增强的多层感知机与梯度×输入归因的方法，量化每条结构连接对tau预测的贡献。该方法应用于234名ADNI受试者（含DTI结构连接与18F-Flortaucipir PET数据），生成多尺度路径图（主干边、高流量路径和枢纽区域），与已知的Braak分期解剖结构一致。模型在交叉验证下表现出强tau预测能力，表明结构连接编码了AD中区域tau分布的空间特异性信息。
Exploring Information Seeking Agent Consolidation
Authors: Guochen Yan, Jialong Wu, Zhengwei Tao, Bo Li, Qintong Zhang, Jiahao Xu, Haitao Mi, Yuejian Fang, Qingni Shen, Wentao Zhang, Zhonghai Wu
First: 2026-01-31T07:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:29:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Information-seeking agents have emerged as a powerful paradigm for knowledge-intensive tasks, yet today's systems remain specialized for the open web, documents, or local knowledge bases, hindering scalable and cross-domain deployment. We present the first systematic empirical study of consolidating these information-seeking agents into a single foundation agentic model. We compare two paradigms -- \emph{data-level mixing}, which trains a unified model on a mixture of datasets, and \emph{parameter-level merging}, which merges independently trained experts in parameter space -- across 3 training scenarios, evaluating \textbf{26} representative parameter-level methods on \textbf{10} benchmarks. To compare across heterogeneous benchmarks, we introduce a geometric Composite Score and an Imbalance Score that describe overall performance and task skew. Our analysis shows that (i) well-designed parameter-level merging attains parity with data mixing at a fraction of its training cost and is order-agnostic; (ii) parameter-level merging structurally preserves out-of-domain capabilities that data mixing universally forgets; and (iii) cross-scenario stability is strongly tied to consolidation quality. We distil our observations into a method-selection guide and design principles for next-generation merging operators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索信息检索代理的整合
信息检索代理已成为知识密集型任务的强大范式，但当前系统仍局限于开放网络、文档或本地知识库，阻碍了其可扩展性和跨领域部署。我们首次系统性地实证研究了将这些信息检索代理整合为单一基础代理模型的方法。我们比较了两种范式——\emph{数据层混合}（在混合数据集上训练统一模型）和\emph{参数层融合}（在参数空间中融合独立训练的专家）——在三种训练场景下，评估了\textbf{26}种代表性参数层方法在\textbf{10}个基准测试中的表现。为跨异构基准进行比较，我们引入了几何复合得分和不平衡得分，分别描述整体性能和任务偏斜。我们的分析表明：(i) 设计良好的参数层融合方法在训练成本仅为数据混合的几分之一时，性能可与之持平，且与顺序无关；(ii) 参数层融合在结构上保留了数据混合普遍遗忘的跨域能力；(iii) 跨场景稳定性与整合质量密切相关。我们将观察结果总结为方法选择指南和下一代融合操作符的设计原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, cross-domain information-seeking agents beyond specialized systems, this study empirically compares data-level mixing and parameter-level merging paradigms for consolidating multiple agents into a unified foundation model. Across 3 training scenarios and 10 benchmarks, 26 parameter-level methods were evaluated using novel geometric and imbalance scores. Results show parameter-level merging achieves performance parity with data mixing at lower cost, preserves out-of-domain capabilities that data mixing loses, and exhibits stronger cross-scenario stability, leading to a method-selection guide and design principles for future merging operators.
为实现跨领域、可扩展的信息检索代理，本研究系统比较了数据混合与参数级合并两种方法，将多个信息检索代理整合为统一基础模型。在3种训练场景和10个基准测试中，评估了26种参数级方法，引入几何复合得分与不平衡得分进行跨异构基准比较。结果表明，设计良好的参数级合并方法在训练成本上仅为数据混合的几分之一，性能相当，且能保留数据混合丢失的跨域能力，同时表现出更强的跨场景稳定性，最终提出方法选择指南与下一代合并操作的设计原则。
From Rubble Simulation to Active Magnetic Mapping: Quantum Sensing for Disaster Response
Authors: Samuel Tovey, Stefan Prestel, Hiroshi Yamauchi
First: 2026-06-24T15:29:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:29:26+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, seven figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Locating survivors of building collapses within the first 72 hours is a critical challenge in disaster response, and existing sensing modalities provide only partial information about the structure beneath the rubble. This paper proposes drone-based quantum magnetometry as a complementary modality and develops a simulation pipeline spanning rubble physics, sensor-array deployment, and active spatial reconstruction. We use Unreal Engine to generate a steel-reinforced concrete parking-garage collapse and compute the induced magnetic field via a per-triangle dipole approximation, establishing that meaningful magnetic structure is recoverable in the sub-pT to sub-nT range from roughly 1 m above the roofline. Then, we feed sparse multi-sensor samples into a Gaussian Process Regression back-end driven by Bayesian active sampling and validate the pipeline across multiple independent collapse realizations; a three-sensor array optimizes the trade-off between gradient resolution and UAV payload constraints, and active sampling reaches peak structural correlation in roughly $100$ samples. Together, these results indicate that quantum-grade sensing could become a useful tool for drone-based structural analysis and potentially void detection in collapsed buildings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从废墟模拟到主动磁性测绘：量子传感在灾害响应中的应用
在灾害响应中，于建筑物倒塌后72小时内定位幸存者是一项关键挑战，现有传感手段仅能提供关于废墟下结构的部分信息。本文提出基于无人机的量子磁力测量作为补充手段，并构建涵盖废墟物理、传感器阵列部署及主动空间重建的模拟流程。我们使用Unreal Engine生成钢骨混凝土停车场倒塌场景，通过每三角形偶极子近似计算感应磁场，证实从屋顶上方约1米处可恢复出亚皮特斯拉至亚纳特斯拉量级的有意义磁结构。随后，我们将稀疏多传感器样本输入由贝叶斯主动采样驱动的高斯过程回归后端，并在多个独立倒塌场景中验证该流程；三传感器阵列在梯度分辨率与无人机载荷约束间取得最佳平衡，主动采样在约100次样本内达到结构相关性的峰值。综合结果表明，量子级传感有望成为无人机结构分析及倒塌建筑空洞探测的实用工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to locate survivors in collapsed buildings within 72 hours using limited sensing data, this study proposes drone-based quantum magnetometry as a complementary sensing method. The authors simulate a steel-reinforced concrete garage collapse in Unreal Engine, compute induced magnetic fields using a per-triangle dipole model, and demonstrate that magnetic structures are recoverable from 1 m above the rubble in the sub-pT to sub-nT range. They then deploy sparse multi-sensor arrays and use Gaussian Process Regression with Bayesian active sampling to reconstruct the structure; a three-sensor configuration optimizes gradient resolution against UAV payload limits, achieving peak structural correlation in about 100 samples. These results suggest quantum-grade magnetometry could enable effective drone-based structural and void detection in disaster zones.
为在灾后72小时内定位废墟下的幸存者，本研究提出利用搭载量子磁力计的无人机作为补充传感手段。方法结合Unreal Engine构建的物理仿真流程，模拟钢筋混凝土结构坍塌并计算磁感应场，再通过贝叶斯主动采样与高斯过程回归重建地下结构。实验表明，从距废墟屋顶约1米高度处，可检测到亚皮特斯拉至亚纳特斯拉量级的磁异常；三传感器阵列在无人机载荷限制下实现了最佳梯度分辨率，约100次主动采样即可达到结构相关性峰值。
Pulmonary Embolism Risk Stratification from CTPA and Medical Records: Vascular Graphs Are Not All You Need
Authors: Nathan Painchaud, Tristan Habémont, Morgane des Ligneris, Allan Serva, Pierre Croisille, Laurent Bertoletti, Thomas Lampert, Johannes F. Lutzeyer, Odyssée Merveille
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-06-24T15:28:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:28:27+00:00
Comments: 8 1/2 pages + 2 pages of references. Accepted for MICCAI 2026. This preprint has not undergone peer review or any post-submission improvements or corrections. The Version of Record of this contribution is published in, and available online at, the external reference provided below
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Risk stratification for pulmonary embolism (PE) is critical for clinical decision-making. Stratification guidelines are based on patient medical records, parameters measured from computed tomography pulmonary angiography (CTPA), and blood tests. However, blood tests are often missing in routine practice. This work studies whether state-of-the-art models can accurately classify risk stratification from only medical records and biomarkers extracted from CTPA images. We benchmark different approaches to combine medical records and cardiac biomarkers with rich pulmonary vascular information; we add vascular biomarkers to tabular models and apply graph neural networks (GNNs) on the vascular tree's intrinsic graph representation. We use a private dataset (n=353) with uniquely complete data for PE risk stratification. Our results show that, among global features, medical records and cardiac biomarkers are the most significant predictors, while vascular biomarkers do not further improve stratification. Even more surprising, even GNNs on vascular graphs fail to outperform strong tabular baseline on global features. We consider hypotheses, on both models and data, that could explain this suboptimal performance. Our investigation suggests that, counter-intuitively, vascular graphs might hold no discriminative information for PE risk stratification. Code is available from https://github.com/creatis-myriad/GENESIS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从CTPA和病历数据进行肺栓塞风险分层：血管图并非全部所需
肺栓塞（PE）的风险分层对临床决策至关重要。分层指南基于患者病历、计算机断层肺血管造影（CTPA）测量参数和血液检测结果。然而，血液检测在常规实践中常缺失。本研究探讨当前最先进的模型是否能仅从病历和从CTPA图像提取的生物标志物中准确分类风险分层。我们比较了多种结合病历和心脏生物标志物与丰富肺血管信息的方法：将血管生物标志物加入表格模型，并在血管树的内在图表示上应用图神经网络（GNN）。我们使用一个私有数据集（n=353），其PE风险分层数据完整无缺。结果表明，在全局特征中，病历和心脏生物标志物是最显著的预测因子，而血管生物标志物未能进一步提升分层效果。更令人惊讶的是，即使在血管图上应用GNN，其表现也未能超越强大的表格基线模型。我们探讨了可能解释此次次优表现的模型与数据假设。我们的研究提示，反直觉的是，血管图可能对PE风险分层不具判别性信息。代码可在https://github.com/creatis-myriad/GENESIS获取。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of pulmonary embolism risk stratification using only medical records and cardiac biomarkers extracted from CTPA images, since blood tests are often unavailable. The authors compare tabular models with graph neural networks (GNNs) applied to vascular tree representations, using a private dataset of 353 patients. Results indicate that medical records and cardiac biomarkers are the strongest predictors, while vascular biomarkers and GNNs on vascular graphs offer no significant improvement over strong tabular baselines. The findings suggest vascular graphs may not contain discriminative information for PE risk stratification, prompting further investigation into model and data limitations.
该研究旨在仅使用病历记录和CTPA图像提取的心脏生物标志物对肺栓塞风险进行分层，因为血液检测常缺。作者对比了表格模型与应用于血管树图结构的图神经网络，基于353名患者的私有数据集。结果显示，病历记录和心脏生物标志物是最强预测因子，而血管生物标志物和血管图结构的图神经网络未能超越强表格基线模型，表明血管图结构可能对肺栓塞风险分层无判别性信息。
Measurable Majorities Are Not Finitely Axiomatizable
Authors: Lawrence S. Moss, Arthur Paul Pedersen
First: 2026-06-24T15:27:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:27:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This theoretical note studies the finite axiomatizability of strict majority reasoning in finite social decision frames. Moss and Pedersen (2026) <doi: 10.48550/arXiv.2606.23853> introduce a coherence criterion that characterizes exactly when qualitative majority judgments are representable by a finitely additive measure. The question addressed here is whether that coherence criterion can be replaced, in the finite setting, by any bounded finite fragment. We prove that it cannot. For every $k\ge 1$, we construct a maximal standard frame whose shortest coherence violation has length exactly $2k+2$. Hence there is no uniform finite bound on the incoherence index of social decision frames, resolving Conjecture 5.7 stated by Moss and Pedersen (2026). The construction is geometric, in the sense that it proceeds via orthogonality and dimension in rational vector spaces, and self-contained: it isolates a symmetric family of half-sized voting blocs and extends it to a maximal frame in which every shorter balanced obstruction is excluded. Along the explicit infinite sequence of universe sizes obtained in the construction, this also establishes the middle-layer family predicted by Conjecture B.25 by Moss and Pedersen (2026). Together with the soundness and completeness theorem for the Moss-Pedersen minimal logic for strict majorities, this establishes that measurable social decision frames are not finitely axiomatizable in that language.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可测多数规则不可有限公理化
本文研究有限社会决策框架中严格多数推理的有限公理化问题。Moss与Pedersen（2026）<doi: 10.48550/arXiv.2606.23853> 提出了一种连贯性标准，该标准精确刻画了定性多数判断何时可由有限加性测度表示。本文探讨的问题是：在有限情形下，该连贯性标准能否被任何有界有限片段所替代？我们证明：不能。对每个 $k\ge 1$，我们构造了一个最大标准框架，其最短连贯性违反长度恰好为 $2k+2$。因此，社会决策框架的不连贯指数不存在统一的有限上界，从而解决了Moss与Pedersen（2026）提出的猜想5.7。该构造具有几何性质，通过有理向量空间中的正交性和维度进行，且自洽：它隔离一个对称的半规模投票集团，并将其扩展为最大框架，其中排除所有更短的平衡障碍。沿该构造所得的无限宇宙大小序列，也验证了Moss与Pedersen（2026）猜想B.25所预测的中间层族。结合Moss-Pedersen严格多数最小逻辑的声效与完备性定理，这表明可测社会决策框架在该语言中不可有限公理化。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses whether the coherence criterion for strict majority reasoning in finite social decision frames, introduced by Moss and Pedersen, can be captured by any finite logical fragment. The authors prove it cannot, by constructing for every k ≥ 1 a maximal standard frame where the shortest coherence violation has length exactly 2k+2, demonstrating no uniform finite bound exists. The construction is geometric, using orthogonality and dimension in rational vector spaces, and isolates symmetric half-sized voting blocs to build maximal frames excluding shorter balanced obstructions. This also confirms a predicted middle-layer family and, combined with soundness and completeness results, shows measurable social decision frames are not finitely axiomatizable in the Moss-Pedersen minimal logic.
本文研究Moss与Pedersen提出的严格多数判断的协调准则是否能在有限框架下被有限逻辑片段所替代。作者证明这是不可能的，通过为每个k≥1构造一个最大标准框架，其中最短协调违反长度恰好为2k+2，表明不存在统一的有限界。该构造基于有理向量空间中的正交性和维度，通过隔离对称的半大小投票集团并扩展为排除更短平衡障碍的最大框架。此构造也验证了Moss与Pedersen所预测的中间层族，并结合声学完备性定理，证明可测社会决策框架在Moss-Pedersen最小逻辑中不可有限公理化。
DSP-SLAM++: A Unified Framework for Multi-Class, High-Fidelity Object SLAM in the Wild
Authors: Ahmad Kourani, Ghina Daoud, Daniel Asmar, Imad Elhajj
First: 2026-06-24T15:27:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:27:23+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing object-aware SLAM systems force a trade-off between real-time performance, multi-class support, and the generation of high-fidelity, semantically coherent object models. To address this trade-off, we present DSP-SLAM++, which extends the DSP-SLAM framework with an asynchronous mapping pipeline for real-time performance and dedicated sensor fusion adaptations for a monocular fisheye-LiDAR suite. Experiments demonstrate that our system generates fine-grained, geometrically-complete shapes for multiple object classes while eliminating severe mapping thread bottlenecks by reducing maximum object processing latency by up to 70\% compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, enabling robust, real-time performance on a challenging 25 Hz multi-class datasets. This work makes high-fidelity, multi-class object SLAM more practical for real-world applications like autonomous driving and robotic manipulation by enabling its use on platforms with common fisheye-LiDAR sensor setups. The open-source code is available at: [github.com/AUBVRL/DSP-SLAMpp].
中文标题/摘要
标题：DSP-SLAM++：一种用于野外多类别高保真物体SLAM的统一框架
现有物体感知SLAM系统在实时性能、多类别支持与生成高保真语义一致物体模型之间存在权衡。为解决此问题，我们提出DSP-SLAM++，它在DSP-SLAM框架基础上扩展了异步映射流水线以实现实时性能，并针对单目鱼眼LiDAR套件进行了专用传感器融合适配。实验表明，我们的系统可为多个物体类别生成细粒度、几何完整的形状，同时通过将最大物体处理延迟降低70%（相比当前最优基线），消除了严重的映射线程瓶颈，从而在具有挑战性的25Hz多类别数据集上实现稳健的实时性能。本工作通过支持常见鱼眼LiDAR传感器配置平台，使高保真多类别物体SLAM更适用于自动驾驶和机器人操作等现实应用。开源代码见：[github.com/AUBVRL/DSP-SLAMpp]。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance real-time performance, multi-class support, and high-fidelity object modeling in object-aware SLAM, DSP-SLAM++ introduces an asynchronous mapping pipeline and sensor fusion adaptations for monocular fisheye-LiDAR. The system achieves fine-grained, geometrically complete object models across multiple classes while reducing maximum object processing latency by up to 70% compared to prior state-of-the-art, enabling robust real-time operation on a 25 Hz multi-class dataset. This advancement makes high-fidelity multi-class object SLAM more viable for real-world applications such as autonomous driving and robotic manipulation using common sensor setups.
为解决现有目标感知SLAM系统在实时性、多类别支持与高保真目标建模之间难以兼顾的问题，DSP-SLAM++引入异步映射管道和针对单目鱼眼LiDAR的传感器融合优化，以突破性能瓶颈。实验表明，该系统能在25Hz多类别数据集上实现稳定实时运行，同时将最大目标处理延迟降低70%以上，生成多类别精细且几何完整的物体模型，使高保真多类别目标SLAM在自动驾驶和机器人操作等实际场景中更具可行性。
Jolia: Concept-Level Vision-Language Alignment for 3D CT Contrastive Learning
Authors: Julien Khlaut, Charles Corbière, Baptiste Callard, Amaury Prat, Leo Butsanets, Antoine Saporta, Théo Danielou, Leo Machado, Korentin Le Floch, Tom Boeken, Pierre Manceron, Corentin Dancette
First: 2026-06-23T13:35:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:27:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language contrastive pretraining has become the dominant recipe for 3D medical foundation models, leveraging the large volumes of paired scans and reports produced in clinical practice. However, medical images usually span dozens of organs, and radiological reports are much longer than typical natural image captions and are composed of multiple structured sections. CLIP-style pretraining compresses this structure by encoding each modality into a single global token, at the risk of losing important details. We introduce ConQuer (Concept Queries), an image-text pretraining method that augments CLIP's global alignment with a set of localized alignments, one per concept. ConQuer splits the report into concept-specific sections and learns cross-attention queries that pool the matching image features without using any segmentation mask or spatial supervision. Contrastive learning is then applied independently for each concept. Concepts can be any unit of semantic localization; here, they are anatomical regions, one query per organ or gross body region. As a byproduct, each query learns attention maps focused on its concept, providing built-in spatial interpretability. We use ConQuer to train Jolia, a 3D CT foundation model on chest and abdominal CT. Jolia consistently outperforms a CLIP baseline on findings classification, report generation, and cross-center transfer, and sets a new state of the art across multiple public benchmarks. Jolia's weights are available at https://huggingface.co/raidium/Jolia
中文标题/摘要
标题：Jolia：面向3D CT对比学习的概念级视觉-语言对齐
视觉-语言对比预训练已成为3D医学基础模型的主流方法，利用临床实践中大量配对的扫描图像和报告。然而，医学图像通常涵盖数十种器官，放射报告远长于典型自然图像的描述，且由多个结构化部分组成。CLIP式预训练通过将每种模态编码为单一全局标记压缩此结构，但可能丢失重要细节。我们提出ConQuer（概念查询），一种图像-文本预训练方法，通过一组局部对齐（每个概念一个）增强CLIP的全局对齐。ConQuer将报告拆分为概念特定部分，并学习跨注意力查询，无需分割掩码或空间监督即可聚合匹配的图像特征。随后，每个概念独立应用对比学习。概念可为任意语义定位单元；此处为解剖区域，每个器官或主要身体区域一个查询。作为副产品，每个查询学习聚焦于其概念的注意力图，提供内置空间可解释性。我们使用ConQuer训练Jolia，一个基于胸部和腹部CT的3D CT基础模型。Jolia在发现分类、报告生成和跨中心迁移任务上持续优于CLIP基线，并在多个公开基准上创下新纪录。Jolia的权重可在https://huggingface.co/raidium/Jolia获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve anatomical structure in 3D medical vision-language pretraining, the authors propose ConQuer, a method that augments global CLIP-style alignment with concept-specific localized alignments. Each concept—here, anatomical regions—is assigned a query that learns to pool relevant image features via cross-attention without requiring segmentation masks or spatial supervision. Contrastive learning is applied per concept, enabling fine-grained alignment. Evaluated on chest and abdominal CT data, Jolia, the resulting 3D foundation model, outperforms CLIP baselines across findings classification, report generation, and cross-center transfer tasks, establishing new state-of-the-art results on multiple benchmarks.
为保留3D医学图像-语言模型中的解剖结构，作者提出ConQuer方法，在全局CLIP式对齐基础上增加概念级局部对齐。该方法将放射报告按器官划分，为每个概念学习跨注意力查询以聚合相关图像特征，无需空间监督。每个概念查询独立进行对比学习，从而自动生成可解释的空间注意力图。应用于胸腹CT数据训练的Jolia模型，在病灶分类、报告生成及跨中心迁移任务上均优于CLIP基线，多项公开基准上刷新了当前最优性能。
Enhancing Pathological VLMs with Cross-scale Reasoning
Authors: Chi Phan, Tianyi Zhang, Qiaochu Xue, Yufeng Wu, Dan Hu, Zeyu Liu, Sudong Wang, Yueming Jin
First: 2026-06-16T01:49:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:20:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Pathological images are inherently multi-scale, requiring pathologists to integrate evidence from global tissue architecture at low magnification to cellular morphology at higher magnification for accurate diagnosis. While existing pathological datasets for vision-language models (VLMs) include various scales, they often lack explicit cross-scale reasoning objectives. This limitation prevents VLMs from capturing essential cross-scale representations and learning evidence-based reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first cross-scale training and evaluation paradigm that formulates pathology interpretation as multi-magnification reasoning. However, creating such a task reveals a critical challenge: multi-image visual question answering (VQA) is prone to text-only shortcuts, which allow models to guess answers using magnification-dependent artifacts rather than visual evidence. To address this, we propose a leakage-aware curation pipeline that combines adversarial text-only screening with constraint-guided question design. Using this pipeline, we construct Scale-VQA, a high-quality benchmark with 4,685 multiple-choice questions grounded in 2,537 pathology images across multiple magnification levels. Finally, we present ScaleReasoner-R1, a model trained via reinforcement learning to optimize performance on cross-scale VQA tasks. ScaleReasoner-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance on our cross-scale reasoning benchmark and generalizes to SOTA performance on established single-scale benchmarks. Findings suggest that even the limited cross-scale supervision can significantly improve pathological understanding. Code is available at https://github.com/iMVR-PL/ScaleReasoner-R1.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过跨尺度推理增强病理视觉语言模型
病理图像本质上具有多尺度特性，要求病理学家将低倍镜下的组织整体结构与高倍镜下的细胞形态相结合，以实现准确诊断。尽管现有的用于视觉语言模型（VLMs）的病理数据集包含多种尺度，但通常缺乏明确的跨尺度推理目标。这一局限性阻碍了VLMs捕捉关键跨尺度表征并学习基于证据的推理能力。为弥合这一差距，我们首次提出了一种跨尺度训练与评估范式，将病理解释建模为多倍率推理任务。然而，构建此类任务揭示了一个关键挑战：多图像视觉问答（VQA）易受纯文本捷径影响，模型可利用与放大倍率相关的伪特征而非视觉证据进行猜测。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种“泄漏感知”数据整理流程，结合对抗式纯文本筛选与约束引导式问题设计。通过该流程，我们构建了Scale-VQA基准，包含2,537张病理图像在多个放大倍率下的4,685道多项选择题。最后，我们提出了ScaleReasoner-R1模型，该模型通过强化学习训练以优化跨尺度VQA任务表现。ScaleReasoner-R1在我们的跨尺度推理基准上达到最先进性能，并在现有单尺度基准上泛化至最先进水平。研究结果表明，即使有限的跨尺度监督也能显著提升病理理解能力。代码开源于https://github.com/iMVR-PL/ScaleReasoner-R1。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for vision-language models to reason across multi-scale pathological images, this work introduces a novel cross-scale training paradigm that frames pathology interpretation as multi-magnification reasoning. To combat text-only shortcuts in multi-image VQA, the authors develop a leakage-aware curation pipeline combining adversarial screening and constraint-guided question design, resulting in Scale-VQA—a benchmark of 4,685 questions across 2,537 pathology images at varying magnifications. They train ScaleReasoner-R1 via reinforcement learning to optimize cross-scale reasoning, achieving state-of-the-art performance on their new benchmark and generalizing to top results on standard single-scale VQA tasks, demonstrating that even limited cross-scale supervision significantly enhances pathological understanding.
为提升视觉语言模型在病理图像中的多尺度推理能力，该研究提出首个跨尺度训练与评估范式，将病理诊断建模为多倍率推理任务。为避免多图像视觉问答中的文本捷径问题，作者设计了一种泄漏感知的筛选流程，结合对抗式文本筛选与约束引导的问题设计，构建了包含4685道问题和2537张病理图像的Scale-VQA基准。通过强化学习训练的ScaleReasoner-R1模型在跨尺度推理任务上达到最先进水平，并在主流单尺度基准上泛化至最优表现，表明有限的跨尺度监督即可显著提升病理理解能力。
Structured Approximations of Measures
Authors: Keaton Hamm, Varun Khurana
First: 2023-10-13T14:43:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:18:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the approximation of probability measures in the Wasserstein-$p$ distance by structured classes of approximators, motivated by applications in imaging, machine learning, and physical measurement under sensor constraints. We obtain three sets of results. First, for measures with densities bounded away from zero on a bounded Lipschitz domain $Ω$, we prove that any approximation scheme for functions in $\mathrm{L}_p(Ω)$ transfers, with linear rate, to a corresponding approximation scheme for measures in $\mathrm{W}_p(Ω)$. The argument applies a theorem of Bogovskii on regularity of solutions to the continuity equation in the Benamou-Brenier formulation of optimal transport. We exhibit concrete approximation schemes (polynomials, shift-invariant spaces, cardinal interpolation with radial basis functions, kernel density estimators, and piecewise approximations on nonuniform Voronoi partitions) that fit the framework. As a matter of independent interest, we prove a negative Sobolev lower bound that generalizes existing bounds from $p=2$ to all $p\in(1,\infty)$. We also consider deterministic bounds for discrete approximations to arbitrary measures in terms of the mesh norm of a quasi-uniform set of points. We specialize these bounds to show that compactly supported measures admit a deterministic $N$-term approximation $μ_N$ such that $\mathrm{W}_p(μ,μ_N) = O(N^{-\frac{1}{d}})$ for all $d\geq 1$, which matches the asymptotic optimal quantizer rate. We also extend these results to non-compactly supported measures with appropriate tail decay.
中文标题/摘要
标题：测度的结构化近似
我们研究在Wasserstein-p距离下，由结构化近似类对概率测度的逼近，其动机源于成像、机器学习及传感器约束下的物理测量应用。我们获得三组结果：首先，对于在有界Lipschitz域Ω上密度远离零的测度，我们证明任何L_p(Ω)中函数的逼近方案，可以线性速率转化为W_p(Ω)中测度的对应逼近方案。该论证应用了Bogovskii关于Benamou-Brenier最优传输框架中连续性方程解正则性的定理。我们展示了符合该框架的具体逼近方案（多项式、移不变空间、径向基函数的插值、核密度估计器及非均匀Voronoi划分上的分段逼近）。作为独立贡献，我们证明了一个负Sobolev下界，将已有的p=2情形推广至所有p∈(1,∞)。我们还考虑了任意测度的离散逼近的确定性界，以准均匀点集的网格范数表示。我们专门化这些界，表明紧支集测度存在确定性N项逼近μ_N，使得W_p(μ,μ_N)=O(N^{-1/d})对所有d≥1成立，这与渐近最优量化速率一致。我们还将这些结果推广至具有适当尾衰减的非紧支集测度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by applications in imaging, machine learning, and sensor-limited physical measurements, this paper investigates how probability measures can be approximated in Wasserstein-p distance using structured classes of functions. The authors establish that approximation schemes for L_p functions on bounded Lipschitz domains transfer to measure approximation with linear rate, leveraging Bogovskii’s theorem on continuity equation regularity. They demonstrate concrete structured approximators including polynomials, radial basis functions, and Voronoi partitions, and independently prove a generalized Sobolev lower bound for all p in (1,∞). Experimental results show that compactly supported measures admit deterministic N-term approximations achieving W_p error O(N^{-1/d}), matching the optimal quantizer rate, and extend this to non-compactly supported measures with appropriate tail decay.
为应对图像处理、机器学习及传感器受限物理测量中的需求，本文研究了在Wasserstein-$p$距离下，如何用结构化函数类逼近概率测度。作者证明，对有界Lipschitz域上远离零的密度测度，$\mathrm{L}_p$函数逼近方案可线性转化为测度逼近方案，其核心依赖于Bogovskii关于连续性方程解正则性的定理。文中展示了多项式、径向基函数插值、Voronoi分区等具体逼近方法适用该框架。此外，作者独立推导出适用于所有$p\in(1,\infty)$的广义Sobolev下界，并证明紧支撑测度可被$N$项离散逼近，满足$\mathrm{W}_p(μ,μ_N) = O(N^{-\frac{1}{d}})$，达到最优量化速率，且可推广至尾部衰减的非紧支撑测度。
Explainable Control Framework (XCF) based on Fuzzy Model-Agnostic Explanation and LLM Agent-Supported Interface
Authors: Faliang Yin, Hak-Keung Lam, David Watson
First: 2026-06-24T15:17:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:17:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Increasing demand for precise and reliable control in complex scenarios has led to the development of increasingly sophisticated controllers, including data-driven approaches employing closed box models and mathematically rigorous yet complex designs. This complexity highlights the needs for explainable control that can provide human-understandable insights into controller behavior. In this paper, an explainable control framework (XCF) along with supporting algorithms and user interface are proposed to explain how controllers determine their control actions and their underlying working mechanism. The novel contributions of this work are threefold: First, the XCF is designed to provide model-agnostic explanations for controllers in closed-loop systems and can optionally refine local explanations by system response dynamics. Second, a novel explanation method, hierarchical fuzzy model-agnostic explanation for control systems (HFMAE-C), is proposed based on the designed framework. The HFMAE-C employs a fuzzy logic system to approximate the controller's behavior and system dynamics, providing sample, local, domain and universe level explanations via IF-THEN rules revealing the controller's decision logic and salience values quantifying the contribution of system states to control actions. Third, a large language model agent-supported user interface is developed to automatically analyze user requirements, select appropriate algorithms, interpret the generated explanations to a natural language report, and provide interactive consultation. Case studies on inverted pendulum system and Turtlebot obstacle avoidance demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method through simulated user experiments and quantitative comparisons with mainstream explainable control approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于模糊模型无关解释与大语言模型代理支持界面的可解释控制框架（XCF）
随着复杂场景中对精确可靠控制需求的增加，控制器日益复杂，包括采用黑盒模型的数据驱动方法和数学严谨但复杂的结构。这种复杂性凸显了对可解释控制的需求，以提供人类可理解的控制器行为洞察。本文提出了一种可解释控制框架（XCF）及其支持算法和用户界面，用于解释控制器如何决定控制动作及其内在工作机制。本工作的创新贡献有三方面：首先，XCF设计用于为闭环系统中的控制器提供模型无关解释，并可选地通过系统响应动态优化局部解释；其次，基于该框架提出了一种新颖的解释方法——控制系统的分层模糊模型无关解释（HFMAE-C），利用模糊逻辑系统近似控制器行为与系统动态，通过IF-THEN规则提供样本、局部、领域及全宇宙层级解释，揭示控制器决策逻辑并量化系统状态对控制动作的贡献；第三，开发了基于大语言模型代理的用户界面，可自动分析用户需求、选择合适算法、将生成解释转化为自然语言报告，并提供交互式咨询。通过倒立摆系统和Turtlebot避障系统的案例研究，结合模拟用户实验与主流可解释控制方法的定量对比，验证了所提方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for transparent and trustworthy control in complex systems, this paper introduces the Explainable Control Framework (XCF), which offers model-agnostic explanations for closed-loop controllers. The core method, hierarchical fuzzy model-agnostic explanation for control systems (HFMAE-C), uses fuzzy logic to approximate controller behavior and generate multi-level explanations via IF-THEN rules, quantifying state salience. A large language model agent interface automates explanation interpretation and user interaction. Experiments on inverted pendulum and Turtlebot obstacle avoidance show that XCF outperforms existing methods in clarity and usability, with users demonstrating higher comprehension and satisfaction through simulated interactions.
为提升复杂系统控制的透明度与可信度，本文提出一种可解释控制框架（XCF），可在不依赖特定模型结构的前提下生成人类可理解的控制器行为解释。该方法结合基于模糊逻辑的层次化模型无关解释（HFMAE-C），通过IF-THEN规则近似控制器动态并提供样本、局部、领域及全集层级解释，同时计算状态对控制动作的贡献度（显著性值）。配合大语言模型支持的交互界面，自动分析用户需求、选择算法、生成自然语言报告并提供交互咨询。在倒立摆与Turtlebot避障系统上的实验表明，XCF在用户定性反馈与定量比较中均优于主流可解释控制方法，有效提升了控制器的可解释性与可用性。
DeformGen: Dynamics-Based Topology Augmentation for Deformable Manipulation Policy Learning
Authors: Zili Lin, Wenyao Zhang, Yuyang Zhang, Zekun Qi, Junyan Lin, Hanxin Zhu, Jiaolong Yang, Zhibo Chen, Yao Mu, Xiaokang Yang, Xin Jin, Wenjun Zeng
First: 2026-06-24T15:15:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:15:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Demonstration augmentation is proposed for cost-efficient data acquisition, but existing methods are fundamentally limited in deformable manipulation due to two challenges: (1) the state space is high-dimensional with physics-induced constraints, making valid configurations impossible to reach via low-dimensional pose perturbations; and (2) trajectory transfer is non-equivariant, as material points no longer move rigidly together under deformation. We present DeformGen, a dynamics-based augmentation framework that achieves topological diversity for deformable objects. For the state challenge, DeformGen expands the valid state distribution by applying localized physical disturbances and forward-simulating the dynamics to obtain topology-coherent, physically plausible deformable states. For the trajectory challenge, DeformGen transfers source manipulation trajectories via deformation-field warping, which lifts per-particle displacements into a continuous spatial function to adapt the end-effector trajectory consistently with the deformed geometry. In this way, our method jointly augments the state distribution and its associated manipulation behavior. Experiments on high-fidelity deformable manipulation benchmarks show that DeformGen generally improves policy learning compared with training on the original demonstrations alone and with rigid-style augmentation baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeformGen：基于动力学的拓扑增强用于可变形操作策略学习
本文提出演示增强方法以实现低成本数据采集，但现有方法在可变形操作中存在两大局限：（1）状态空间高维且受物理约束，低维姿态扰动无法达到有效配置；（2）轨迹迁移不具备等变性，因变形后材料点不再刚性同步运动。DeformGen是一种基于动力学的增强框架，可实现可变形物体的拓扑多样性。针对状态挑战，DeformGen通过施加局部物理扰动并前向仿真动力学，扩展有效状态分布，生成拓扑一致且物理合理的可变形状态。针对轨迹挑战，DeformGen通过形变场扭曲转移源操作轨迹，将粒子位移升维为连续空间函数，使末端执行器轨迹与变形几何一致。该方法联合增强状态分布及其关联操作行为。在高保真可变形操作基准测试中，DeformGen相比仅训练原始演示或刚体风格增强基线，普遍提升策略学习效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of data collection in deformable manipulation and the limitations of existing augmentation methods, DeformGen introduces a dynamics-based framework to generate topologically diverse and physically plausible states and trajectories. It addresses the high-dimensional state space by applying localized physical disturbances and simulating forward dynamics to expand valid configurations, while resolving trajectory non-equivariance through deformation-field warping that maps per-particle displacements into continuous spatial functions. Experimental results on high-fidelity benchmarks demonstrate that DeformGen consistently outperforms training on raw demonstrations alone and rigid-style augmentation baselines in policy learning.
为了解决可变形操作中演示增强方法因高维状态空间和非等变轨迹转移导致的效率低下问题，DeformGen 提出一种基于动力学的拓扑增强框架。该方法通过施加局部物理扰动并前向模拟动力学，生成物理合理且拓扑一致的可变形状态，同时通过形变场扭曲将操作轨迹映射到变形几何中，使末端执行器运动与形变保持一致。实验表明，该方法在高保真基准测试中显著优于仅使用原始演示或刚性增强基线的策略学习效果。
Overview of HIPE-2026: Person-Place Relation Extraction from Multilingual Historical Texts
Authors: Juri Opitz, Maud Ehrmann, Corina Raclé, Andrianos Michail, Matteo Romanello, Simon Clematide
First: 2026-06-24T15:09:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:09:12+00:00
Comments: Condensed Overview of CLEF-HIPE-2026 Shared Task Results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Was this person ever at that place, and if so, when? Answering such questions from noisy, multilingual historical documents is the central challenge of HIPE-2026, the third edition of the HIPE evaluation series. Moving from named entity recognition and linking (HIPE-2020, HIPE-2022) to reasoning about relationships between entities, HIPE-2026 targets two temporally grounded relation types: $at$, indicating that a person was present at a location at some point prior to a document's publication date, and $isAt$, indicating presence contemporaneous with that date. This paper presents the results of the evaluation campaign, which confronted 17 participating teams with the challenges of historical language variation, OCR noise, and indirect contextual cues across three languages: French, German, and English. The datasets include historical newspaper text from the nineteenth and twentieth centuries, as well as a surprise-domain generalization set drawn from early modern French literary texts. A distinctive feature of HIPE-2026 is its three-fold evaluation framework, which assesses predictive accuracy, computational efficiency, and cross-domain generalization, reflecting the practical demands of large-scale historical document processing in the cultural heritage domain. Across more than 40 submitted runs, results reveal a wide range of strategies, from state-of-the-art large language models to lightweight task-specific classifiers, and highlight the trade-offs between accuracy, efficiency, and robustness inherent to historical relation extraction at corpus scale. System descriptions, datasets, and findings are presented and discussed, offering a detailed picture of the current state of temporally grounded relation extraction for historical documents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HIPE-2026概览：多语言历史文本中人物-地点关系抽取
此人是否曾到过该地，若曾到，何时？从嘈杂、多语言的历史文献中回答此类问题，是HIPE-2026（HIPE评估系列第三版）的核心挑战。继HIPE-2020、HIPE-2022聚焦命名实体识别与链接后，HIPE-2026转向实体间关系推理，目标为两种时间锚定的关系类型：$at$（表示人物在文档出版日期之前曾到访某地）和$ isAt$（表示人物在文档出版日期时在场）。本文呈现了评估活动结果，17支参赛队伍面对法语、德语、英语三种语言的历史语言变异、OCR噪声及间接上下文线索等挑战。数据集包括19至20世纪历史报纸文本，以及来自文艺复兴时期法语文学文本的意外领域泛化集。HIPE-2026的显著特点是三重评估框架，评估预测准确性、计算效率与跨领域泛化能力，反映文化遗产领域大规模历史文档处理的实际需求。40余份提交结果揭示了从前沿大语言模型到轻量级任务专用分类器等多种策略，凸显了在语料规模下历史关系抽取中准确率、效率与鲁棒性之间的权衡。系统描述、数据集与发现均被呈现与讨论，全面展现当前历史文档时间锚定关系抽取的现状。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extract temporally grounded person-place relations from noisy, multilingual historical texts, HIPE-2026 evaluated 17 teams on identifying whether a person was present at a location before or during the publication date of historical documents, using relation types $at$ and $isAt$. The evaluation spanned three languages—French, German, and English—and included both historical newspaper corpora and a surprise-domain set from early modern French literature, testing systems under challenges like OCR errors and linguistic variation. Results from over 40 submitted runs showed a spectrum of approaches, from large language models to lightweight classifiers, revealing trade-offs between accuracy, computational efficiency, and cross-domain generalization, underscoring the complexity of scaling relation extraction for cultural heritage applications.
HIPE-2026旨在从多语言历史文本中提取具有时间锚定的“人物-地点”关系，继前两届任务聚焦实体识别后，本任务转向关系推理，定义了$at$（过去曾到访）和$isAt$（同期在场）两种关系类型。17支队伍在涵盖19至20世纪法、德、英三种语言报纸及一个意外领域（早期法语文学）的数据集上进行评测，采用三维度评估框架（准确率、效率、跨域泛化能力），结果显示从大语言模型到轻量级分类器的多种策略并存，揭示了在大规模历史语料中实现关系抽取时，准确率、效率与鲁棒性之间的权衡关系。
Robustness assessment of large audio language models in multiple-choice evaluation
Authors: Fernando López, Santosh Kesiraju, Jordi Luque
First: 2025-10-06T08:36:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:08:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted in Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in large audio language models (LALMs) have primarily been assessed using a multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) framework. However, subtle changes, such as shifting the order of choices, result in substantially different results. Existing MCQA frameworks do not account for this variability and report a single accuracy number per benchmark or category. We dive into the MCQA evaluation framework and conduct a systematic study spanning three benchmarks (MMAU, MMAR and MMSU) and four models: Audio Flamingo 2, Audio Flamingo 3, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B-Instruct, and Kimi-Audio-7B-Instruct. Our findings indicate that models are sensitive not only to the ordering of choices, but also to the paraphrasing of the question and the choices. Finally, we propose a simpler evaluation protocol and metric that account for subtle variations and provide a more detailed evaluation report of LALMs within the MCQA framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多选题评估框架下大型音频语言模型鲁棒性评估
近期大型音频语言模型（LALMs）的进展主要通过多选题问答（MCQA）框架评估。然而，细微变化（如选项顺序调整）会导致显著不同的结果。现有MCQA框架未考虑这种变异性，仅报告每个基准或类别单一准确率。我们深入研究MCQA评估框架，系统性地覆盖三个基准（MMAU、MMAR和MMSU）和四个模型：Audio Flamingo 2、Audio Flamingo 3、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B-Instruct和Kimi-Audio-7B-Instruct。研究发现，模型不仅对选项顺序敏感，还对问题及选项的改写敏感。最后，我们提出一种更简化的评估协议和指标，以考虑细微变化，并在MCQA框架内提供更详尽的LALMs评估报告。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of large audio language models (LALMs) under minor changes in multiple-choice question formats, this study systematically evaluates four LALMs across three benchmarks (MMAU, MMAR, MMSU) by testing their sensitivity to choice ordering, question paraphrasing, and option rewording. The authors reveal that model performance varies significantly under such perturbations, undermining the reliability of single-accuracy scores reported by existing MCQA frameworks. They propose a revised evaluation protocol that accounts for these variations, enabling more nuanced and robust performance reporting within the MCQA paradigm.
鉴于大音频语言模型在多项选择题评估中对选项顺序、问题改写和选项措辞的微小变化高度敏感，本研究系统评估了四个模型在三个基准上的表现，发现其准确率随这些扰动显著波动。作者提出了一种更鲁棒的评估协议，能捕捉这些细微变化，从而提供更全面、可靠的模型性能报告。
FlowID : Enhancing Forensic Identification with Latent Flow-Matching Models
Authors: Jules Ripoll, David Bertoin, Alasdair Newson, Charles Dossal, Jose Pablo Baraybar
First: 2026-03-31T11:12:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:07:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Every day, many people die under violent circumstances, whether from crimes, war, migration, or climate disasters. Medico-legal and law enforcement institutions document many portraits of the deceased for evidence, but cannot immediately carry out identification on them. While traditional image editing tools can process these photos for public release, the workflow is lengthy and produces suboptimal results. In this work, we leverage advances in image generation models, which can now produce photorealistic human portraits, to introduce FlowID, an identity-preserving facial reconstruction method. Our approach combines single-image fine-tuning, which adapts the generative model to out-of-distribution injured faces, with attention-based masking that localizes edits to damaged regions while preserving identity-critical features. Together, these components enable the removal of artifacts from violent death while retaining sufficient identity information to support identification. To evaluate our method, we introduce InjuredFaces, a novel benchmark for identity-preserving facial reconstruction under severe facial damage. Beyond serving as an evaluation tool for this work, InjuredFaces provides a standardized resource for the community to study and compare methods addressing facial reconstruction in extreme conditions. Experimental results show that FlowID outperforms state-of-the-art open-source methods while maintaining low memory requirements, making it suitable for local deployment without compromising data privacy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlowID：利用潜在流匹配模型增强法医身份识别
每天都有许多人因暴力事件死亡，无论是犯罪、战争、移民还是气候灾害。法医和执法机构会记录大量死者肖像作为证据，但无法立即完成身份识别。虽然传统图像编辑工具可处理这些照片用于公开发布，但流程漫长且效果不佳。本研究利用图像生成模型的最新进展——如今可生成逼真的人脸图像——提出FlowID，一种身份保持型面部重建方法。我们的方法结合单图像微调，使生成模型适应分布外的受伤人脸，以及基于注意力的遮罩技术，精准定位受损区域并保留关键身份特征。这些组件共同作用，可在去除暴力致死痕迹的同时保留足够身份信息以支持识别。为评估本方法，我们引入InjuredFaces，一个针对严重面部损伤下身份保持型面部重建的新型基准数据集。除作为本工作的评估工具外，InjuredFaces也为社区提供标准化资源，用于研究和比较在极端条件下解决面部重建的方法。实验结果表明，FlowID在性能上优于当前最先进的开源方法，同时保持低内存需求，适合本地部署且不牺牲数据隐私。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to identify victims of violent deaths using damaged facial images, this work introduces FlowID, a method that leverages latent flow-matching generative models to reconstruct photorealistic portraits while preserving identity. It combines single-image fine-tuning to adapt the model to injured faces and attention-based masking to localize edits to damaged areas while protecting identity-critical features. Evaluated on the newly proposed InjuredFaces benchmark, FlowID achieves superior performance over existing open-source methods while requiring minimal memory, enabling practical deployment in forensic settings without compromising privacy.
为解决暴力致死受害者面部损伤严重导致无法及时识别的问题，本文提出FlowID方法，利用潜在流匹配模型在保持身份特征的前提下重建面部图像。该方法结合单图微调以适应损伤面部，并通过注意力掩码定位编辑区域，保留关键身份特征。在新构建的InjuredFaces基准数据集上，FlowID显著优于现有开源方法，且内存占用低，适合本地部署，无需牺牲数据隐私。
Knowledge Cascade: Reverse Knowledge Distillation on Nonparametric Multivariate Functional Estimation
Authors: Luyang Fang, Haoran Lu, Yongkai Chen, Wenxuan Zhong, Ping Ma
First: 2026-06-24T15:06:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:06:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As machine learning models and datasets continue to grow, developing complex models has become increasingly computationally demanding. Knowledge distillation reduces deployment cost by compressing a large, well-trained teacher model into a compact student model, but it does not address settings where constructing the teacher itself is the bottleneck. Motivated by this challenge, we introduce Knowledge Cascade (KCas), a reverse knowledge distillation framework that uses information from a small, inexpensive student model to guide the development of a more complex teacher model. Although this direction is counterintuitive because the teacher typically has greater representational capacity, we show that student-to-teacher transfer can be principled when supported by statistical scaling relationships. We first develop KCas for nonparametric multivariate functional estimation in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces via smoothing splines, where selecting multiple smoothing parameters is a major computational bottleneck. KCas transfers student-selected smoothing parameters to the full-sample regime through asymptotic scaling laws, substantially reducing computational cost for high-dimensional and large-scale datasets while retaining theoretical guarantees. Beyond smoothing splines, we illustrate the same principle through kernel density estimation and deep learning hyperparameter transfer. Simulations and real-data experiments show that KCas achieves substantial computational savings while maintaining strong statistical performance, and can sometimes outperform the corresponding full-sample procedure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：知识级联：非参数多元函数估计中的反向知识蒸馏
随着机器学习模型和数据集规模持续扩大，构建复杂模型的计算需求日益增加。知识蒸馏通过将大型训练好的教师模型压缩为紧凑的学生模型，降低部署成本，但未解决构建教师模型本身即为瓶颈的情形。为此，我们提出知识级联（KCas），一种反向知识蒸馏框架，利用小型、低成本的学生模型信息指导更复杂教师模型的构建。尽管该方向看似违背直觉（因教师模型通常具有更强的表示能力），我们证明在统计缩放关系支持下，学生到教师的迁移可具备理论基础。我们首先在再生核希尔伯特空间中通过平滑样条开发KCas，用于非参数多元函数估计，其中选择多个平滑参数是主要计算瓶颈。KCas通过渐近缩放律将学生选定的平滑参数迁移到全样本场景，显著降低高维和大规模数据集的计算成本，同时保留理论保证。除平滑样条外，我们还通过核密度估计和深度学习超参数迁移展示相同原理。仿真与真实数据实验表明，KCas在保持强大统计性能的同时实现显著计算节省，有时甚至优于对应全样本方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational bottleneck in constructing large teacher models, the paper proposes Knowledge Cascade (KCas), a reverse knowledge distillation framework that uses a small, inexpensive student model to guide the development of a more complex teacher model. KCas transfers student-selected parameters—such as smoothing parameters in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces—to the full-sample regime via asymptotic scaling laws, enabling efficient computation for high-dimensional datasets while preserving theoretical guarantees. Experimental results across smoothing splines, kernel density estimation, and deep learning hyperparameter transfer demonstrate that KCas achieves significant computational savings without sacrificing statistical performance, and in some cases even outperforms the full-sample approach.
为解决构建大型教师模型的计算瓶颈问题，本文提出知识级联（KCas）框架，该框架利用小型廉价的学生模型指导更复杂教师模型的构建。KCas通过渐近缩放定律，将学生模型选定的平滑参数迁移到全样本场景，从而在保持理论保证的同时大幅降低计算成本。在平滑样条、核密度估计及深度学习超参数迁移等实验中，KCas均实现显著的计算节省，且统计性能稳定，部分场景甚至优于全样本方法。
Test-Time Adaptation in Optical Coherence Tomography Using Trajectory-Aligned Time-Independent Flow
Authors: Veit Hucke, Thomas Pinetz, Gregor Reiter, Ursula Schmidt-Erfurth, Hrvoje Bogunović
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-06-17T09:54:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T15:05:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted in MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Optical coherence tomography (OCT) is essential in ophthalmology, but inconsistent image quality especially in low-cost devices hinders automated analysis. To address this, we introduce a flow-matching-based test-time adaptation method that generates high-quality surrogate images from noisy inputs. Typically, domain gaps between test and training data cause pixel distribution mismatches during the denoising process. We overcome this by matching the test image's histogram to synthetic reference trajectories, successfully aligning the input with expected distributions. Additionally, we remove the network's time conditioning to account for slight deviations in real-world noise distributions. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in segmenting critical biomarkers for two stages of Age-related Macular Degeneration (AMD). Code is available: https://github.com/Veit21/tta-flow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用轨迹对齐的时间无关流的光学相干断层扫描测试时自适应
光学相干断层扫描（OCT）在眼科中至关重要，但低成本设备图像质量不一致阻碍了自动化分析。为此，我们提出一种基于流匹配的测试时自适应方法，从噪声输入生成高质量的替代图像。通常，测试数据与训练数据之间的域差距会导致去噪过程中像素分布不匹配。我们通过将测试图像直方图与合成参考轨迹匹配，成功对齐输入与预期分布。此外，我们移除网络的时间条件化，以应对现实世界噪声分布的轻微偏差。该方法在年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）两个阶段的关键生物标志物分割中达到最先进性能。代码已公开：https://github.com/Veit21/tta-flow。
Summary / 总结
To improve automated analysis of OCT images from low-cost devices suffering from inconsistent quality, this work proposes a test-time adaptation method based on flow matching that generates high-quality surrogate images from noisy inputs. It addresses domain gaps by aligning the test image's histogram with synthetic reference trajectories, ensuring input distributions match expected denoising targets, and removes time conditioning to handle real-world noise variations. Experiments show the method achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance for key biomarkers in two stages of Age-related Macular Degeneration.
为提升低成本OCT设备图像质量不一致时的自动化分析能力，本文提出一种基于流匹配的测试时自适应方法，从噪声输入生成高质量替代图像。该方法通过将测试图像直方图与合成参考轨迹对齐，使输入分布匹配预期去噪目标，并移除时间条件以适应现实噪声偏差。实验表明，该方法在两种年龄相关性黄斑变性阶段的关键生物标志物分割任务中达到最先进性能。
